   168  class Gemma4ClippableLinear(nn.Module):
   169      def __init__(
   170          self,
   171          config: Gemma4VisionConfig | Gemma4AudioConfig,
   172          in_features: int,
   173          out_features: int,
   174      ) -> None:
   175          super().__init__()
   176          self.use_clipped_linears = config.use_clipped_linears
   177          self.linear = nn.Linear(in_features, out_features, bias=False)
   178  
   179          if self.use_clipped_linears:
   180              self.input_min = nn.Buffer(torch.tensor(-float("inf")))
   181              self.input_max = nn.Buffer(torch.tensor(float("inf")))
   182              self.output_min = nn.Buffer(torch.tensor(-float("inf")))
   183              self.output_max = nn.Buffer(torch.tensor(float("inf")))
   184  
   185      def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   186          if self.use_clipped_linears:
   187              hidden_states = torch.clamp(hidden_states, self.input_min, self.input_max)
   188  
   189          hidden_states = self.linear(hidden_states)
   190  
   191          if self.use_clipped_linears:
   192              hidden_states = torch.clamp(hidden_states, self.output_min, self.output_max)
   193  
   194          return hidden_states

   197  class Gemma4RMSNorm(nn.Module):
   198      def __init__(self, dim: int, eps: float = 1e-6, with_scale: bool = True):
   199          super().__init__()
   200          self.eps = eps
   201          self.with_scale = with_scale
   202  
   203          if self.with_scale:
   204              self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(dim), requires_grad=True)
   205  
   206      def _norm(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor):
   207          mean_squared = hidden_states.pow(2).mean(-1, keepdim=True) + self.eps
   208          # Use torch.pow() (over torch.sqrt() or torch.rsqrt()) to address compiler differences between Torch and JAX
   209          return hidden_states * torch.pow(mean_squared, -0.5)
   210  
   211      def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   212          normed_output = self._norm(hidden_states.float())
   213          if self.with_scale:
   214              normed_output = normed_output * self.weight.float()
   215          return normed_output.type_as(hidden_states)
   216

   218  class Gemma4AudioRelPositionalEncoding(nn.Module):
   219      """Sinusoidal relative positional encoding for the audio encoder.
   220  
   221      Produces position embeddings of shape [1, context_size // 2 + 1, hidden_size] with
   222      concatenated [sin..., cos...] layout matching the original Gemma4 convention.
   223      """
   224  
   225      inv_timescales: torch.Tensor
   226  
   227      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4AudioConfig):
   228          super().__init__()
   229          self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
   230          self.context_size = (
   231              config.attention_chunk_size + config.attention_context_left - 1 + config.attention_context_right
   232          )
   233          min_timescale = 1.0
   234          max_timescale = 10000.0
   235          num_timescales = self.hidden_size // 2
   236          log_timescale_increment = math.log(max_timescale / min_timescale) / max(num_timescales - 1, 1)
   237          inv_timescales = min_timescale * torch.exp(torch.arange(num_timescales) * -log_timescale_increment)
   238          self.inv_timescales = nn.Buffer(inv_timescales.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), persistent=False)
   239  
   240      @torch.no_grad()
   241      def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   242          position_ids = torch.arange(self.context_size // 2, -1, -1, device=hidden_states.device)
   243          position_ids = position_ids[..., None]
   244          scaled_time = position_ids * self.inv_timescales.to(device=hidden_states.device)
   245          pos_embed = torch.cat([torch.sin(scaled_time), torch.cos(scaled_time)], dim=-1)
   246          return pos_embed.to(dtype=hidden_states.dtype)
   247  
   248  
   249  class Gemma4AudioAttention(nn.Module):
   250      """Chunked local attention with relative position bias"""
   251  
   252      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4AudioConfig, layer_idx: int):
   253          super().__init__()
   254          self.config = config
   255          self.layer_idx = layer_idx
   256          self.attention_logits_soft_cap = config.attention_logit_cap
   257          self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads
   258          self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
   259  
   260          self.q_scale = (self.head_dim**-0.5) / math.log(2)
   261          self.k_scale = math.log(1 + math.e) / math.log(2)
   262  
   263          self.chunk_size = config.attention_chunk_size
   264          self.max_past_horizon = config.attention_context_left - 1
   265          self.max_future_horizon = config.attention_context_right
   266          self.context_size = self.chunk_size + self.max_past_horizon + self.max_future_horizon
   267  
   268          self.q_proj = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.head_dim)
   269          self.k_proj = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.head_dim)
   270          self.v_proj = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.head_dim)
   271          self.post = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
   272  
   273          self.relative_k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.head_dim, bias=False)
   274          self.per_dim_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.head_dim))
   275  
   276          self.softcap = nn.Buffer(torch.tensor(self.attention_logits_soft_cap), persistent=False)
   277  
   278      def _convert_to_block(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   279          """Splits a `(batch_size, seq_len, num_heads, head_dim)` tensor into non-overlapping blocks of `chunk_size` along the sequence dim."""
   280          batch_size, seq_len, num_heads, head_dim = hidden_states.shape
   281          num_blocks = (seq_len + self.chunk_size - 1) // self.chunk_size
   282          pad = num_blocks * self.chunk_size - seq_len
   283          hidden_states = F.pad(hidden_states, (0, 0, 0, 0, 0, pad))
   284          return hidden_states.reshape(batch_size, num_blocks, self.chunk_size, num_heads, head_dim).contiguous()
   285  
   286      def _extract_block_context(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   287          """Extracts overlapping context windows of `context_size` for every block, strided by `chunk_size`."""
   288          batch_size, seq_len, num_heads, head_dim = hidden_states.shape
   289          hidden_states = F.pad(
   290              hidden_states, (0, 0, 0, 0, self.max_past_horizon, self.max_future_horizon + self.chunk_size - 1)
   291          )
   292          hidden_states = hidden_states.unfold(1, self.context_size, self.chunk_size)
   293          hidden_states = torch.movedim(hidden_states, -1, 2)
   294          return hidden_states.contiguous()
   295  
   296      def _rel_shift(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   297          """Relative position shift for blocked attention. See appendix B of https://huggingface.co/papers/1901.02860."""
   298          batch_size, num_heads, num_blocks, block_size, position_length = x.shape
   299          context_size = self.context_size
   300          x = F.pad(x, (0, context_size + 1 - position_length))
   301          x = x.view(batch_size, num_heads, num_blocks, block_size * (context_size + 1))
   302          x = x[..., : block_size * context_size]
   303          return x.view(batch_size, num_heads, num_blocks, block_size, context_size)
   304  
   305      def forward(
   306          self,
   307          hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
   308          position_embeddings: torch.Tensor,
   309          attention_mask: torch.BoolTensor | None = None,
   310      ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, None]:
   311          batch_size, seq_length, _ = hidden_states.shape
   312          hidden_shape = (batch_size, seq_length, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
   313  
   314          query_states = self.q_proj(hidden_states).float().view(hidden_shape)
   315          key_states = self.k_proj(hidden_states).float().view(hidden_shape)
   316          value_states = self.v_proj(hidden_states).float().view(hidden_shape)
   317  
   318          query_states = query_states * self.q_scale * F.softplus(self.per_dim_scale)
   319          key_states = key_states * self.k_scale
   320  
   321          query_states = self._convert_to_block(query_states)
   322          key_states = self._extract_block_context(key_states)
   323          value_states = self._extract_block_context(value_states)
   324          num_blocks = query_states.shape[1]
   325  
   326          relative_key_states = self.relative_k_proj(position_embeddings)
   327          relative_key_states = relative_key_states.view(-1, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
   328          relative_key_states = relative_key_states.to(dtype=query_states.dtype)
   329  
   330          queries = query_states.permute(0, 3, 1, 2, 4)
   331          matrix_ac = queries @ key_states.permute(0, 3, 1, 4, 2)
   332  
   333          queries_flat = queries.reshape(batch_size, self.num_heads, -1, self.head_dim)
   334          matrix_bd = queries_flat @ relative_key_states.permute(1, 2, 0)
   335          matrix_bd = matrix_bd.reshape(batch_size, self.num_heads, num_blocks, self.chunk_size, -1)
   336          matrix_bd = self._rel_shift(matrix_bd)
   337  
   338          attn_weights = matrix_ac + matrix_bd
   339          attn_weights = attn_weights / self.softcap
   340          attn_weights = torch.tanh(attn_weights)
   341          attn_weights = attn_weights * self.softcap
   342  
   343          if attention_mask is not None:
   344              attn_weights = attn_weights.masked_fill(
   345                  attention_mask.logical_not(), self.config.attention_invalid_logits_value
   346              )
   347  
   348          attn_weights = F.softmax(attn_weights, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(value_states.dtype)
   349          attn_output = attn_weights @ value_states.permute(0, 3, 1, 2, 4)
   350          attn_output = attn_output.permute(0, 2, 3, 1, 4).reshape(batch_size, num_blocks * self.chunk_size, -1)
   351          attn_output = attn_output[:, :seq_length].contiguous()
   352          attn_output = self.post(attn_output.to(hidden_states.dtype))
   353  
   354          return attn_output, attn_weights
   355  
   356  
   357  class Gemma4AudioSubSampleConvProjectionLayer(nn.Module):
   358      def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels, norm_eps):
   359          super().__init__()
   360          self.conv = nn.Conv2d(
   361              in_channels=in_channels,
   362              out_channels=out_channels,
   363              kernel_size=(3, 3),
   364              stride=(2, 2),
   365              padding=1,
   366              bias=False,
   367          )
   368          self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(out_channels, eps=norm_eps, elementwise_affine=True, bias=False)
   369          self.act = nn.ReLU()
   370  
   371      def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor, mask: torch.Tensor | None = None):
   372          if mask is not None:
   373              mask = mask.to(device=hidden_states.device)
   374              hidden_states = hidden_states * mask[:, None, :, None]
   375  
   376          hidden_states = self.conv(hidden_states.to(self.conv.weight.dtype))
   377          hidden_states = self.act(self.norm(hidden_states.permute(0, 2, 3, 1)).permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous())
   378  
   379          if mask is not None:
   380              mask = mask[:, ::2]
   381  
   382          return hidden_states, mask
   383  
   384  
   385  class Gemma4AudioSubSampleConvProjection(nn.Module):
   386      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4AudioConfig):
   387          super().__init__()
   388          self.layer0 = Gemma4AudioSubSampleConvProjectionLayer(
   389              in_channels=1,
   390              out_channels=config.subsampling_conv_channels[0],
   391              norm_eps=config.rms_norm_eps,
   392          )
   393          self.layer1 = Gemma4AudioSubSampleConvProjectionLayer(
   394              in_channels=config.subsampling_conv_channels[0],
   395              out_channels=config.subsampling_conv_channels[1],
   396              norm_eps=config.rms_norm_eps,
   397          )
   398          proj_input_dim = (config.subsampling_conv_channels[0] // 4) * config.subsampling_conv_channels[1]
   399          self.input_proj_linear = nn.Linear(proj_input_dim, config.hidden_size, bias=False)
   400  
   401      def forward(
   402          self,
   403          input_features: torch.Tensor,
   404          input_features_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
   405      ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
   406          hidden_states = input_features.unsqueeze(1)
   407          hidden_states, mask = self.layer0(hidden_states, input_features_mask)
   408          hidden_states, mask = self.layer1(hidden_states, mask)
   409  
   410          batch_size, _, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape
   411          hidden_states = hidden_states.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).contiguous().reshape(batch_size, seq_len, -1)
   412          return self.input_proj_linear(hidden_states), mask
   413  
   414  
   415  class Gemma4AudioFeedForward(nn.Module):
   416      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4AudioConfig):
   417          super().__init__()
   418          self.config = config
   419  
   420          self.ffw_layer_1 = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size * 4)
   421          self.ffw_layer_2 = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.hidden_size * 4, config.hidden_size)
   422  
   423          self.pre_layer_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(config.hidden_size)
   424          self.post_layer_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(config.hidden_size)
   425          self.act_fn = ACT2FN[config.hidden_act]
   426  
   427          self.gradient_clipping = config.gradient_clipping
   428          self.post_layer_scale = config.residual_weight
   429  
   430      def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   431          # This is needed to avoid any underflow/overflow issues when clipping
   432          gradient_clipping = min(self.gradient_clipping, torch.finfo(hidden_states.dtype).max)
   433  
   434          residual = hidden_states
   435          hidden_states = torch.clamp(hidden_states, -gradient_clipping, gradient_clipping)
   436          hidden_states = self.pre_layer_norm(hidden_states)
   437  
   438          hidden_states = self.ffw_layer_1(hidden_states)
   439          hidden_states = self.act_fn(hidden_states)
   440          hidden_states = self.ffw_layer_2(hidden_states)
   441  
   442          hidden_states = torch.clamp(hidden_states, -gradient_clipping, gradient_clipping)
   443          hidden_states = self.post_layer_norm(hidden_states)
   444          hidden_states *= self.post_layer_scale
   445          hidden_states += residual
   446  
   447          return hidden_states
   448  
   449  
   450  # TODO: this could be imported from Voxtral realtime
   451  class Gemma4AudioCausalConv1d(nn.Conv1d):
   452      # def __init__(
   453      #     self,
   454      #     in_channels: int,
   455      #     out_channels: int,
   456      #     kernel_size: int,
   457      #     # cache_key: str,
   458      #     stride: int = 1,
   459      #     dilation: int = 1,
   460      #     bias: bool = True,
   461      # ):
   462      #     super().__init__(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size, stride=stride, dilation=dilation, bias=bias)
   463      # self.cache_key = cache_key
   464  
   465      @cached_property
   466      def left_pad(self):
   467          effective_kernel_size = (self.kernel_size[0] - 1) * self.dilation[0] + 1
   468          return effective_kernel_size - self.stride[0]
   469  
   470      def forward(
   471          self,
   472          x: torch.Tensor,
   473          # padding_cache: VoxtralRealtimeConv1dPaddingCache | None = None,  # TODO: we might want to add a cache?
   474      ) -> torch.Tensor:
   475          # if padding_cache is not None:
   476          #     x = padding_cache.update(x, self.cache_key, self)
   477          # else:
   478          #     x = nn.functional.pad(x, (self.left_pad, 0))
   479          x = nn.functional.pad(x, (self.left_pad, 0))
   480  
   481          return super().forward(x)
   482  
   483  
   484  class Gemma4AudioLightConv1d(nn.Module):
   485      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4AudioConfig):
   486          super().__init__()
   487          self.config = config
   488  
   489          self.linear_start = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size * 2)
   490          self.linear_end = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
   491          self.depthwise_conv1d = Gemma4AudioCausalConv1d(
   492              in_channels=config.hidden_size,
   493              out_channels=config.hidden_size,
   494              kernel_size=config.conv_kernel_size,
   495              groups=config.hidden_size,
   496              bias=False,
   497          )
   498  
   499          self.pre_layer_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps, with_scale=True)
   500          self.conv_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps, with_scale=True)
   501          self.act_fn = ACT2FN[config.hidden_act]
   502  
   503          self.gradient_clipping = config.gradient_clipping
   504  
   505      def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   506          residual = hidden_states
   507  
   508          hidden_states = self.pre_layer_norm(hidden_states)
   509          hidden_states = self.linear_start(hidden_states)
   510          hidden_states = nn.functional.glu(hidden_states, dim=-1)
   511  
   512          hidden_states = self.depthwise_conv1d(hidden_states.transpose(1, 2)).transpose(1, 2)
   513  
   514          # This is needed to avoid any underflow/overflow issues when clipping
   515          gradient_clipping = min(self.gradient_clipping, torch.finfo(hidden_states.dtype).max)
   516          hidden_states = torch.clamp(hidden_states, -gradient_clipping, gradient_clipping)
   517          hidden_states = self.conv_norm(hidden_states)
   518  
   519          hidden_states = self.act_fn(hidden_states)
   520          hidden_states = self.linear_end(hidden_states)
   521          hidden_states += residual
   522          return hidden_states
   523  
   524  
   525  class Gemma4AudioLayer(nn.Module):
   526      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4AudioConfig, layer_idx: int):
   527          super().__init__()
   528          self.config = config
   529  
   530          self.feed_forward1 = Gemma4AudioFeedForward(config)
   531          self.feed_forward2 = Gemma4AudioFeedForward(config)
   532          self.self_attn = Gemma4AudioAttention(config, layer_idx)
   533          self.lconv1d = Gemma4AudioLightConv1d(config)
   534  
   535          self.norm_pre_attn = Gemma4RMSNorm(config.hidden_size)
   536          self.norm_post_attn = Gemma4RMSNorm(config.hidden_size)
   537          self.norm_out = Gemma4RMSNorm(config.hidden_size)
   538  
   539          self.gradient_clipping = config.gradient_clipping
   540  
   541      def forward(
   542          self,
   543          hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
   544          attention_mask: torch.BoolTensor | None,
   545          position_embeddings: torch.Tensor,
   546          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
   547      ) -> torch.Tensor:
   548          # This is needed to avoid any underflow/overflow issues when clipping
   549          gradient_clipping = min(self.gradient_clipping, torch.finfo(self.norm_pre_attn.weight.dtype).max)
   550  
   551          hidden_states = self.feed_forward1(hidden_states)
   552          residual = hidden_states
   553  
   554          hidden_states = torch.clamp(hidden_states, -gradient_clipping, gradient_clipping)
   555          hidden_states = self.norm_pre_attn(hidden_states)
   556  
   557          hidden_states, _ = self.self_attn(
   558              hidden_states=hidden_states,
   559              position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
   560              attention_mask=attention_mask,
   561          )
   562  
   563          hidden_states = torch.clamp(hidden_states, -gradient_clipping, gradient_clipping)
   564          hidden_states = self.norm_post_attn(hidden_states)
   565          hidden_states += residual
   566  
   567          hidden_states = self.lconv1d(hidden_states)
   568          hidden_states = self.feed_forward2(hidden_states)
   569  
   570          hidden_states = torch.clamp(hidden_states, -gradient_clipping, gradient_clipping)
   571          hidden_states = self.norm_out(hidden_states)
   572  
   573          return hidden_states
   574  
   575  
   576  # ---- Vision Encoder Layers ----
   577  
   578  
   579  class Gemma4VisionPatchEmbedder(nn.Module):
   580      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4VisionConfig):
   581          super().__init__()
   582          self.config = config
   583          self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
   584          self.patch_size = config.patch_size
   585          self.position_embedding_size = config.position_embedding_size
   586  
   587          self.input_proj = nn.Linear(3 * self.patch_size**2, self.hidden_size, bias=False)
   588          self.position_embedding_table = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(2, self.position_embedding_size, self.hidden_size))
   589  
   590      def _position_embeddings(self, pixel_position_ids: torch.Tensor, padding_positions: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   591          """Compute 2-D patch position embeddings via embedding lookup.
   592  
   593          ``pixel_position_ids`` has shape ``(batch, num_patches, 2)`` where the
   594          last dimension holds (x, y) indices into ``position_embedding_table``
   595          (shape ``(2, position_embedding_size, hidden_size)``).  The result is the
   596          sum of the x- and y-embeddings for each patch.
   597          """
   598          # Clamp only the lower bound: negative values encode padding and must be
   599          # mapped to a valid index before lookup (padding embeddings are zeroed
   600          # out unconditionally below).  Valid positions are always in-range by
   601          # construction, so no upper-bound clamping is needed.
   602          clamped_positions = pixel_position_ids.clamp(min=0)
   603          # position_embedding_table: (2, position_embedding_size, hidden_size)
   604          # clamped_positions[..., 0]: (batch, num_patches) — x indices
   605          # clamped_positions[..., 1]: (batch, num_patches) — y indices
   606          x_emb = F.embedding(clamped_positions[..., 0], self.position_embedding_table[0])
   607          y_emb = F.embedding(clamped_positions[..., 1], self.position_embedding_table[1])
   608          position_embeddings = x_emb + y_emb
   609          position_embeddings = torch.where(padding_positions.unsqueeze(-1), 0.0, position_embeddings)
   610          return position_embeddings
   611  
   612      def forward(
   613          self, pixel_values: torch.Tensor, pixel_position_ids: torch.Tensor, padding_positions: torch.Tensor
   614      ) -> torch.Tensor:
   615          # Gemma4 applies no normalization and instead scales in model code
   616          pixel_values = 2 * (pixel_values - 0.5)
   617          if (target_dtype := self.input_proj.weight.dtype).is_floating_point:
   618              pixel_values = pixel_values.to(target_dtype)
   619          hidden_states = self.input_proj(pixel_values)
   620          position_embeddings = self._position_embeddings(pixel_position_ids, padding_positions)
   621          return hidden_states + position_embeddings
   622  
   623  
   624  class Gemma4VisionPooler(nn.Module):
   625      """Spatial pooling and ``sqrt(hidden_size)`` scaling for vision encodings.
   626  
   627      The scaling expands the activation magnitude, which can exceed the float16 range, so it is
   628      computed in float32 and the pooled features are returned in float32. The caller
   629      (``Gemma4VisionModel.forward``) standardizes them and casts back to the working dtype.
   630      """
   631  
   632      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4VisionConfig):
   633          super().__init__()
   634          self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
   635          self.root_hidden_size = self.hidden_size**0.5
   636  
   637      def _avg_pool_by_positions(
   638          self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor, pixel_position_ids: torch.Tensor, length: int
   639      ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
   640          """
   641          2D spatial pooling according to patch positions.
   642          Pools the input tokens by averaging patches within a `k^2` grid, where `k` is determined by the ratio between
   643          input and output lengths
   644          """
   645          input_seq_len = hidden_states.shape[1]
   646          k = int((input_seq_len // length) ** 0.5)
   647          k_squared = k**2
   648          if k_squared * length != input_seq_len:
   649              raise ValueError(
   650                  f"Cannot pool {hidden_states.shape} to {length}: {k=}^2 times {length=} must be {input_seq_len}."
   651              )
   652  
   653          # Clamp padding positions (which are -1) to 0 so they don't break one_hot.
   654          # Padding patches have zero hidden states so they contribute nothing to the average.
   655          clamped_positions = pixel_position_ids.clamp(min=0)
   656          max_x = clamped_positions[..., 0].max(dim=-1, keepdim=True)[0] + 1
   657          kernel_idxs = torch.div(clamped_positions, k, rounding_mode="floor")
   658          kernel_idxs = kernel_idxs[..., 0] + (max_x // k) * kernel_idxs[..., 1]
   659          weights = F.one_hot(kernel_idxs.long(), length).float() / k_squared
   660          output = weights.transpose(1, 2) @ hidden_states.float()
   661          mask = torch.logical_not((weights == 0).all(dim=1))
   662          return output.to(hidden_states.dtype), mask
   663  
   664      def forward(
   665          self,
   666          hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
   667          pixel_position_ids: torch.Tensor,
   668          padding_positions: torch.Tensor,
   669          output_length: int | None = None,
   670      ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
   671          if output_length > hidden_states.shape[1]:
   672              raise ValueError(
   673                  f"Cannot output more soft tokens (requested {output_length}) than there are patches"
   674                  f" ({hidden_states.shape[1]}). Change the value of `num_soft_tokens` when processing."
   675              )
   676  
   677          hidden_states = hidden_states.masked_fill(padding_positions.unsqueeze(-1), 0.0)
   678  
   679          if hidden_states.shape[1] != output_length:
   680              hidden_states, padding_positions = self._avg_pool_by_positions(
   681                  hidden_states, pixel_position_ids, output_length
   682              )
   683  
   684          # Scale in float32 and return float32: the sqrt(hidden_size) scaling can push the
   685          # activations past the float16 range (max 65504), so the magnitude is kept in float32
   686          # until the caller standardizes it.
   687          hidden_states = hidden_states.float() * self.root_hidden_size
   688          return hidden_states, padding_positions
   689  
   690  
   691  class Gemma4VisionMLP(nn.Module):
   692      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4VisionConfig):
   693          super().__init__()
   694          self.config = config
   695          self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
   696          self.intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size
   697          self.gate_proj = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, self.hidden_size, self.intermediate_size)
   698          self.up_proj = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, self.hidden_size, self.intermediate_size)
   699          self.down_proj = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, self.intermediate_size, self.hidden_size)
   700          self.act_fn = ACT2FN[config.hidden_activation]
   701  
   702      def forward(self, x):
   703          down_proj = self.down_proj(self.act_fn(self.gate_proj(x)) * self.up_proj(x))
   704          return down_proj
   705  
   706  
   707  class Gemma4VisionRotaryEmbedding(nn.Module):
   708      @deprecate_kwarg("device", version="5.18")
   709      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4VisionConfig, device=None):
   710          super().__init__()
   711          self.max_seq_len_cached = config.max_position_embeddings
   712          self.original_max_seq_len = config.max_position_embeddings
   713  
   714          self.config = config
   715  
   716          self.rope_type = self.config.rope_parameters["rope_type"]
   717          rope_init_fn: Callable = self.compute_default_rope_parameters
   718          if self.rope_type != "default":
   719              rope_init_fn = ROPE_INIT_FUNCTIONS[self.rope_type]
   720          inv_freq, self.attention_scaling = rope_init_fn(self.config, device)
   721  
   722          self.inv_freq = nn.Buffer(inv_freq, persistent=False)
   723          self.original_inv_freq = nn.Buffer(inv_freq.clone(), persistent=False)
   724  
   725      @staticmethod
   726      @deprecate_kwarg("device", version="5.18")
   727      def compute_default_rope_parameters(
   728          config: Gemma4VisionConfig, device=None, **kwargs
   729      ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, float]:
   730          """
   731          Computes the inverse frequencies according to the original RoPE implementation
   732          Args:
   733              config ([`~transformers.PreTrainedConfig`]):
   734                  The model configuration.
   735          Returns:
   736              Tuple of (`torch.Tensor`, `float`), containing the inverse frequencies for the RoPE embeddings and the
   737              post-processing scaling factor applied to the computed cos/sin (unused in this type of RoPE).
   738          """
   739          base = config.rope_parameters["rope_theta"]
   740          dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", None) or config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads
   741  
   742          # The reference implementation computes RoPE frequencies INDEPENDENTLY
   743          # for each spatial dimension using the partitioned head_dim (head_dim // ndim),
   744          # so both x and y dimensions get identical frequency ranges.
   745          # This is different from splitting the global inv_freq between dimensions.
   746          spatial_dim = dim // 2
   747  
   748          attention_factor = 1.0  # Unused in this type of RoPE
   749          inv_freq = 1.0 / (base ** (torch.arange(0, spatial_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float) / spatial_dim))
   750          return inv_freq.to(device), attention_factor
   751  
   752      @torch.no_grad()
   753      @dynamic_rope_update  # power user: used with advanced RoPE types (e.g. dynamic rope)
   754      def forward(self, x, position_ids):
   755          inv_freq_expanded = self.inv_freq[None, :, None].float().expand(position_ids.shape[0], -1, 1).to(x.device)
   756          device_type = x.device.type if isinstance(x.device.type, str) and x.device.type != "mps" else "cpu"
   757  
   758          # Multidimensional positions: [batch, num_patches, ndim]. Apply rotations to each spatial dim separately
   759          all_cos, all_sin = [], []
   760          for i in range(2):
   761              dim_position_ids = position_ids[:, :, i]
   762              dim_position_ids_expanded = dim_position_ids[:, None, :].float()
   763  
   764              with maybe_autocast(device_type=device_type, enabled=False):  # Force float32
   765                  freqs = (inv_freq_expanded.float() @ dim_position_ids_expanded.float()).transpose(1, 2)
   766                  emb = torch.cat((freqs, freqs), dim=-1)
   767                  cos = emb.cos() * self.attention_scaling
   768                  sin = emb.sin() * self.attention_scaling
   769              all_cos.append(cos)
   770              all_sin.append(sin)
   771  
   772          cos = torch.cat(all_cos, dim=-1).to(dtype=x.dtype)
   773          sin = torch.cat(all_sin, dim=-1).to(dtype=x.dtype)
   774          return cos, sin
   775  
   776  
   777  def rotate_half(x):
   778      """Rotates half the hidden dims of the input."""
   779      x1 = x[..., : x.shape[-1] // 2]
   780      x2 = x[..., x.shape[-1] // 2 :]
   781      return torch.cat((-x2, x1), dim=-1)
   782  
   783  
   784  def apply_rotary_pos_emb(x: torch.Tensor, cos: torch.Tensor, sin: torch.Tensor, unsqueeze_dim: int = 1):
   785      """Applies Rotary Position Embedding to the query and key tensors.
   786  
   787      Args:
   788          x (`torch.Tensor`): The tensor to embed.
   789          cos (`torch.Tensor`): The cosine part of the rotary embedding.
   790          sin (`torch.Tensor`): The sine part of the rotary embedding.
   791          unsqueeze_dim (`int`, *optional*, defaults to 1):
   792              The 'unsqueeze_dim' argument specifies the dimension along which to unsqueeze cos[position_ids] and
   793              sin[position_ids] so that they can be properly broadcasted to the dimensions of q and k. For example, note
   794              that cos[position_ids] and sin[position_ids] have the shape [batch_size, seq_len, head_dim]. Then, if q and
   795              k have the shape [batch_size, heads, seq_len, head_dim], then setting unsqueeze_dim=1 makes
   796              cos[position_ids] and sin[position_ids] broadcastable to the shapes of q and k. Similarly, if q and k have
   797              the shape [batch_size, seq_len, heads, head_dim], then set unsqueeze_dim=2.
   798      Returns:
   799          `tuple(torch.Tensor)` comprising of the query and key tensors rotated using the Rotary Position Embedding.
   800      """
   801      cos = cos.unsqueeze(unsqueeze_dim)
   802      sin = sin.unsqueeze(unsqueeze_dim)
   803      return (x * cos) + (rotate_half(x) * sin)
   804  
   805  
   806  def repeat_kv(hidden_states: torch.Tensor, n_rep: int) -> torch.Tensor:
   807      """
   808      This is the equivalent of torch.repeat_interleave(x, dim=1, repeats=n_rep). The hidden states go from (batch,
   809      num_key_value_heads, seqlen, head_dim) to (batch, num_attention_heads, seqlen, head_dim)
   810      """
   811      batch, num_key_value_heads, slen, head_dim = hidden_states.shape
   812      if n_rep == 1:
   813          return hidden_states
   814      hidden_states = hidden_states[:, :, None, :, :].expand(batch, num_key_value_heads, n_rep, slen, head_dim)
   815      return hidden_states.reshape(batch, num_key_value_heads * n_rep, slen, head_dim)
   816  
   817  
   818  def eager_attention_forward(
   819      module: nn.Module,
   820      query: torch.Tensor,
   821      key: torch.Tensor,
   822      value: torch.Tensor,
   823      attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
   824      dropout: float | int = 0.0,
   825      scaling: float | None = None,
   826      softcap: float | None = None,
   827      **kwargs,
   828  ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
   829      if scaling is None:
   830          scaling = module.head_dim**-0.5
   831  
   832      key_states = repeat_kv(key, module.num_key_value_groups)
   833      value_states = repeat_kv(value, module.num_key_value_groups)
   834  
   835      attn_weights = torch.matmul(query, key_states.transpose(2, 3)) * scaling
   836  
   837      if softcap is not None:
   838          attn_weights = attn_weights / softcap
   839          attn_weights = torch.tanh(attn_weights)
   840          attn_weights = attn_weights * softcap
   841      if attention_mask is not None:
   842          attn_weights = attn_weights + attention_mask
   843  
   844      # upcast attention to fp32
   845      attn_weights = nn.functional.softmax(attn_weights, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(query.dtype)
   846      attn_weights = nn.functional.dropout(attn_weights, p=dropout, training=module.training)
   847      attn_output = torch.matmul(attn_weights, value_states)
   848      attn_output = attn_output.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
   849      return attn_output, attn_weights
   850  
   851  
   852  def apply_multidimensional_rope(
   853      x: torch.Tensor,
   854      cos: torch.Tensor,
   855      sin: torch.Tensor,
   856      position_ids: torch.Tensor,
   857      unsqueeze_dim: int = 2,
   858  ) -> torch.Tensor:
   859      """Applies multidimensional RoPE to inputs.
   860  
   861      Args:
   862          x (`torch.Tensor`): The tensor to embed.
   863          cos (`torch.Tensor`): The cosine part of the rotary embedding.
   864          sin (`torch.Tensor`): The sine part of the rotary embedding.
   865          position_ids (`torch.Tensor`, *optional*):
   866              If position_ids.ndim + 2 == x.ndim, then this function passes through to `apply_rotary_pos_emb()`.
   867              Otherwise, position_ids is used to split the inputs, x, into multiple pieces, where each piece is fed to
   868              `apply_rotary_pos_emb()`, and then concatenated back together.
   869          unsqueeze_dim (`int`, *optional*, defaults to 1):
   870              The 'unsqueeze_dim' argument specifies the dimension along which to unsqueeze cos[position_ids] and
   871              sin[position_ids] so that they can be properly broadcasted to the dimensions of q and k. For example, note
   872              that cos[position_ids] and sin[position_ids] have the shape [batch_size, seq_len, head_dim]. Then, if q and
   873              k have the shape [batch_size, heads, seq_len, head_dim], then setting unsqueeze_dim=1 makes
   874              cos[position_ids] and sin[position_ids] broadcastable to the shapes of q and k. Similarly, if q and k have
   875              the shape [batch_size, seq_len, heads, head_dim], then set unsqueeze_dim=2.
   876  
   877      Returns:
   878        Tensor of shape [B, L, N, H] with RoPE applied.
   879      """
   880      ndim = position_ids.shape[-1]
   881      num_input_channels = x.shape[-1]
   882      num_rotated_channels_per_dim = 2 * (num_input_channels // (2 * ndim))
   883  
   884      if num_rotated_channels_per_dim <= 0:
   885          raise ValueError(
   886              "Invalid configuration: num_rotated_channels_per_dim must be > 0, got"
   887              f" {num_rotated_channels_per_dim} (num_input_channels={num_input_channels},"
   888              f" ndim={ndim})"
   889          )
   890  
   891      # Correctly split the input tensor into ndim parts
   892      split_sizes = [num_rotated_channels_per_dim] * ndim
   893      x_parts = torch.split(x, split_sizes, dim=-1)
   894      cos_parts = torch.split(cos, split_sizes, dim=-1)
   895      sin_parts = torch.split(sin, split_sizes, dim=-1)
   896      y_parts = [
   897          apply_rotary_pos_emb(
   898              x=x_parts[k],
   899              cos=cos_parts[k],
   900              sin=sin_parts[k],
   901              unsqueeze_dim=unsqueeze_dim,
   902          )
   903          for k in range(ndim)
   904      ]
   905      return torch.cat(y_parts, dim=-1)
   906  
   907  
   908  class Gemma4VisionAttention(nn.Module):
   909      """Multi-headed attention from 'Attention Is All You Need' paper"""
   910  
   911      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4VisionConfig, layer_idx: int):
   912          super().__init__()
   913          self.layer_type = config.layer_types[layer_idx] if hasattr(config, "layer_types") else None
   914          self.config = config
   915          self.layer_idx = layer_idx
   916          self.head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads)
   917          self.num_key_value_groups = config.num_attention_heads // config.num_key_value_heads
   918          self.scaling = 1.0
   919          self.attention_dropout = self.config.attention_dropout
   920          self.is_causal = False
   921          self.q_proj = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.hidden_size, config.num_attention_heads * self.head_dim)
   922          self.k_proj = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.hidden_size, config.num_key_value_heads * self.head_dim)
   923          self.v_proj = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.hidden_size, config.num_key_value_heads * self.head_dim)
   924          self.o_proj = Gemma4ClippableLinear(config, config.num_attention_heads * self.head_dim, config.hidden_size)
   925  
   926          self.q_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(dim=config.head_dim, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
   927          self.k_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(dim=config.head_dim, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
   928          self.v_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.head_dim, eps=config.rms_norm_eps, with_scale=False)
   929  
   930      def forward(
   931          self,
   932          hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
   933          position_embeddings: torch.Tensor = None,
   934          attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
   935          position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
   936          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
   937      ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None, tuple[torch.Tensor] | None]:
   938          input_shape = hidden_states.shape[:-1]
   939          hidden_shape = (*input_shape, -1, self.head_dim)
   940  
   941          cos, sin = position_embeddings
   942  
   943          query_states = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape)
   944          query_states = self.q_norm(query_states)
   945          query_states = apply_multidimensional_rope(query_states, cos, sin, position_ids)
   946          query_states = query_states.transpose(1, 2)
   947  
   948          key_states = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape)
   949          key_states = self.k_norm(key_states)
   950          key_states = apply_multidimensional_rope(key_states, cos, sin, position_ids)
   951          key_states = key_states.transpose(1, 2)
   952  
   953          value_states = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape)
   954          value_states = self.v_norm(value_states)
   955          value_states = value_states.transpose(1, 2)
   956  
   957          attention_interface: Callable = ALL_ATTENTION_FUNCTIONS.get_interface(
   958              self.config._attn_implementation, eager_attention_forward
   959          )
   960  
   961          attn_output, attn_weights = attention_interface(
   962              self,
   963              query_states,
   964              key_states,
   965              value_states,
   966              attention_mask,
   967              dropout=self.attention_dropout if self.training else 0.0,
   968              scaling=self.scaling,
   969              **kwargs,
   970          )
   971  
   972          attn_output = attn_output.reshape(*input_shape, -1).contiguous()
   973          attn_output = self.o_proj(attn_output)
   974          return attn_output, attn_weights
   975  
   976  
   977  class Gemma4VisionEncoderLayer(GradientCheckpointingLayer):
   978      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4VisionConfig, layer_idx: int):
   979          super().__init__()
   980          self.config = config
   981          self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
   982          self.layer_idx = layer_idx
   983          self.self_attn = Gemma4VisionAttention(config=config, layer_idx=layer_idx)
   984          self.mlp = Gemma4VisionMLP(config)
   985          self.input_layernorm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
   986          self.post_attention_layernorm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
   987          self.pre_feedforward_layernorm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
   988          self.post_feedforward_layernorm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
   989  
   990      def forward(
   991          self,
   992          hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
   993          position_embeddings: torch.Tensor = None,
   994          attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
   995          position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
   996          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
   997      ) -> tuple[torch.FloatTensor, tuple[torch.FloatTensor, torch.FloatTensor] | None]:
   998          residual = hidden_states
   999  
  1000          hidden_states = self.input_layernorm(hidden_states)
  1001  
  1002          hidden_states, _ = self.self_attn(
  1003              hidden_states=hidden_states,
  1004              position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
  1005              attention_mask=attention_mask,
  1006              position_ids=position_ids,
  1007              **kwargs,
  1008          )
  1009          hidden_states = self.post_attention_layernorm(hidden_states)
  1010          hidden_states = residual + hidden_states
  1011  
  1012          residual = hidden_states
  1013          hidden_states = self.pre_feedforward_layernorm(hidden_states)
  1014          hidden_states = self.mlp(hidden_states)
  1015          hidden_states = self.post_feedforward_layernorm(hidden_states)
  1016          hidden_states = residual + hidden_states
  1017  
  1018          return hidden_states
  1019  
  1020  
  1021  class Gemma4VisionEncoder(nn.Module):
  1022      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4VisionConfig):
  1023          super().__init__()
  1024          self.config = config
  1025          self.num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers
  1026          self.rotary_emb = Gemma4VisionRotaryEmbedding(config)
  1027          self.layers = nn.ModuleList(
  1028              [Gemma4VisionEncoderLayer(config=config, layer_idx=i) for i in range(self.num_layers)]
  1029          )
  1030  
  1031      def forward(
  1032          self,
  1033          inputs_embeds: torch.Tensor,
  1034          attention_mask: torch.Tensor,
  1035          pixel_position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  1036          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
  1037      ) -> BaseModelOutputWithPast:
  1038          r"""
  1039          pixel_position_ids (torch.Tensor):
  1040              Patch positions as (x, y) coordinates in the image as [batch, num_patches, 2].
  1041          """
  1042          attention_mask = create_bidirectional_mask(
  1043              config=self.config,
  1044              inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
  1045              attention_mask=attention_mask,
  1046          )
  1047  
  1048          # embed positions
  1049          hidden_states = inputs_embeds
  1050          position_embeddings = self.rotary_emb(hidden_states, pixel_position_ids)
  1051  
  1052          # decoder layers
  1053          for decoder_layer in self.layers[: self.config.num_hidden_layers]:
  1054              hidden_states = decoder_layer(
  1055                  hidden_states,
  1056                  attention_mask=attention_mask,
  1057                  position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
  1058                  position_ids=pixel_position_ids,
  1059                  **kwargs,
  1060              )
  1061  
  1062          return BaseModelOutputWithPast(last_hidden_state=hidden_states)
  1063  
  1064  
  1065  class Gemma4TextMLP(nn.Module):
  1066      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4TextConfig, layer_idx: int):
  1067          super().__init__()
  1068          first_kv_shared_layer_idx = config.num_hidden_layers - config.num_kv_shared_layers
  1069          is_kv_shared_layer = layer_idx >= first_kv_shared_layer_idx > 0
  1070          use_double_wide_mlp = config.use_double_wide_mlp and is_kv_shared_layer
  1071          self.config = config
  1072          self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
  1073          self.intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size * (2 if use_double_wide_mlp else 1)
  1074          self.gate_proj = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.intermediate_size, bias=False)
  1075          self.up_proj = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.intermediate_size, bias=False)
  1076          self.down_proj = nn.Linear(self.intermediate_size, self.hidden_size, bias=False)
  1077          self.act_fn = ACT2FN[config.hidden_activation]
  1078  
  1079      def forward(self, x):
  1080          down_proj = self.down_proj(self.act_fn(self.gate_proj(x)) * self.up_proj(x))
  1081          return down_proj
  1082  
  1083  
  1084  class Gemma4TextRotaryEmbedding(nn.Module):
  1085      @deprecate_kwarg("device", version="5.18")
  1086      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4TextConfig, device=None):
  1087          super().__init__()
  1088          self.max_seq_len_cached = config.max_position_embeddings
  1089          self.original_max_seq_len = config.max_position_embeddings
  1090  
  1091          self.config = config
  1092          self.layer_types = set(config.layer_types)
  1093          self.rope_init_fns: dict[str, Callable[..., tuple[torch.Tensor, float]]] = {}
  1094          self.rope_type: dict[str, str] = {}
  1095  
  1096          for layer_type in self.layer_types:
  1097              rope_params = self.config.rope_parameters[layer_type]
  1098              if rope_params is None:
  1099                  continue
  1100  
  1101              if (rope_type := rope_params["rope_type"]) != "default":
  1102                  rope_init_fn = ROPE_INIT_FUNCTIONS[rope_type]
  1103              else:
  1104                  rope_init_fn = self.compute_default_rope_parameters
  1105  
  1106              self.rope_init_fns[layer_type] = rope_init_fn
  1107              self.rope_type[layer_type] = rope_type
  1108  
  1109              # `inv_freq` depends on the head dim, which varies by layer type, so initialise
  1110              # from a config resolved for this layer type rather than the global one.
  1111              rope_config = config.per_layer_config[layer_type]
  1112              curr_inv_freq, curr_attention_scaling = rope_init_fn(rope_config, device, layer_type=layer_type)
  1113              setattr(self, f"{layer_type}_inv_freq", nn.Buffer(curr_inv_freq, persistent=False))
  1114              setattr(self, f"{layer_type}_original_inv_freq", nn.Buffer(curr_inv_freq.clone(), persistent=False))
  1115              setattr(self, f"{layer_type}_attention_scaling", curr_attention_scaling)
  1116  
  1117      @staticmethod
  1118      @deprecate_kwarg("device", version="5.18")
  1119      def compute_default_rope_parameters(
  1120          config: Gemma4TextConfig, device=None, layer_type: str | None = None, **kwargs
  1121      ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, float]:
  1122          """
  1123          Computes the inverse frequencies according to the original RoPE implementation
  1124          Args:
  1125              config ([`~transformers.PreTrainedConfig`]):
  1126                  The model configuration.
  1127              layer_type (`str`):
  1128                  The current layer type if the model has different RoPE parameters per type.
  1129                  Should not be used unless `config.layer_types is not None`
  1130  
  1131          Returns:
  1132              Tuple of (`torch.Tensor`, `float`), containing the inverse frequencies for the RoPE embeddings and the
  1133              post-processing scaling factor applied to the computed cos/sin (unused in this type of RoPE).
  1134          """
  1135          # For backward compatibility standardize the `rope_parameters_dict` if it uses old format
  1136          base = config.rope_parameters[layer_type]["rope_theta"]
  1137          dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", None) or config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads
  1138  
  1139          attention_factor = 1.0  # Unused in this type of RoPE
  1140          # Compute the inverse frequencies
  1141          inv_freq = 1.0 / (base ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=torch.float) / dim))
  1142          return inv_freq.to(device), attention_factor
  1143  
  1144      @torch.no_grad()
  1145      @dynamic_rope_update  # power user: used with advanced RoPE types (e.g. dynamic rope)
  1146      def forward(self, x, position_ids, layer_type):
  1147          inv_freq = getattr(self, f"{layer_type}_inv_freq")
  1148          attention_scaling = getattr(self, f"{layer_type}_attention_scaling")
  1149  
  1150          inv_freq_expanded = (
  1151              inv_freq[None, :, None].expand(position_ids.shape[0], -1, 1).to(dtype=torch.float, device=x.device)
  1152          )
  1153          position_ids_expanded = position_ids[:, None, :].float()
  1154  
  1155          device_type = x.device.type if isinstance(x.device.type, str) and x.device.type != "mps" else "cpu"
  1156          # Disable any outside autocast context if any, to really force fp32
  1157          with maybe_autocast(device_type=device_type, enabled=False):
  1158              freqs = (inv_freq_expanded @ position_ids_expanded).transpose(1, 2)
  1159              emb = torch.cat((freqs, freqs), dim=-1)
  1160              cos = emb.cos() * attention_scaling
  1161              sin = emb.sin() * attention_scaling
  1162  
  1163          return cos.to(dtype=x.dtype), sin.to(dtype=x.dtype)
  1164  
  1165  
  1166  class Gemma4TextAttention(nn.Module):
  1167      """Multi-headed attention from 'Attention Is All You Need' paper"""
  1168  
  1169      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4TextConfig, layer_idx: int):
  1170          super().__init__()
  1171          self.layer_type = config.layer_types[layer_idx] if hasattr(config, "layer_types") else None
  1172          self.config = config
  1173          self.layer_idx = layer_idx
  1174          self.is_sliding = self.layer_type == "sliding_attention"
  1175          self.sliding_window = config.sliding_window if self.is_sliding else None
  1176  
  1177          layer_config = config.per_layer_config[layer_idx]
  1178          self.head_dim = layer_config.head_dim
  1179          self.use_alternative_attention = config.attention_k_eq_v and not self.is_sliding
  1180          self.num_key_value_groups = config.num_attention_heads // layer_config.num_key_value_heads
  1181          self.scaling = 1.0
  1182          self.attention_dropout = self.config.attention_dropout
  1183          self.is_causal = config.use_bidirectional_attention != "all"
  1184  
  1185          # Shared kv cache
  1186          first_kv_shared_layer_idx = self.config.num_hidden_layers - getattr(self.config, "num_kv_shared_layers", 0)
  1187          self.is_kv_shared_layer = layer_idx >= first_kv_shared_layer_idx >= 0
  1188          prev_layers = config.layer_types[:first_kv_shared_layer_idx]
  1189          self.store_full_length_kv = not self.is_kv_shared_layer and layer_idx == len(prev_layers) - 1 - prev_layers[
  1190              ::-1
  1191          ].index(config.layer_types[layer_idx])
  1192  
  1193          self.q_proj = nn.Linear(
  1194              config.hidden_size, config.num_attention_heads * self.head_dim, bias=config.attention_bias
  1195          )
  1196          self.q_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(dim=self.head_dim, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1197  
  1198          # Layers sharing kv states don't need any weight matrices
  1199          if not self.is_kv_shared_layer:
  1200              self.k_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(dim=self.head_dim, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1201              self.v_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.head_dim, eps=config.rms_norm_eps, with_scale=False)
  1202  
  1203              self.k_proj = nn.Linear(
  1204                  config.hidden_size, layer_config.num_key_value_heads * self.head_dim, bias=config.attention_bias
  1205              )
  1206              self.v_proj = (
  1207                  nn.Linear(
  1208                      config.hidden_size, layer_config.num_key_value_heads * self.head_dim, bias=config.attention_bias
  1209                  )
  1210                  if not self.use_alternative_attention
  1211                  else None
  1212              )
  1213  
  1214          self.o_proj = nn.Linear(
  1215              config.num_attention_heads * self.head_dim, config.hidden_size, bias=config.attention_bias
  1216          )
  1217  
  1218      def forward(
  1219          self,
  1220          hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
  1221          position_embeddings: torch.Tensor,
  1222          attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
  1223          shared_kv_states: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]],
  1224          past_key_values: Cache | None = None,
  1225          **kwargs: Unpack[FlashAttentionKwargs],
  1226      ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
  1227          input_shape = hidden_states.shape[:-1]
  1228          hidden_shape = (*input_shape, -1, self.head_dim)
  1229  
  1230          cos, sin = position_embeddings
  1231  
  1232          query_states = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape)
  1233          query_states = self.q_norm(query_states)
  1234          query_states = apply_rotary_pos_emb(query_states, cos, sin, unsqueeze_dim=2)
  1235          query_states = query_states.transpose(1, 2)
  1236  
  1237          # For layers with shared KV (from kv sharing point onwards), we reuse the same keys/values states as the last non-sharing layer.
  1238          # We cannot simply reuse the cached state if we have a Cache, as sliding layers will not remember the full states in their Cache
  1239          # once we are past the sliding window - so we always use `shared_kv_states` instead, even when past_key_values is not None
  1240          if self.is_kv_shared_layer:
  1241              key_states, value_states = shared_kv_states[self.layer_type]
  1242              # Device of past layer may be different from current one
  1243              key_states = key_states.to(query_states.device)
  1244              value_states = value_states.to(query_states.device)
  1245          else:
  1246              key_states = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape)
  1247              value_states = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape) if self.v_proj is not None else key_states
  1248  
  1249              key_states = self.k_norm(key_states)
  1250              key_states = apply_rotary_pos_emb(key_states, cos, sin, unsqueeze_dim=2)
  1251              key_states = key_states.transpose(1, 2)
  1252  
  1253              value_states = self.v_norm(value_states)
  1254              value_states = value_states.transpose(1, 2)
  1255  
  1256          if past_key_values is not None and not self.is_kv_shared_layer:
  1257              key_states, value_states = past_key_values.update(key_states, value_states, self.layer_idx)
  1258          if self.store_full_length_kv:
  1259              shared_kv_states[self.layer_type] = key_states, value_states
  1260  
  1261          attention_interface: Callable = ALL_ATTENTION_FUNCTIONS.get_interface(
  1262              self.config._attn_implementation, eager_attention_forward
  1263          )
  1264  
  1265          attn_output, attn_weights = attention_interface(
  1266              self,
  1267              query_states,
  1268              key_states,
  1269              value_states,
  1270              attention_mask,
  1271              dropout=self.attention_dropout if self.training else 0.0,
  1272              scaling=self.scaling,
  1273              sliding_window=self.sliding_window,
  1274              **kwargs,
  1275          )
  1276  
  1277          attn_output = attn_output.reshape(*input_shape, -1).contiguous()
  1278          attn_output = self.o_proj(attn_output)
  1279          return attn_output, attn_weights
  1280  
  1281  
  1282  @use_experts_implementation
  1283  class Gemma4TextExperts(nn.Module):
  1284      """Collection of expert weights stored as 3D tensors."""
  1285  
  1286      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4TextConfig):
  1287          super().__init__()
  1288          self.num_experts = config.num_experts
  1289          self.hidden_dim = config.hidden_size
  1290          self.intermediate_dim = config.moe_intermediate_size
  1291          self.gate_up_proj = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.num_experts, 2 * self.intermediate_dim, self.hidden_dim))
  1292          self.down_proj = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.num_experts, self.hidden_dim, self.intermediate_dim))
  1293          self.act_fn = ACT2FN[config.hidden_activation]
  1294  
  1295      def forward(
  1296          self,
  1297          hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
  1298          top_k_index: torch.Tensor,
  1299          top_k_weights: torch.Tensor,
  1300      ) -> torch.Tensor:
  1301          final_hidden_states = torch.zeros_like(hidden_states)
  1302          with torch.no_grad():
  1303              expert_mask = torch.nn.functional.one_hot(top_k_index, num_classes=self.num_experts)
  1304              expert_mask = expert_mask.permute(2, 1, 0)
  1305              expert_hit = torch.greater(expert_mask.sum(dim=(-1, -2)), 0).nonzero()
  1306  
  1307          for expert_idx in expert_hit:
  1308              expert_idx = expert_idx[0]
  1309              if expert_idx == self.num_experts:
  1310                  continue
  1311              top_k_pos, token_idx = torch.where(expert_mask[expert_idx])
  1312              current_state = hidden_states[token_idx]
  1313              gate, up = nn.functional.linear(current_state, self.gate_up_proj[expert_idx]).chunk(2, dim=-1)
  1314              current_hidden_states = self.act_fn(gate) * up
  1315              current_hidden_states = nn.functional.linear(current_hidden_states, self.down_proj[expert_idx])
  1316              current_hidden_states = current_hidden_states * top_k_weights[token_idx, top_k_pos, None]
  1317              final_hidden_states.index_add_(0, token_idx, current_hidden_states.to(final_hidden_states.dtype))
  1318  
  1319          return final_hidden_states
  1320  
  1321  
  1322  class Gemma4TextRouter(nn.Module):
  1323      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4TextConfig):
  1324          super().__init__()
  1325          self.config = config
  1326          self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
  1327          self.scalar_root_size = self.hidden_size**-0.5
  1328          self.eps = config.rms_norm_eps
  1329  
  1330          self.norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=self.eps, with_scale=False)
  1331          self.proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.num_experts, bias=False)
  1332          self.scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.hidden_size))
  1333          self.per_expert_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(config.num_experts))
  1334  
  1335      def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
  1336          hidden_states = self.norm(hidden_states)
  1337          hidden_states = hidden_states * self.scale * self.scalar_root_size
  1338  
  1339          expert_scores = self.proj(hidden_states)  # [B*S, E]
  1340          # fp32 for numerical stability
  1341          router_probabilities = nn.functional.softmax(expert_scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32)
  1342  
  1343          # topk returns both values (probabilities) and indices directly
  1344          top_k_weights, top_k_index = torch.topk(
  1345              router_probabilities,
  1346              k=self.config.top_k_experts,
  1347              dim=-1,
  1348          )  # both [B*S, K]
  1349  
  1350          # Normalize the top-k weights so they sum to 1 per token
  1351          top_k_weights /= top_k_weights.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
  1352  
  1353          # Apply per-expert scale directly to the weights
  1354          top_k_weights = top_k_weights * self.per_expert_scale[top_k_index]
  1355  
  1356          return router_probabilities, top_k_weights, top_k_index
  1357  
  1358  
  1359  class Gemma4TextDecoderLayer(GradientCheckpointingLayer):
  1360      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4TextConfig | Gemma4VisionConfig, layer_idx: int):
  1361          super().__init__()
  1362          self.config = config
  1363          self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
  1364          self.layer_idx = layer_idx
  1365          self.self_attn = Gemma4TextAttention(config=config, layer_idx=layer_idx)
  1366          self.mlp = Gemma4TextMLP(config, layer_idx)
  1367          self.input_layernorm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1368          self.post_attention_layernorm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1369          self.pre_feedforward_layernorm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1370          self.post_feedforward_layernorm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1371          self.layer_scalar = nn.Buffer(torch.ones(1))
  1372  
  1373          self.hidden_size_per_layer_input = config.hidden_size_per_layer_input
  1374          if self.hidden_size_per_layer_input:
  1375              self.act_fn = ACT2FN[config.hidden_activation]
  1376              self.per_layer_input_gate = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.hidden_size_per_layer_input, bias=False)
  1377              self.per_layer_projection = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size_per_layer_input, self.hidden_size, bias=False)
  1378              self.post_per_layer_input_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1379  
  1380          self.enable_moe_block = config.enable_moe_block
  1381          if self.enable_moe_block:
  1382              self.router = Gemma4TextRouter(config)
  1383              self.experts = Gemma4TextExperts(config)
  1384              self.post_feedforward_layernorm_1 = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1385              self.post_feedforward_layernorm_2 = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1386              self.pre_feedforward_layernorm_2 = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1387  
  1388      def forward(
  1389          self,
  1390          hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
  1391          per_layer_input: torch.Tensor = None,
  1392          shared_kv_states: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]] | None = None,
  1393          position_embeddings: torch.Tensor = None,
  1394          attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  1395          position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  1396          past_key_values: Cache | None = None,
  1397          **kwargs,
  1398      ) -> torch.Tensor:
  1399          residual = hidden_states
  1400  
  1401          hidden_states = self.input_layernorm(hidden_states)
  1402          hidden_states, _ = self.self_attn(
  1403              hidden_states=hidden_states,
  1404              position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
  1405              attention_mask=attention_mask,
  1406              shared_kv_states=shared_kv_states,
  1407              position_ids=position_ids,
  1408              past_key_values=past_key_values,
  1409              **kwargs,
  1410          )
  1411          hidden_states = self.post_attention_layernorm(hidden_states)
  1412          hidden_states = residual + hidden_states
  1413  
  1414          residual = hidden_states
  1415          hidden_states = self.pre_feedforward_layernorm(hidden_states)
  1416          hidden_states = self.mlp(hidden_states)
  1417  
  1418          if self.enable_moe_block:
  1419              hidden_states_1 = self.post_feedforward_layernorm_1(hidden_states)
  1420  
  1421              # Take hidden states before MLP here
  1422              hidden_states_flat = residual.reshape(-1, residual.shape[-1])
  1423              _, top_k_weights, top_k_index = self.router(hidden_states_flat)
  1424              hidden_states_2 = self.pre_feedforward_layernorm_2(hidden_states_flat)
  1425              hidden_states_2 = self.experts(hidden_states_2, top_k_index, top_k_weights)
  1426              hidden_states_2 = hidden_states_2.reshape(residual.shape)
  1427              hidden_states_2 = self.post_feedforward_layernorm_2(hidden_states_2)
  1428  
  1429              # Combine mlp and moe outputs
  1430              hidden_states = hidden_states_1 + hidden_states_2
  1431  
  1432          hidden_states = self.post_feedforward_layernorm(hidden_states)
  1433          hidden_states = residual + hidden_states
  1434  
  1435          if self.hidden_size_per_layer_input:
  1436              residual = hidden_states
  1437              hidden_states = self.per_layer_input_gate(hidden_states)
  1438              hidden_states = self.act_fn(hidden_states)
  1439              hidden_states = hidden_states * per_layer_input
  1440              hidden_states = self.per_layer_projection(hidden_states)
  1441              hidden_states = self.post_per_layer_input_norm(hidden_states)
  1442              hidden_states = residual + hidden_states
  1443  
  1444          hidden_states *= self.layer_scalar
  1445          return hidden_states
  1446  
  1447  
  1448  class Gemma4TextScaledWordEmbedding(nn.Embedding):
  1449      """
  1450      This module overrides nn.Embeddings' forward by multiplying with embeddings scale.
  1451      """
  1452  
  1453      def __init__(self, num_embeddings: int, embedding_dim: int, padding_idx: int, embed_scale: float = 1.0):
  1454          super().__init__(num_embeddings, embedding_dim, padding_idx)
  1455          self.scalar_embed_scale = embed_scale
  1456          self.embed_scale = nn.Buffer(torch.tensor(embed_scale), persistent=False)
  1457  
  1458      def forward(self, input_ids: torch.Tensor):
  1459          return super().forward(input_ids) * self.embed_scale.to(self.weight.dtype)
  1460  
  1461  
  1462  @auto_docstring
  1463  class Gemma4PreTrainedModel(PreTrainedModel):
  1464      config: Gemma4Config
  1465      base_model_prefix = "model"
  1466      supports_gradient_checkpointing = True
  1467      _no_split_modules = [
  1468          "Gemma4TextDecoderLayer",
  1469          "Gemma4VisionEncoderLayer",
  1470          "Gemma4VisionPatchEmbedder",
  1471          "Gemma4AudioLayer",
  1472      ]
  1473      _skip_keys_device_placement = ["past_key_values", "shared_kv_states"]
  1474      _supports_flash_attn = True
  1475      _supports_sdpa = True
  1476      _supports_flex_attn = True
  1477  
  1478      _can_compile_fullgraph = True
  1479      _supports_attention_backend = True
  1480      _can_record_outputs = None  # override
  1481      input_modalities = ("image", "text", "video", "audio")
  1482  
  1483      @torch.no_grad()
  1484      def _init_weights(self, module):
  1485          super()._init_weights(module)
  1486          if isinstance(module, Gemma4VisionPatchEmbedder):
  1487              init.ones_(module.position_embedding_table)
  1488          elif isinstance(module, Gemma4AudioRelPositionalEncoding):
  1489              min_timescale = 1.0
  1490              max_timescale = 10000.0
  1491              num_timescales = module.hidden_size // 2
  1492              log_timescale_increment = math.log(max_timescale / min_timescale) / max(num_timescales - 1, 1)
  1493              inv_timescales = min_timescale * torch.exp(torch.arange(num_timescales) * -log_timescale_increment)
  1494              init.copy_(module.inv_timescales, inv_timescales.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0))
  1495          elif isinstance(module, Gemma4AudioAttention):
  1496              init.constant_(module.softcap, module.attention_logits_soft_cap)
  1497              init.zeros_(module.per_dim_scale)
  1498          elif isinstance(module, Gemma4TextRotaryEmbedding):
  1499              for layer_type, rope_init_fn in module.rope_init_fns.items():
  1500                  rope_config = module.config.per_layer_config[layer_type]
  1501                  curr_inv_freq, _ = rope_init_fn(rope_config, layer_type=layer_type)
  1502                  init.copy_(getattr(module, f"{layer_type}_inv_freq"), curr_inv_freq)
  1503                  init.copy_(getattr(module, f"{layer_type}_original_inv_freq"), curr_inv_freq)
  1504          elif isinstance(module, Gemma4VisionRotaryEmbedding):
  1505              rope_fn = (
  1506                  ROPE_INIT_FUNCTIONS[module.rope_type]
  1507                  if module.rope_type != "default"
  1508                  else module.compute_default_rope_parameters
  1509              )
  1510              buffer_value, _ = rope_fn(module.config)
  1511              init.copy_(module.inv_freq, buffer_value)
  1512              init.copy_(module.original_inv_freq, buffer_value)
  1513          elif isinstance(module, Gemma4TextScaledWordEmbedding):
  1514              init.constant_(module.embed_scale, module.scalar_embed_scale)
  1515          elif isinstance(module, Gemma4TextRouter):
  1516              init.ones_(module.scale)
  1517              init.ones_(module.per_expert_scale)
  1518          elif isinstance(module, Gemma4TextExperts):
  1519              std = self.config.initializer_range
  1520              init.normal_(module.gate_up_proj, mean=0.0, std=std)
  1521              init.normal_(module.down_proj, mean=0.0, std=std)
  1522          elif isinstance(module, Gemma4TextDecoderLayer):
  1523              init.ones_(module.layer_scalar)
  1524          elif isinstance(module, Gemma4ClippableLinear) and module.use_clipped_linears:
  1525              init.constant_(module.input_min, -float("inf"))
  1526              init.constant_(module.input_max, float("inf"))
  1527              init.constant_(module.output_min, -float("inf"))
  1528              init.constant_(module.output_max, float("inf"))
  1529          elif isinstance(module, Gemma4VisionModel) and module.config.standardize:
  1530              init.zeros_(module.std_bias)
  1531              init.ones_(module.std_scale)
  1532  
  1533      def get_per_layer_input_embeddings(self):
  1534          return self.base_model.embed_tokens_per_layer
  1535  
  1536      def set_per_layer_input_embeddings(self, value):
  1537          self.base_model.embed_tokens_per_layer = value
  1538  
  1539      def resize_token_embeddings(
  1540          self,
  1541          new_num_tokens: int | None = None,
  1542          pad_to_multiple_of: int | None = None,
  1543          mean_resizing: bool = True,
  1544      ) -> nn.Embedding:
  1545          inputs_embeds = super().resize_token_embeddings(
  1546              new_num_tokens=new_num_tokens,
  1547              pad_to_multiple_of=pad_to_multiple_of,
  1548              mean_resizing=mean_resizing,
  1549          )
  1550          self._resize_per_layer_embeddings(new_num_tokens, pad_to_multiple_of, mean_resizing)
  1551          return inputs_embeds
  1552  
  1553      def _resize_per_layer_embeddings(
  1554          self,
  1555          new_num_tokens: int | None = None,
  1556          pad_to_multiple_of: int | None = None,
  1557          mean_resizing: bool = True,
  1558      ):
  1559          self.config.get_text_config().vocab_size_per_layer_input = self.vocab_size
  1560          if self.config.get_text_config().hidden_size_per_layer_input:
  1561              embed_tokens_per_layer = self.get_per_layer_input_embeddings()
  1562              new_embeddings_per_layer = self._get_resized_embeddings(
  1563                  embed_tokens_per_layer, new_num_tokens, pad_to_multiple_of, mean_resizing
  1564              )
  1565              if hasattr(embed_tokens_per_layer, "_hf_hook"):
  1566                  hook = embed_tokens_per_layer._hf_hook
  1567                  add_hook_to_module(new_embeddings_per_layer, hook)
  1568              new_embeddings_per_layer.requires_grad_(embed_tokens_per_layer.weight.requires_grad)
  1569              self.set_per_layer_input_embeddings(new_embeddings_per_layer)
  1570  
  1571  
  1572  @auto_docstring(custom_intro="The base Gemma 4 language model without a language modeling head.")
  1573  class Gemma4TextModel(Gemma4PreTrainedModel):
  1574      config: Gemma4TextConfig
  1575      input_modalities = ("text",)
  1576      _can_record_outputs = {
  1577          "router_logits": OutputRecorder(Gemma4TextRouter, index=0),
  1578          "hidden_states": Gemma4TextDecoderLayer,
  1579          "attentions": Gemma4TextAttention,
  1580      }
  1581  
  1582      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4TextConfig):
  1583          super().__init__(config)
  1584          self.padding_idx = config.pad_token_id
  1585          self.vocab_size = config.vocab_size
  1586  
  1587          # Gemma4 downcasts the below to bfloat16, causing sqrt(3072)=55.4256 to become 55.5. See https://github.com/huggingface/transformers/pull/29402
  1588          self.embed_tokens = Gemma4TextScaledWordEmbedding(
  1589              config.vocab_size, config.hidden_size, self.padding_idx, embed_scale=self.config.hidden_size**0.5
  1590          )
  1591          self.layers = nn.ModuleList(
  1592              [Gemma4TextDecoderLayer(config, layer_idx) for layer_idx in range(config.num_hidden_layers)]
  1593          )
  1594          self.norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1595          self.rotary_emb = Gemma4TextRotaryEmbedding(config)
  1596          self.gradient_checkpointing = False
  1597          self.unique_layer_types = set(self.config.layer_types)
  1598  
  1599          # Per-Layer Embeddings (PLE): auxiliary embedding that feeds a residual signal
  1600          # into each decoder layer. See `get_per_layer_inputs()` and `project_per_layer_inputs()`
  1601          # for the full pipeline. The embedding is packed: total dim = num_layers * per_layer_dim.
  1602          self.hidden_size_per_layer_input = config.hidden_size_per_layer_input
  1603          if self.hidden_size_per_layer_input:
  1604              self.embed_tokens_per_layer = Gemma4TextScaledWordEmbedding(
  1605                  config.vocab_size_per_layer_input,
  1606                  config.num_hidden_layers * config.hidden_size_per_layer_input,
  1607                  self.padding_idx,
  1608                  embed_scale=config.hidden_size_per_layer_input**0.5,
  1609              )
  1610              self.per_layer_input_scale = 2.0**-0.5
  1611              self.per_layer_model_projection = nn.Linear(
  1612                  config.hidden_size,
  1613                  config.num_hidden_layers * config.hidden_size_per_layer_input,
  1614                  bias=False,
  1615              )
  1616              self.per_layer_model_projection_scale = config.hidden_size**-0.5
  1617              self.per_layer_projection_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(config.hidden_size_per_layer_input, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)
  1618  
  1619          # Update `_keys_to_ignore_on_load_unexpected` to drop all k/v proj and norms for the shared layers
  1620          self._keys_to_ignore_on_load_unexpected = []
  1621          for i, layer in enumerate(self.layers):
  1622              if layer.self_attn.is_kv_shared_layer:
  1623                  self._keys_to_ignore_on_load_unexpected.extend(
  1624                      [f"layers.{i}.self_attn.{name}" for name in ("k_proj", "v_proj", "k_norm", "v_norm")]
  1625                  )
  1626  
  1627          # Initialize weights and apply final processing
  1628          self.post_init()
  1629  
  1630      @merge_with_config_defaults
  1631      @capture_outputs
  1632      @auto_docstring
  1633      def forward(
  1634          self,
  1635          input_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  1636          attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  1637          position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  1638          past_key_values: Cache | None = None,
