  1639          inputs_embeds: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
  1640          per_layer_inputs: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  1641          use_cache: bool | None = None,
  1642          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
  1643      ) -> Gemma4TextModelOutputWithPast:
  1644          r"""
  1645          per_layer_inputs (`torch.Tensor`, *optional*):
  1646              Pre-computed per-layer input text embeddings of shape `(batch_size, sequence_length, num_hidden_layers,
  1647              hidden_size_per_layer_input)`. When provided, these are used directly instead of being computed from `input_ids`
  1648              via `get_per_layer_inputs()` in the text model. If calling the `forward` with `inputs_embeds` instead of `input_ids`,
  1649              you should probably precompute them and forward them along `inputs_embeds`, otherwise recomputing them needs
  1650              to reverse the main embedding, which is expensive.
  1651          """
  1652          if (input_ids is None) ^ (inputs_embeds is not None):
  1653              raise ValueError("You must specify exactly one of input_ids or inputs_embeds")
  1654  
  1655          if input_ids is not None and per_layer_inputs is not None:
  1656              raise ValueError("You cannot specify per_layer_inputs if input_ids is provided")
  1657  
  1658          if input_ids is not None:
  1659              inputs_embeds = self.embed_tokens(input_ids)
  1660  
  1661          if self.hidden_size_per_layer_input:
  1662              if per_layer_inputs is None:
  1663                  per_layer_inputs = self.get_per_layer_inputs(input_ids, inputs_embeds)
  1664              per_layer_inputs = self.project_per_layer_inputs(inputs_embeds, per_layer_inputs)
  1665  
  1666          if use_cache and past_key_values is None:
  1667              past_key_values = DynamicCache(config=self.config)
  1668  
  1669          if position_ids is None:
  1670              past_seen_tokens = past_key_values.get_seq_length() if past_key_values is not None else 0
  1671              position_ids = torch.arange(inputs_embeds.shape[1], device=inputs_embeds.device) + past_seen_tokens
  1672              position_ids = position_ids.unsqueeze(0)
  1673  
  1674          # It may already have been prepared by e.g. `generate`
  1675          if not isinstance(causal_mask_mapping := attention_mask, dict):
  1676              # Prepare mask arguments
  1677              mask_kwargs = {
  1678                  "config": self.config,
  1679                  "inputs_embeds": inputs_embeds,
  1680                  "attention_mask": attention_mask,
  1681                  "past_key_values": past_key_values,
  1682                  "position_ids": position_ids,
  1683              }
  1684              # Create the masks
  1685              causal_mask_mapping = {
  1686                  "full_attention": create_causal_mask(**mask_kwargs),
  1687                  "sliding_attention": create_sliding_window_causal_mask(**mask_kwargs),
  1688              }
  1689  
  1690          # embed positions
  1691          hidden_states = inputs_embeds
  1692          position_embeddings = {}
  1693          for layer_type in self.unique_layer_types:
  1694              position_embeddings[layer_type] = self.rotary_emb(hidden_states, position_ids, layer_type)
  1695  
  1696          # Initialize as empty dict, or reuse past shared states. We use a UserDict instead of built-in dict (it behaves
  1697          # the same) for fsdp2 support (otherwise, `_apply_to_tensors` rebuilds every dict it recurses into, and `shared_kv_states`
  1698          # is not correctly shared, see https://github.com/pytorch/pytorch/blob/v2.10.0/torch/distributed/utils.py#L223-L255)
  1699          shared_kv_states = kwargs.pop("shared_kv_states", UserDict())
  1700  
  1701          # decoder layers
  1702          for i, decoder_layer in enumerate(self.layers[: self.config.num_hidden_layers]):
  1703              per_layer_input = per_layer_inputs[:, :, i, :] if per_layer_inputs is not None else None
  1704  
  1705              hidden_states = decoder_layer(
  1706                  hidden_states,
  1707                  per_layer_input,
  1708                  shared_kv_states=shared_kv_states,
  1709                  position_embeddings=position_embeddings[self.config.layer_types[i]],
  1710                  attention_mask=causal_mask_mapping[self.config.layer_types[i]],
  1711                  position_ids=position_ids,
  1712                  past_key_values=past_key_values,
  1713                  **kwargs,
  1714              )
  1715  
  1716          hidden_states = self.norm(hidden_states)
  1717  
  1718          return Gemma4TextModelOutputWithPast(
  1719              last_hidden_state=hidden_states,
  1720              past_key_values=past_key_values,
  1721              shared_kv_states=shared_kv_states if kwargs.get("return_shared_kv_states", False) else None,
  1722          )
  1723  
  1724      def get_per_layer_inputs(self, input_ids: torch.Tensor | None, inputs_embeds: torch.Tensor | None) -> torch.Tensor:
  1725          """Compute the token-identity component of Per-Layer Embeddings (PLE).
  1726  
  1727          Looks up `input_ids` in `embed_tokens_per_layer` (a scaled embedding that multiplies
  1728          by `sqrt(hidden_size_per_layer_input)`) and reshapes the packed output from
  1729          `[batch, seq, num_hidden_layers * hidden_size_per_layer_input]` to
  1730          `[batch, seq, num_hidden_layers, hidden_size_per_layer_input]`.
  1731  
  1732          If only `inputs_embeds` is provided (no `input_ids`), reverses the main embedding
  1733          to recover `input_ids` for the PLE lookup.
  1734          """
  1735          if not self.hidden_size_per_layer_input:
  1736              raise RuntimeError(
  1737                  "Attempting to call get_per_layer_inputs() from a model initialized with a config that does not support"
  1738                  f" per-layer embeddings. {self.config}"
  1739              )
  1740  
  1741          # If only inputs_embeds are provided, reverse main embedding to find the input_ids - this allows to `generate`
  1742          # from `inputs_embeds` only as other models (otherwise it would need the value from both embeddings)
  1743          if input_ids is None:
  1744              with torch.no_grad():
  1745                  input_ids = (
  1746                      (
  1747                          inputs_embeds[:, :, None, :]
  1748                          == self.embed_tokens.weight[None, None, :, :] * self.config.hidden_size**0.5
  1749                      )
  1750                      .all(dim=3)
  1751                      .nonzero()[:, 2]
  1752                  )
  1753                  try:
  1754                      input_ids = input_ids.view(inputs_embeds.shape[:2])
  1755                  except RuntimeError:
  1756                      raise RuntimeError(
  1757                          "It seems like you tried to call `forward` from `inputs_embeds` without providing `input_ids`, and that "
  1758                          "the `inputs_embeds` you provided do not exactly match the embedding weights. Since Gemma4 needs to reverse "
  1759                          "the embedding to compute another embedding, make sure you provide exact `inputs_embeds`"
  1760                      )
  1761  
  1762          return self.embed_tokens_per_layer(input_ids).reshape(
  1763              *input_ids.shape,
  1764              self.config.num_hidden_layers,
  1765              self.hidden_size_per_layer_input,
  1766          )
  1767  
  1768      def project_per_layer_inputs(
  1769          self,
  1770          inputs_embeds: torch.Tensor,
  1771          per_layer_inputs: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  1772      ) -> torch.Tensor:
  1773          """Compute the context-aware component of PLE and combine with token-identity.
  1774  
  1775          Projects `inputs_embeds` through `per_layer_model_projection` (Linear), scales by
  1776          `1/sqrt(hidden_size)`, reshapes to `[batch, seq, num_layers, ple_dim]`, and normalizes
  1777          with `per_layer_projection_norm` (RMSNorm).
  1778  
  1779          If `per_layer_inputs` (the token-identity component from `get_per_layer_inputs()`)
  1780          is provided, combines both: `(context_projection + token_identity) * (1/sqrt(2))`.
  1781          If `per_layer_inputs` is None (e.g. for multimodal inputs where input_ids are not
  1782          available), returns just the context projection.
  1783          """
  1784          if not self.hidden_size_per_layer_input:
  1785              raise RuntimeError(
  1786                  "Attempting to call project_per_layer_inputs() from a model initialized with a config that does not"
  1787                  f" support per-layer embeddings. {self.config}"
  1788              )
  1789  
  1790          per_layer_projection = self.per_layer_model_projection(inputs_embeds) * self.per_layer_model_projection_scale
  1791          per_layer_projection = per_layer_projection.reshape(
  1792              *inputs_embeds.shape[:-1],
  1793              self.config.num_hidden_layers,
  1794              self.hidden_size_per_layer_input,
  1795          )
  1796          per_layer_projection = self.per_layer_projection_norm(per_layer_projection)
  1797  
  1798          if per_layer_inputs is None:
  1799              return per_layer_projection
  1800  
  1801          return (per_layer_projection + per_layer_inputs) * self.per_layer_input_scale
  1802  
  1803  
  1804  @auto_docstring(custom_intro="The base Gemma 4 language model with a language modeling head.")
  1805  class Gemma4ForCausalLM(Gemma4PreTrainedModel, GenerationMixin):
  1806      _tied_weights_keys = {"lm_head.weight": "model.embed_tokens.weight"}
  1807      _tp_plan = {"lm_head": "colwise_gather_output"}
  1808      _pp_plan = {"lm_head": (["hidden_states"], ["logits"])}
  1809      _fsdp_plan = {"lm_head": "keep_full_weight"}
  1810      config: Gemma4TextConfig
  1811      base_model_prefix = "model"
  1812  
  1813      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4TextConfig):
  1814          super().__init__(config)
  1815          self.model = Gemma4TextModel(config)
  1816          self.vocab_size = config.vocab_size
  1817          self.lm_head = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.vocab_size, bias=False)
  1818  
  1819          # Initialize weights and apply final processing
  1820          self.post_init()
  1821  
  1822      @can_return_tuple
  1823      @auto_docstring
  1824      def forward(
  1825          self,
  1826          input_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  1827          attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  1828          position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  1829          past_key_values: Cache | None = None,
  1830          inputs_embeds: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
  1831          labels: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  1832          use_cache: bool | None = None,
  1833          logits_to_keep: int | torch.Tensor = 0,
  1834          per_layer_inputs: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  1835          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
  1836      ) -> Gemma4CausalLMOutputWithPast:
  1837          r"""
  1838          per_layer_inputs (`torch.Tensor`, *optional*):
  1839              Pre-computed per-layer input text embeddings of shape `(batch_size, sequence_length, num_hidden_layers,
  1840              hidden_size_per_layer_input)`. When provided, these are used directly instead of being computed from `input_ids`
  1841              via `get_per_layer_inputs()` in the text model. If calling the `forward` with `inputs_embeds` instead of `input_ids`,
  1842              you should probably precompute them and forward them along `inputs_embeds`, otherwise recomputing them needs
  1843              to reverse the main embedding, which is expensive.
  1844  
  1845          Example:
  1846  
  1847          ```python
  1848          >>> from transformers import AutoTokenizer, Gemma4ForCausalLM
  1849  
  1850          >>> model = Gemma4ForCausalLM.from_pretrained("google/gemma-4-E2B-it")
  1851          >>> tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("google/gemma-4-E2B-it")
  1852  
  1853          >>> prompt = "What is your favorite condiment?"
  1854          >>> inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors="pt")
  1855  
  1856          >>> # Generate
  1857          >>> generate_ids = model.generate(inputs.input_ids, max_length=30)
  1858          >>> tokenizer.batch_decode(generate_ids, skip_special_tokens=True, clean_up_tokenization_spaces=False)[0]
  1859          "What is your favorite condiment?"
  1860          ```"""
  1861          # decoder outputs consists of (dec_features, layer_state, dec_hidden, dec_attn)
  1862          outputs: Gemma4TextModelOutputWithPast = self.model(
  1863              input_ids=input_ids,
  1864              attention_mask=attention_mask,
  1865              position_ids=position_ids,
  1866              past_key_values=past_key_values,
  1867              inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
  1868              per_layer_inputs=per_layer_inputs,
  1869              use_cache=use_cache,
  1870              **kwargs,
  1871          )
  1872  
  1873          hidden_states = outputs.last_hidden_state
  1874          # Only compute necessary logits, and do not upcast them to float if we are not computing the loss
  1875          slice_indices = slice(-logits_to_keep, None) if isinstance(logits_to_keep, int) else logits_to_keep
  1876          logits = self.lm_head(hidden_states[:, slice_indices, :])
  1877          if self.config.final_logit_softcapping is not None:
  1878              logits = logits / self.config.final_logit_softcapping
  1879              logits = torch.tanh(logits)
  1880              logits = logits * self.config.final_logit_softcapping
  1881  
  1882          loss = None
  1883          if labels is not None:
  1884              loss = self.loss_function(logits, labels, self.vocab_size, **kwargs)
  1885  
  1886          return Gemma4CausalLMOutputWithPast(
  1887              loss=loss,
  1888              logits=logits,
  1889              past_key_values=outputs.past_key_values,
  1890              hidden_states=outputs.hidden_states,
  1891              attentions=outputs.attentions,
  1892              shared_kv_states=outputs.shared_kv_states,
  1893          )
  1894  
  1895  
  1896  def sliding_window_mask_function(sliding_window: tuple[int, int]) -> Callable:
  1897      """
  1898      This creates uni/bidirectional attention mask with sliding window.
  1899      """
  1900  
  1901      def inner_mask(batch_idx: int, head_idx: int, q_idx: int, kv_idx: int) -> bool:
  1902          left_window_size, right_window_size = sliding_window
  1903  
  1904          dist = q_idx - kv_idx
  1905          left_mask = (dist >= 0) & (dist < left_window_size)
  1906          right_mask = (dist < 0) & (-dist < right_window_size)
  1907          return left_mask | right_mask
  1908  
  1909      return inner_mask
  1910  
  1911  
  1912  class Gemma4AudioModel(Gemma4PreTrainedModel):
  1913      """An audio encoder based on the [Universal Speech Model](https://huggingface.co/papers/2303.01037) architecture."""
  1914  
  1915      config: Gemma4AudioConfig
  1916      main_input_name = "input_features"
  1917      base_model_prefix = "model.audio_tower"  # prefix for Gemma4ForConditionalGeneration saved checkpoints, required for Gemma4AudioModel.from_pretrained()
  1918      _can_record_outputs = {
  1919          "hidden_states": Gemma4AudioLayer,
  1920          "attentions": Gemma4AudioAttention,
  1921      }
  1922  
  1923      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4AudioConfig):
  1924          super().__init__(config)
  1925          self.config = config
  1926  
  1927          self.subsample_conv_projection = Gemma4AudioSubSampleConvProjection(config)
  1928          self.rel_pos_enc = Gemma4AudioRelPositionalEncoding(config)
  1929          self.layers = nn.ModuleList(
  1930              [Gemma4AudioLayer(config, layer_idx) for layer_idx in range(config.num_hidden_layers)]
  1931          )
  1932          self.output_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.output_proj_dims, bias=True)
  1933  
  1934          self.post_init()
  1935  
  1936      def _convert_4d_mask_to_blocked_5d(self, mask_4d: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
  1937          """
  1938          Convert a standard 4D attention mask `[batch_size, 1, seq_len, seq_len]` to the 5D blocked format
  1939          `[batch_size, 1, num_blocks, chunk_size, context_size]` expected by the chunked local attention,
  1940          """
  1941          batch_size, _, seq_len, _ = mask_4d.shape
  1942          device = mask_4d.device
  1943  
  1944          chunk_size = self.config.attention_chunk_size
  1945          max_past_horizon = self.config.attention_context_left - 1
  1946          max_future_horizon = self.config.attention_context_right
  1947  
  1948          num_blocks = (seq_len + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size
  1949          padded_seq_len = num_blocks * chunk_size
  1950          pad_amount = padded_seq_len - seq_len
  1951  
  1952          mask_4d = F.pad(mask_4d, (0, pad_amount, 0, pad_amount), value=False)
  1953          mask_5d = mask_4d.reshape(batch_size, 1, num_blocks, chunk_size, padded_seq_len)
  1954          mask_5d = F.pad(mask_5d, (max_past_horizon, max_future_horizon), value=False)
  1955  
  1956          block_starts = torch.arange(num_blocks, device=device) * chunk_size
  1957          offsets = torch.arange(chunk_size + max_past_horizon + max_future_horizon, device=device)
  1958          kv_indices = block_starts[:, None] + offsets[None, :]
  1959          kv_indices = kv_indices[None, None, :, None, :].expand(batch_size, 1, -1, chunk_size, -1)
  1960  
  1961          return mask_5d.gather(-1, kv_indices)
  1962  
  1963      @merge_with_config_defaults
  1964      @capture_outputs
  1965      @auto_docstring(custom_intro="Encodes audio features to soft tokens.")
  1966      def forward(
  1967          self,
  1968          input_features: torch.Tensor,
  1969          attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  1970          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
  1971      ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.BoolTensor]:
  1972          hidden_states, output_mask = self.subsample_conv_projection(input_features, attention_mask)
  1973          position_embeddings = self.rel_pos_enc(hidden_states)
  1974  
  1975          attention_mask = create_bidirectional_mask(
  1976              config=self.config,
  1977              inputs_embeds=hidden_states,
  1978              attention_mask=output_mask,
  1979              and_mask_function=sliding_window_mask_function(
  1980                  (self.config.attention_context_left - 1, self.config.attention_context_right)
  1981              ),
  1982          )
  1983          if attention_mask is not None:
  1984              attention_mask = self._convert_4d_mask_to_blocked_5d(attention_mask)
  1985  
  1986          for encoder_layer in self.layers[: self.config.num_hidden_layers]:
  1987              hidden_states = encoder_layer(
  1988                  hidden_states,
  1989                  attention_mask=attention_mask,
  1990                  position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
  1991                  **kwargs,
  1992              )
  1993  
  1994          hidden_states = self.output_proj(hidden_states)
  1995          return Gemma4AudioModelOutput(last_hidden_state=hidden_states, attention_mask=output_mask)
  1996  
  1997  
  1998  class Gemma4VisionModel(Gemma4PreTrainedModel):
  1999      """The Gemma 4 Vision Encoder."""
  2000  
  2001      config = Gemma4VisionConfig
  2002      _can_record_outputs = {
  2003          "hidden_states": Gemma4VisionEncoderLayer,
  2004          "attentions": Gemma4VisionAttention,
  2005      }
  2006  
  2007      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4VisionConfig):
  2008          super().__init__(config)
  2009          self.patch_embedder = Gemma4VisionPatchEmbedder(config)
  2010          self.encoder = Gemma4VisionEncoder(config)
  2011          self.pooler = Gemma4VisionPooler(config)
  2012  
  2013          if self.config.standardize:
  2014              self.std_bias = nn.Buffer(torch.empty(self.config.hidden_size))
  2015              self.std_scale = nn.Buffer(torch.empty(self.config.hidden_size))
  2016  
  2017          self.post_init()
  2018  
  2019      @merge_with_config_defaults
  2020      @capture_outputs
  2021      @auto_docstring(custom_intro="Encodes image pixels to soft tokens from patches.")
  2022      def forward(
  2023          self,
  2024          pixel_values: torch.FloatTensor,
  2025          pixel_position_ids: torch.LongTensor,
  2026          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
  2027      ) -> BaseModelOutputWithPast:
  2028          r"""
  2029          pixel_values (`torch.FloatTensor` or `list[torch.FloatTensor]`):
  2030              The images to encode. Either a single `[batch, channels, height, width]` tensor
  2031              (all images same size) or a list of `[1, channels, height, width]` tensors (different sizes).
  2032          pixel_position_ids (`torch.LongTensor` of shape `(batch_size, max_patches, 2)`):
  2033              The patch positions as (x, y) coordinates in the image. Padding patches are indicated by (-1, -1).
  2034          """
  2035          pooling_kernel_size = self.config.pooling_kernel_size
  2036          output_length = pixel_values.shape[-2] // (pooling_kernel_size * pooling_kernel_size)
  2037  
  2038          padding_positions = (pixel_position_ids == -1).all(dim=-1)
  2039          inputs_embeds = self.patch_embedder(pixel_values, pixel_position_ids, padding_positions)
  2040          output = self.encoder(
  2041              inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
  2042              attention_mask=~padding_positions,  # encoder expects True=valid, padding_positions is True=padding
  2043              pixel_position_ids=pixel_position_ids,
  2044              **kwargs,
  2045          )
  2046  
  2047          hidden_states, pooler_mask = self.pooler(
  2048              hidden_states=output.last_hidden_state,
  2049              pixel_position_ids=pixel_position_ids,
  2050              padding_positions=padding_positions,
  2051              output_length=output_length,
  2052          )
  2053  
  2054          # Strip padding tokens. pooler_mask is True = valid, False = padding.
  2055          hidden_states = hidden_states[pooler_mask]
  2056  
  2057          # The pooler returns float32-scaled features. Standardize in float32 (the std_bias
  2058          # subtraction cancels large values) and cast back to the working dtype.
  2059          if self.config.standardize:
  2060              hidden_states = (hidden_states - self.std_bias.float()) * self.std_scale.float()
  2061          hidden_states = hidden_states.to(inputs_embeds.dtype)
  2062  
  2063          return BaseModelOutputWithPast(last_hidden_state=hidden_states)
  2064  
  2065  
  2066  class Gemma4MultimodalEmbedder(nn.Module):
  2067      """Embeds token ids or soft tokens for multimodal content into language model space."""
  2068  
  2069      def __init__(
  2070          self,
  2071          multimodal_config: Gemma4AudioConfig | Gemma4VisionConfig,
  2072          text_config: Gemma4TextConfig,
  2073      ):
  2074          super().__init__()
  2075  
  2076          self.multimodal_hidden_size = getattr(multimodal_config, "output_proj_dims", multimodal_config.hidden_size)
  2077          self.eps = multimodal_config.rms_norm_eps
  2078          self.text_hidden_size = text_config.hidden_size
  2079          self.embedding_projection = nn.Linear(self.multimodal_hidden_size, self.text_hidden_size, bias=False)
  2080          self.embedding_pre_projection_norm = Gemma4RMSNorm(self.multimodal_hidden_size, eps=self.eps, with_scale=False)
  2081  
  2082      def forward(self, inputs_embeds: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
  2083          """Embeds token ids or soft tokens for multimodal content into language model space.
  2084          Args:
  2085              inputs_embeds: A torch.Tensor containing the soft tokens to embed.
  2086          Returns:
  2087              A torch.Tensor of embeddings with shape `[batch_size, seq_len, self.config.text_config.hidden_size]`.
  2088          """
  2089          embs_normed = self.embedding_pre_projection_norm(inputs_embeds)
  2090          return self.embedding_projection(embs_normed)
  2091  
  2092  
  2093  def create_masks_for_vision_model(
  2094      config: PreTrainedConfig,
  2095      inputs_embeds: torch.Tensor,
  2096      attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
  2097      past_key_values: Cache | None,
  2098      position_ids: torch.Tensor | None,
  2099      block_sequence_ids: torch.Tensor,
  2100  ) -> dict:
  2101      """Create full_attention and sliding_attention masks with correct composition.
  2102  
  2103      For global (full attention) layers:  causal only (no bidirectional)
  2104      For local (sliding window) layers:  AND(sliding_window, OR(causal, blockwise))
  2105  
  2106      Unlike Gemma 3 (which applies bidirectional attention on all layers), Gemma 4
  2107      explicitly disables bidirectional attention on global attention layers.
  2108      """
  2109      mask_kwargs = {
  2110          "config": config,
  2111          "inputs_embeds": inputs_embeds,
  2112          "attention_mask": attention_mask,
  2113          "past_key_values": past_key_values,
  2114          "position_ids": position_ids,
  2115      }
  2116  
  2117      # Full attention: causal only — no bidirectional blockwise overlay.
  2118      full_mask = create_causal_mask(**mask_kwargs)
  2119  
  2120      # We need to manually pad the sequence IDs for the sliding mask
  2121      # as it's passed as an `or_mask_function` which bypasses internal padding.
  2122      early_exit, _, _, _, kv_length, _, kv_offset = _preprocess_mask_arguments(
  2123          **mask_kwargs,
  2124          layer_idx=0,
  2125      )
  2126      if early_exit:
  2127          padded_block_sequence_ids = block_sequence_ids
  2128      else:
  2129          padded_block_sequence_ids = maybe_pad_block_sequence_ids(
  2130              block_sequence_ids, attention_mask, kv_length, kv_offset
  2131          )
  2132  
  2133      # Sliding attention: AND(sliding_window, OR(causal, blockwise))
  2134      # Pass blockwise as or_mask_function (applied as step 2 in create_causal_mask)
  2135      # Pass sliding_window as and_mask_function (applied as step 3, after OR)
  2136      sliding_mask = create_causal_mask(
  2137          **mask_kwargs,
  2138          or_mask_function=blockwise_overlay(padded_block_sequence_ids),
  2139          and_mask_function=sliding_window_overlay(config.sliding_window),
  2140      )
  2141  
  2142      return {
  2143          "full_attention": full_mask,
  2144          "sliding_attention": sliding_mask,
  2145      }
  2146  
  2147  
  2148  def get_block_sequence_ids_for_mask(mm_token_type_ids: torch.Tensor, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
  2149      mm_token_type_ids = mm_token_type_ids.to(device)
  2150  
  2151      is_vision = (mm_token_type_ids == 1) | (mm_token_type_ids == 2)
  2152      is_prev_vision = torch.roll(is_vision, shifts=1, dims=-1)
  2153      is_prev_vision[..., 0] = False
  2154      new_vision_starts = is_vision & ~is_prev_vision
  2155      vision_group_ids = torch.cumsum(new_vision_starts.int(), dim=1) - 1
  2156      block_sequence_ids = torch.where(is_vision, vision_group_ids, -1)
  2157      return block_sequence_ids
  2158  
  2159  
  2160  @auto_docstring(
  2161      custom_intro="""
  2162      The base Gemma 4 model comprising a vision backbone, an audio backbone, and a language model without a
  2163      language modeling head.
  2164      """
  2165  )
  2166  class Gemma4Model(Gemma4PreTrainedModel):
  2167      # we are filtering the logits/labels so we shouldn't divide the loss based on num_items_in_batch
  2168      accepts_loss_kwargs = False
  2169  
  2170      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4Config):
  2171          super().__init__(config)
  2172          self.vision_tower = AutoModel.from_config(config.vision_config) if config.vision_config is not None else None
  2173          self.vocab_size = config.text_config.vocab_size
  2174  
  2175          language_model = AutoModel.from_config(config=config.text_config)
  2176          self.language_model = language_model
  2177          self.vocab_size_per_layer_input = config.text_config.vocab_size_per_layer_input
  2178          self.audio_tower = AutoModel.from_config(config.audio_config) if config.audio_config is not None else None
  2179          self.embed_vision = (
  2180              Gemma4MultimodalEmbedder(config.vision_config, config.text_config)
  2181              if config.vision_config is not None
  2182              else None
  2183          )
  2184          self.embed_audio = (
  2185              Gemma4MultimodalEmbedder(config.audio_config, config.text_config)
  2186              if config.audio_config is not None
  2187              else None
  2188          )
  2189          self.post_init()
  2190  
  2191      @can_return_tuple
  2192      @auto_docstring(custom_intro="Projects the last hidden state from the vision model into language model space.")
  2193      def get_image_features(
  2194          self,
  2195          pixel_values: torch.FloatTensor,
  2196          image_position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2197          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
  2198      ) -> BaseModelOutputWithPooling:
  2199          r"""
  2200          image_position_ids (`torch.LongTensor` of shape `(batch_size, max_patches, 2)`, *optional*):
  2201              The patch positions as (x, y) coordinates in the image. Padding patches are indicated by (-1, -1).
  2202          """
  2203          vision_outputs = self.vision_tower(
  2204              pixel_values=pixel_values,
  2205              pixel_position_ids=image_position_ids,
  2206              **kwargs,
  2207          )
  2208          last_hidden_state = vision_outputs.last_hidden_state
  2209          pooler_output = self.embed_vision(inputs_embeds=last_hidden_state)
  2210  
  2211          output_length = pixel_values.shape[-2] // (self.config.vision_config.pooling_kernel_size**2)
  2212          k_squared = int((image_position_ids.shape[1] // output_length) ** 0.5) ** 2
  2213          non_pad_mask = (image_position_ids != -1).all(dim=-1)
  2214          split_sizes = (non_pad_mask.sum(dim=-1) // k_squared).tolist()
  2215          vision_outputs.pooler_output = torch.split(pooler_output, split_sizes)
  2216          return vision_outputs
  2217  
  2218      def get_placeholder_mask(
  2219          self,
  2220          input_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2221          inputs_embeds: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
  2222      ) -> tuple[torch.BoolTensor, torch.BoolTensor, torch.BoolTensor]:
  2223          """
  2224          Obtains mask for multimodal placeholders (replaced by soft tokens) and hard text tokens.
  2225  
  2226          Masks will be obtained from `mm_token_type_ids`, `input_ids`, or `inputs_embeds` as available and in that
  2227          precedence order. If passing `input_ids` or `inputs_embeds`, the image mask will be derived using
  2228          `config.image_token_id`. Same goes for audio and video masks
  2229  
  2230          Args:
  2231              input_ids: A tensor containing the hard token IDs from the text tokenizer.
  2232              inputs_embeds: A tensor containing the embeddings for all hard text tokens.
  2233  
  2234          Returns:
  2235              image_mask, video_mask, audio_mask
  2236          """
  2237          if input_ids is not None:
  2238              special_image_mask = input_ids == self.config.image_token_id
  2239              special_video_mask = input_ids == self.config.video_token_id
  2240              special_audio_mask = input_ids == self.config.audio_token_id
  2241          else:
  2242              special_image_mask = (
  2243                  inputs_embeds
  2244                  == self.get_input_embeddings()(
  2245                      torch.full((), self.config.image_token_id, dtype=torch.long, device=inputs_embeds.device)
  2246                  )
  2247              ).all(-1)
  2248              special_video_mask = (
  2249                  inputs_embeds
  2250                  == self.get_input_embeddings()(
  2251                      torch.full((), self.config.video_token_id, dtype=torch.long, device=inputs_embeds.device)
  2252                  )
  2253              ).all(-1)
  2254              special_audio_mask = (
  2255                  inputs_embeds
  2256                  == self.get_input_embeddings()(
  2257                      torch.full((), self.config.audio_token_id, dtype=torch.long, device=inputs_embeds.device)
  2258                  )
  2259              ).all(-1)
  2260  
  2261          return special_image_mask, special_video_mask, special_audio_mask
  2262  
  2263      @merge_with_config_defaults
  2264      @can_return_tuple
  2265      @auto_docstring
  2266      def forward(
  2267          self,
  2268          input_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2269          pixel_values: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
  2270          pixel_values_videos: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
  2271          input_features: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
  2272          attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  2273          input_features_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  2274          position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2275          past_key_values: Cache | None = None,
  2276          mm_token_type_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2277          inputs_embeds: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
  2278          use_cache: bool | None = None,
  2279          image_position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2280          video_position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2281          per_layer_inputs: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  2282          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
  2283      ) -> Gemma4ModelOutputWithPast:
  2284          r"""
  2285          input_features_mask (`torch.FloatTensor` of shape `(num_images, seq_length)`):
  2286              The attention mask for the input audio.
  2287          image_position_ids (`torch.LongTensor` of shape `(batch_size, max_patches, 2)`, *optional*):
  2288              2D patch position coordinates from the image processor, with `(-1, -1)` indicating padding.
  2289              Passed through to the vision encoder for positional embedding computation.
  2290          video_position_ids (`torch.LongTensor` of shape `(num_videos, num_frames, max_patches, 2)`, *optional*):
  2291              2D patch position coordinates from the video processor, with `(-1, -1)` indicating padding.
  2292              Passed through to the vision encoder for positional embedding computation.
  2293          per_layer_inputs (`torch.Tensor`, *optional*):
  2294              Pre-computed per-layer input text embeddings of shape `(batch_size, sequence_length, num_hidden_layers,
  2295              hidden_size_per_layer_input)`. When provided, these are used directly instead of being computed from `input_ids`
  2296              via `get_per_layer_inputs()` in the text model. If calling the `forward` with `inputs_embeds` instead of `input_ids`,
  2297              you should probably precompute them and forward them along `inputs_embeds`, otherwise recomputing them needs
  2298              to reverse the main embedding, which is expensive.
  2299          """
  2300          if (input_ids is None) ^ (inputs_embeds is not None):
  2301              raise ValueError("You must specify exactly one of input_ids or inputs_embeds")
  2302  
  2303          if input_ids is not None and per_layer_inputs is not None:
  2304              raise ValueError("You cannot specify per_layer_inputs if input_ids is provided")
  2305  
  2306          image_mask, video_mask, audio_mask = self.get_placeholder_mask(input_ids, inputs_embeds)
  2307          multimodal_mask = image_mask | video_mask | audio_mask
  2308  
  2309          # Replace image id with PAD if the image token if OOV, to avoid index-errors
  2310          llm_input_ids = None
  2311          if inputs_embeds is None:
  2312              llm_input_ids = input_ids.clone()
  2313              llm_input_ids = torch.where(multimodal_mask, self.config.text_config.pad_token_id, llm_input_ids)
  2314              inputs_embeds = self.get_input_embeddings()(llm_input_ids)
  2315  
  2316          if per_layer_inputs is None and self.config.get_text_config().hidden_size_per_layer_input:
  2317              pad_embedding = self.language_model.embed_tokens.weight[self.config.text_config.pad_token_id, :]
  2318              multimodal_mask = multimodal_mask.to(inputs_embeds.device)
  2319              llm_inputs_embeds = torch.where(multimodal_mask[..., None], pad_embedding.view(1, 1, -1), inputs_embeds)
  2320              per_layer_inputs = self.language_model.get_per_layer_inputs(llm_input_ids, llm_inputs_embeds)
  2321  
  2322          # Merge text and images
  2323          if pixel_values is not None:
  2324              image_features = self.get_image_features(pixel_values, image_position_ids, return_dict=True).pooler_output
  2325              image_features = torch.cat(image_features, dim=0).to(inputs_embeds.device, inputs_embeds.dtype)
  2326  
  2327              # Confirm the number of soft tokens from the vision tower matches the number of slots in the embeddings.
  2328              n_image_tokens = image_mask.sum()
  2329              image_mask = image_mask.unsqueeze(-1).expand_as(inputs_embeds).to(inputs_embeds.device)
  2330              torch_compilable_check(
  2331                  inputs_embeds[image_mask].numel() == image_features.numel(),
  2332                  f"Image features and image tokens do not match, tokens: {n_image_tokens}, features:"
  2333                  f" {image_features.shape[0]}",
  2334              )
  2335  
  2336              inputs_embeds = inputs_embeds.masked_scatter(
  2337                  image_mask.to(inputs_embeds.device), image_features.to(inputs_embeds.device)
  2338              )
  2339  
  2340          if pixel_values_videos is not None:
  2341              video_features = self.get_video_features(
  2342                  pixel_values_videos, video_position_ids, return_dict=True
  2343              ).pooler_output
  2344              video_features = video_features.to(inputs_embeds.device, inputs_embeds.dtype)
  2345  
  2346              # Confirm the number of soft tokens from the vision tower matches the number of slots in the embeddings.
  2347              n_video_tokens = video_mask.sum()
  2348              video_mask = video_mask.unsqueeze(-1).expand_as(inputs_embeds).to(inputs_embeds.device)
  2349              torch_compilable_check(
  2350                  inputs_embeds[video_mask].numel() == video_features.numel(),
  2351                  f"Video features and video tokens do not match, tokens: {n_video_tokens}, features:"
  2352                  f" {video_features.shape[0]}",
  2353              )
  2354  
  2355              inputs_embeds = inputs_embeds.masked_scatter(
  2356                  video_mask.to(inputs_embeds.device), video_features.to(inputs_embeds.device)
  2357              )
  2358  
  2359          # Merge text and audio
  2360          if input_features is not None and input_features_mask is not None:
  2361              audio_output = self.get_audio_features(input_features, input_features_mask, return_dict=True)
  2362              audio_features = audio_output.pooler_output
  2363              audio_mask_from_encoder = audio_output.attention_mask  # True = valid
  2364  
  2365              # Strip padding tokens: only keep real (non-padding) audio soft tokens.
  2366              # audio_mask_from_encoder is True for valid positions, False for padding tokens.
  2367              # This mirrors the vision encoder's padding stripping (see Gemma4VisionEncoder.forward).
  2368              audio_features = audio_features[audio_mask_from_encoder.to(audio_features.device)]
  2369  
  2370              n_audio_tokens = audio_mask.sum()
  2371              audio_mask = audio_mask.unsqueeze(-1).expand_as(inputs_embeds).to(inputs_embeds.device)
  2372              torch_compilable_check(
  2373                  inputs_embeds[audio_mask].numel() == audio_features.numel(),
  2374                  f"Audio features and audio tokens do not match, tokens: {n_audio_tokens}, features:"
  2375                  f" {audio_features.shape[0] * audio_features.shape[1]}",
  2376              )
  2377  
  2378              inputs_embeds = inputs_embeds.masked_scatter(
  2379                  audio_mask.to(inputs_embeds.device), audio_features.to(inputs_embeds.device, inputs_embeds.dtype)
  2380              )
  2381  
  2382          # It may already have been prepared by, e.g., `generate`
  2383          if position_ids is None:
  2384              past_seen_tokens = past_key_values.get_seq_length() if past_key_values is not None else 0
  2385              position_ids = torch.arange(inputs_embeds.shape[1], device=inputs_embeds.device) + past_seen_tokens
  2386              position_ids = position_ids.unsqueeze(0)
  2387  
  2388          if not isinstance(causal_mask_mapping := attention_mask, dict):
  2389              mask_kwargs = {
  2390                  "config": self.config.get_text_config(),
  2391                  "inputs_embeds": inputs_embeds,
  2392                  "attention_mask": attention_mask,
  2393                  "past_key_values": past_key_values,
  2394                  "position_ids": position_ids,
  2395              }
  2396  
  2397              text_config = self.config.get_text_config()
  2398              use_bidir = text_config.use_bidirectional_attention == "vision"
  2399  
  2400              if use_bidir and mm_token_type_ids is not None:
  2401                  block_sequence_ids = get_block_sequence_ids_for_mask(mm_token_type_ids, device=inputs_embeds.device)
  2402                  causal_mask_mapping = create_masks_for_vision_model(
  2403                      block_sequence_ids=block_sequence_ids,
  2404                      **mask_kwargs,
  2405                  )
  2406              else:
  2407                  # Smaller Gemma models (use_bidirectional_attention=None) or
  2408                  # text-only inputs use standard causal masking
  2409                  causal_mask_mapping = create_masks_for_generate(**mask_kwargs)
  2410  
  2411          outputs = self.language_model(
  2412              per_layer_inputs=per_layer_inputs,
  2413              attention_mask=causal_mask_mapping,
  2414              position_ids=position_ids,
  2415              past_key_values=past_key_values,
  2416              inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
  2417              use_cache=use_cache,
  2418              return_dict=True,
  2419              **kwargs,
  2420          )
  2421  
  2422          return Gemma4ModelOutputWithPast(
  2423              last_hidden_state=outputs.last_hidden_state,
  2424              past_key_values=outputs.past_key_values,
  2425              hidden_states=outputs.hidden_states,
  2426              attentions=outputs.attentions,
  2427              image_hidden_states=image_features if pixel_values is not None else None,
  2428              audio_hidden_states=audio_features if input_features is not None else None,
  2429              shared_kv_states=outputs.shared_kv_states,
  2430          )
  2431  
  2432      def get_per_layer_input_embeddings(self):
  2433          return self.language_model.embed_tokens_per_layer
  2434  
  2435      def set_per_layer_input_embeddings(self, value):
  2436          self.language_model.embed_tokens_per_layer = value
  2437  
  2438      @can_return_tuple
  2439      @auto_docstring(custom_intro="Projects the last hidden state from the audio encoder into language model space.")
  2440      def get_audio_features(
  2441          self,
  2442          input_features: torch.Tensor,
  2443          input_features_mask: torch.Tensor,
  2444          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
  2445      ) -> tuple | Gemma4AudioModelOutput:
  2446          r"""
  2447          input_features (`torch.FloatTensor` of shape `(num_images, seq_length, num_features)`):
  2448              The tensors corresponding to the input audio.
  2449          input_features_mask (`torch.FloatTensor` of shape `(num_images, seq_length)`):
  2450              The attention mask for the input audio.
  2451          """
  2452          if self.audio_tower is None:
  2453              raise ValueError(
  2454                  "Audio features were requested, but the model was initialized without an audio_config. "
  2455                  "Cannot process audio without an audio tower and audio embedder."
  2456              )
  2457  
  2458          audio_outputs = self.audio_tower(input_features, input_features_mask, return_dict=True, **kwargs)
  2459          audio_outputs.pooler_output = self.embed_audio(inputs_embeds=audio_outputs.last_hidden_state)
  2460  
  2461          return audio_outputs
  2462  
  2463      @can_return_tuple
  2464      @auto_docstring(custom_intro="Projects the last hidden state from the vision encoder into language model space.")
  2465      def get_video_features(
  2466          self,
  2467          pixel_values_videos: torch.FloatTensor,
  2468          video_position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2469          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
  2470      ) -> BaseModelOutputWithPooling:
  2471          r"""
  2472          video_position_ids (`torch.LongTensor` of shape `(num_videos, num_frames, max_patches, 2)`, *optional*):
  2473              2D patch position coordinates from the video processor, with `(-1, -1)` indicating padding.
  2474              Passed through to the vision encoder for positional embedding computation.
  2475          """
  2476          pixel_values_videos = pixel_values_videos.flatten(0, 1)
  2477          vision_outputs = self.vision_tower(
  2478              pixel_values=pixel_values_videos,
  2479              pixel_position_ids=video_position_ids.flatten(0, 1),
  2480              **kwargs,
  2481          )
  2482          last_hidden_state = vision_outputs.last_hidden_state
  2483          pooler_output = self.embed_vision(inputs_embeds=last_hidden_state)
  2484  
  2485          output_length = pixel_values_videos.shape[-2] // (self.config.vision_config.pooling_kernel_size**2)
  2486          k_squared = int((pixel_values_videos.shape[-2] // output_length) ** 0.5) ** 2
  2487          non_pad_mask = (video_position_ids != -1).all(dim=-1)
  2488          split_sizes = (non_pad_mask.sum(dim=(-2, -1)) // k_squared).tolist()
  2489  
  2490          vision_outputs.pooler_output = torch.split(pooler_output, split_sizes)
  2491          return vision_outputs
  2492  
  2493  
  2494  @auto_docstring(
  2495      custom_intro="""
  2496      The base Gemma 4 model comprising a vision backbone, an audio backbone, a language model, and a language modeling
  2497      head.
  2498      """
  2499  )
  2500  class Gemma4ForConditionalGeneration(Gemma4PreTrainedModel, GenerationMixin):
  2501      _tied_weights_keys = {"lm_head.weight": "model.language_model.embed_tokens.weight"}
  2502      accepts_loss_kwargs = False
  2503      base_model_prefix = "model"
  2504  
  2505      def __init__(self, config: Gemma4Config):
  2506          super().__init__(config)
  2507          self.model = Gemma4Model(config)
  2508          self.lm_head = nn.Linear(config.text_config.hidden_size, config.text_config.vocab_size, bias=False)
  2509          self.post_init()
  2510  
  2511      @auto_docstring
  2512      def get_image_features(
  2513          self,
  2514          pixel_values: torch.FloatTensor,
  2515          image_position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2516          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
  2517      ):
  2518          r"""
  2519          image_position_ids (`torch.LongTensor` of shape `(batch_size, max_patches, 2)`, *optional*):
  2520              2D patch position coordinates from the image processor, with `(-1, -1)` indicating padding.
  2521              Passed through to the vision encoder for positional embedding computation.
  2522          """
  2523          return self.model.get_image_features(pixel_values, image_position_ids, **kwargs)
  2524  
  2525      @can_return_tuple
  2526      @auto_docstring
  2527      def forward(
  2528          self,
  2529          input_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2530          pixel_values: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
  2531          pixel_values_videos: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
  2532          input_features: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
  2533          attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  2534          input_features_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  2535          position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2536          image_position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2537          video_position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2538          past_key_values: Cache | None = None,
  2539          mm_token_type_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2540          inputs_embeds: torch.FloatTensor | None = None,
  2541          labels: torch.LongTensor | None = None,
  2542          use_cache: bool | None = None,
  2543          logits_to_keep: int | torch.Tensor = 0,
  2544          per_layer_inputs: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  2545          **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],
  2546      ) -> Gemma4CausalLMOutputWithPast:
  2547          r"""
  2548          input_features_mask (`torch.FloatTensor` of shape `(num_images, seq_length)`):
  2549              The attention mask for the input audio.
  2550          image_position_ids (`torch.LongTensor` of shape `(batch_size, max_patches, 2)`, *optional*):
  2551              2D patch position coordinates from the image processor, with `(-1, -1)` indicating padding.
  2552              Passed through to the vision encoder for positional embedding computation.
  2553          video_position_ids (`torch.LongTensor` of shape `(num_videos, num_frames, max_patches, 2)`, *optional*):
  2554              2D patch position coordinates from the video processor, with `(-1, -1)` indicating padding.
  2555              Passed through to the vision encoder for positional embedding computation.
  2556          per_layer_inputs (`torch.Tensor`, *optional*):
  2557              Pre-computed per-layer input text embeddings of shape `(batch_size, sequence_length, num_hidden_layers,
  2558              hidden_size_per_layer_input)`. When provided, these are used directly instead of being computed from `input_ids`
  2559              via `get_per_layer_inputs()` in the text model. If calling the `forward` with `inputs_embeds` instead of `input_ids`,
  2560              you should probably precompute them and forward them along `inputs_embeds`, otherwise recomputing them needs
  2561              to reverse the main embedding, which is expensive.
  2562          """
  2563          outputs = self.model(
  2564              input_ids=input_ids,
  2565              pixel_values=pixel_values,
  2566              pixel_values_videos=pixel_values_videos,
  2567              input_features=input_features,
  2568              attention_mask=attention_mask,
  2569              input_features_mask=input_features_mask,
  2570              position_ids=position_ids,
  2571              past_key_values=past_key_values,
  2572              mm_token_type_ids=mm_token_type_ids,
  2573              inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
  2574              per_layer_inputs=per_layer_inputs,
  2575              labels=labels,
  2576              use_cache=use_cache,
  2577              image_position_ids=image_position_ids,
  2578              video_position_ids=video_position_ids,
  2579              return_dict=True,
  2580              **kwargs,
  2581          )
  2582  
  2583          hidden_states = outputs.last_hidden_state
  2584          # Only compute necessary logits, and do not upcast them to float if we are not computing the loss
  2585          slice_indices = slice(-logits_to_keep, None) if isinstance(logits_to_keep, int) else logits_to_keep
  2586          logits = self.lm_head(hidden_states[:, slice_indices, :])
  2587          if (final_logit_softcapping := self.config.get_text_config().final_logit_softcapping) is not None:
  2588              logits = logits / final_logit_softcapping
  2589              logits = torch.tanh(logits)
  2590              logits = logits * final_logit_softcapping
  2591  
  2592          loss = None
  2593          if labels is not None:
  2594              loss = self.loss_function(logits, labels, self.config.get_text_config().vocab_size, **kwargs)
  2595  
  2596          return Gemma4CausalLMOutputWithPast(
  2597              loss=loss,
  2598              logits=logits,
  2599              past_key_values=outputs.past_key_values,
  2600              hidden_states=outputs.hidden_states,
  2601              attentions=outputs.attentions,
  2602              image_hidden_states=outputs.image_hidden_states,
  2603              audio_hidden_states=outputs.audio_hidden_states,
  2604              shared_kv_states=outputs.shared_kv_states,
  2605          )
  2606  
  2607      def get_per_layer_input_embeddings(self):
  2608          return self.model.get_per_layer_input_embeddings()
  2609  
  2610      def set_per_layer_input_embeddings(self, value):
  2611          self.model.set_per_layer_input_embeddings(value)
  2612  
  2613      @staticmethod
  2614      def create_masks_for_generate(
  2615          config: PreTrainedConfig,
  2616          inputs_embeds: torch.Tensor,
  2617          attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
  2618          past_key_values: Cache | None,
  2619          position_ids: torch.Tensor | None,
  2620          mm_token_type_ids: torch.Tensor | None = None,
  2621          is_first_iteration: bool | None = False,
  2622          **kwargs,
  2623      ) -> dict:
  2624          mask_kwargs = {
  2625              "config": config.get_text_config(),
  2626              "inputs_embeds": inputs_embeds,
  2627              "attention_mask": attention_mask,
  2628              "past_key_values": past_key_values,
  2629              "position_ids": position_ids,
  2630          }
  2631  
  2632          text_config = config.get_text_config()
  2633          use_bidir = getattr(text_config, "use_bidirectional_attention", None) == "vision"
  2634  
  2635          if use_bidir and mm_token_type_ids is not None:
  2636              block_sequence_ids = get_block_sequence_ids_for_mask(mm_token_type_ids, device=inputs_embeds.device)
  2637              return create_masks_for_vision_model(
  2638                  block_sequence_ids=block_sequence_ids,
  2639                  **mask_kwargs,
  2640              )
  2641  
  2642          return create_masks_for_generate(**mask_kwargs)
  2643  
  2644      def prepare_inputs_for_generation(self, input_ids, use_cache=True, is_first_iteration=False, **kwargs):
  2645          model_inputs = super().prepare_inputs_for_generation(
  2646              input_ids, use_cache=use_cache, is_first_iteration=is_first_iteration, **kwargs
  2647          )
  2648          if not (is_first_iteration or not use_cache):
  2649              # Don't pass to not apply bidirectional mask on top
  2650              model_inputs["mm_token_type_ids"] = None
  2651          # If `per_layer_inputs` was provided along with `inputs_embeds` for first forward, drop it for subsequent forwards
  2652          if not is_first_iteration:
  2653              _ = model_inputs.pop("per_layer_inputs", None)
  2654  
  2655          return model_inputs
  2656
