    34  def dynamic_rope_update(rope_forward):
    35      """
    36      Decorator function to update the RoPE parameters in the forward pass, if the model is using a dynamic RoPE
    37      (i.e. a RoPE implementation that may recompute its frequencies in the forward pass).
    38  
    39      Args:
    40          rope_forward (Callable):
    41              The forward pass of the RoPE implementation.
    42  
    43      Returns:
    44          The decorated forward pass.
    45      """
    46  
    47      def longrope_frequency_update(self, position_ids, device, layer_type=None):
    48          """Longrope uses long factor if sequence is larger than original pretraining length, short otherwise."""
    49          seq_len = torch.max(position_ids) + 1
    50  
    51          if layer_type is None:
    52              rope_type = self.rope_type
    53              original_inv_freq = self.original_inv_freq
    54              prefix = ""
    55              original_max_position_embeddings = self.config.rope_parameters["original_max_position_embeddings"]
    56          else:
    57              rope_type = self.rope_type[layer_type]
    58              original_inv_freq = getattr(self, f"{layer_type}_original_inv_freq")
    59              prefix = f"{layer_type}_"
    60              original_max_position_embeddings = self.config.rope_parameters[layer_type][
    61                  "original_max_position_embeddings"
    62              ]
    63  
    64          if seq_len > original_max_position_embeddings:
    65              if not hasattr(self, f"{layer_type}_long_inv_freq"):
    66                  rope_init_fn = ROPE_INIT_FUNCTIONS[rope_type]
    67                  long_inv_freq, _ = rope_init_fn(
    68                      self.config,
    69                      device,
    70                      seq_len=original_max_position_embeddings + 1,
    71                      layer_type=layer_type,
    72                  )
    73              self.register_buffer(f"{prefix}inv_freq", long_inv_freq, persistent=False)
    74              setattr(self, f"{prefix}long_inv_freq", long_inv_freq)
    75          else:
    76              # This .to() is needed if the model has been moved to a device after being initialized (because
    77              # the buffer is automatically moved, but not the original copy)
    78              original_inv_freq = original_inv_freq.to(device)
    79              self.register_buffer(f"{prefix}inv_freq", original_inv_freq, persistent=False)
    80              setattr(self, f"{prefix}original_inv_freq", original_inv_freq)
    81  
    82      def dynamic_frequency_update(self, position_ids, device, layer_type=None):
    83          """
    84          dynamic RoPE layers should recompute `inv_freq` in the following situations:
    85          1 - growing beyond the cached sequence length (allow scaling)
    86          2 - the current sequence length is in the original scale (avoid losing precision with small sequences)
    87          """
    88          seq_len = torch.max(position_ids) + 1
    89          if layer_type is None:
    90              rope_type = self.rope_type
    91              max_seq_len_cached = self.max_seq_len_cached
    92              original_inv_freq = self.original_inv_freq
    93              prefix = ""
    94          else:
    95              rope_type = self.rope_type[layer_type]
    96              max_seq_len_cached = getattr(self, f"{layer_type}_max_seq_len_cached", self.max_seq_len_cached)
    97              original_inv_freq = getattr(self, f"{layer_type}_original_inv_freq")
    98              prefix = f"{layer_type}_"
    99  
   100          if seq_len > max_seq_len_cached:  # growth
   101              rope_init_fn = ROPE_INIT_FUNCTIONS[rope_type]
   102              inv_freq, self.attention_scaling = rope_init_fn(
   103                  self.config,
   104                  device,
   105                  seq_len=seq_len,
   106                  layer_type=layer_type,
   107              )
   108              # TODO joao: may break with compilation
   109              self.register_buffer(f"{prefix}inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
   110              setattr(self, f"{prefix}max_seq_len_cached", seq_len)
   111  
   112          if seq_len < self.original_max_seq_len and max_seq_len_cached > self.original_max_seq_len:  # reset
   113              # This .to() is needed if the model has been moved to a device after being initialized (because
   114              # the buffer is automatically moved, but not the original copy)
   115              original_inv_freq = original_inv_freq.to(device)
   116              self.register_buffer(f"{prefix}inv_freq", original_inv_freq, persistent=False)
   117              setattr(self, f"{prefix}original_inv_freq", original_inv_freq)
   118              setattr(self, f"{prefix}max_seq_len_cached", self.original_max_seq_len)
   119  
   120      @wraps(rope_forward)
   121      def wrapper(self, x, position_ids, layer_type=None):
   122          rope_type = self.rope_type if layer_type is None else self.rope_type[layer_type]
   123          kwargs = {"layer_type": layer_type} if layer_type is not None else {}
   124          if "dynamic" in rope_type:
   125              dynamic_frequency_update(self, position_ids, device=x.device, **kwargs)
   126          elif rope_type == "longrope":
   127              longrope_frequency_update(self, position_ids, device=x.device, **kwargs)
   128          return rope_forward(self, x, position_ids, **kwargs)
   129  
   130      return wrapper
   131  
   132  
   133  def _compute_linear_scaling_rope_parameters(
   134      config: Optional["PreTrainedConfig"] = None,
   135      device: Optional["torch.device"] = None,
   136      seq_len: int | None = None,
   137      layer_type: str | None = None,
   138  ) -> tuple["torch.Tensor", float]:
   139      """
   140      Computes the inverse frequencies with linear scaling. Credits to the Reddit user /u/kaiokendev
   141      Args:
   142          config ([`~transformers."PreTrainedConfig"`]):
   143              The model configuration. This function assumes that the config will provide at least the following
   144              properties:
   145  
   146              *   rope_theta (`float`, *optional*): The base wavelength from which the inverse frequencies will be derived. Defaults to `config.default_theta` if omitted.
   147              *   hidden_size (`int`): The numerator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   148              *   num_attention_heads (`int`): The denominator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   149  
   150              Additionally, this function will make use of the following properties if they are found in the config:
   151  
   152              *   head_dim (`int`, *optional*): The size of the key-value heads in the model. If None, this value will be
   153                  derived as hidden_size // num_attention_heads.
   154              *   partial_rotary_factor (`float`, *optional*): If less than 1.0, inverse frequencies will be returned for
   155                  the first fraction of the head_dim. Defaults to 1.0.
   156          device (`torch.device`):
   157              The device to use for initialization of the inverse frequencies.
   158          seq_len (`int`, *optional*):
   159              The current sequence length. Unused for this type of RoPE.
   160  
   161      Returns:
   162          Tuple of (`torch.Tensor`, `float`), containing the inverse frequencies for the RoPE embeddings and the
   163          post-processing scaling factor applied to the computed cos/sin (unused in this type of RoPE).
   164      """
   165      # Sloppy workaround for per-layer-config in gemma4 family which raises error for other models (DS4)
   166      try:
   167          config = config.per_layer_config[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config
   168      except ValueError:
   169          pass
   170  
   171      # For backward compatibility standardize the `rope_parameters_dict` if it uses old format
   172      config.standardize_rope_params()
   173      rope_parameters_dict = config.rope_parameters[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config.rope_parameters
   174      factor = rope_parameters_dict["factor"]
   175  
   176      # Gets the default RoPE parameters
   177      base = rope_parameters_dict["rope_theta"]
   178      partial_rotary_factor = rope_parameters_dict.get("partial_rotary_factor", 1.0)
   179      head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", None) or config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads
   180      dim = int(head_dim * partial_rotary_factor)
   181      attention_factor = 1.0  # Unused in this type of RoPE
   182  
   183      # Compute the inverse frequencies
   184      inv_freq = 1.0 / (base ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=torch.int64).to(device=device, dtype=torch.float) / dim))
   185  
   186      # Then applies linear scaling to the frequencies.
   187      # NOTE: originally, scaling was applied to the position_ids. However, we get `embs = inv_freq @ position_ids`, so
   188      # applying scaling to the inverse frequencies is equivalent.
   189      inv_freq /= factor
   190      return inv_freq, attention_factor
   191  
   192  
   193  def _compute_proportional_rope_parameters(
   194      config: Optional["PreTrainedConfig"] = None,
   195      device: Optional["torch.device"] = None,
   196      seq_len: int | None = None,
   197      layer_type: str | None = None,
   198      head_dim_key: str = "head_dim",
   199  ) -> tuple["torch.Tensor", float]:
   200      """
   201      Computes the inverse frequencies with proportional RoPE.
   202  
   203      Args:
   204          config ([`~transformers.PretrainedConfig`]):
   205              The model configuration. This function assumes that the config will provide at least the following
   206              properties:
   207  
   208              *   rope_theta (`float`, *optional*): The base wavelength from which the inverse frequencies will be derived. Defaults to `config.default_theta` if omitted.
   209              *   hidden_size (`int`): The numerator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   210              *   num_attention_heads (`int`): The denominator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   211  
   212              Additionally, this function will make use of the following properties if they are found in the config:
   213  
   214              *   head_dim (`int`, *optional*): The size of the key-value heads in the model. If None, this value will be
   215                  derived as hidden_size // num_attention_heads.
   216              *   partial_rotary_factor (`float`, *optional*, defaults to 1.0): The proportion of the embedding dimension
   217                  to apply rotary positional encoding, e.g., [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0]. Unlike other RoPE functions
   218                  that use this parameter, proportional RoPE will always return an encoding that is the size of
   219                  `head_dim`.
   220          device (`torch.device`):
   221              The device to use for initialization of the inverse frequencies.
   222          seq_len (`int`, *optional*):
   223              The current sequence length. Unused for this type of RoPE.
   224  
   225      Returns:
   226          Tuple of (`torch.Tensor`, `float`), containing the inverse frequencies for the RoPE embeddings and the
   227          post-processing scaling factor applied to the computed cos/sin (unused in this type of RoPE).
   228      """
   229      # Sloppy workaround for per-layer-config in gemma4 family which raises error for other models (DS4)
   230      try:
   231          config = config.per_layer_config[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config
   232      except ValueError:
   233          pass
   234  
   235      # For backward compatibility standardize the `rope_parameters_dict` if it uses old format
   236      config.standardize_rope_params()
   237      rope_parameters_dict = config.rope_parameters[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config.rope_parameters
   238  
   239      head_dim = getattr(config, head_dim_key, None) or config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads
   240      base = rope_parameters_dict["rope_theta"]
   241      factor = rope_parameters_dict.get("factor", 1.0)
   242      rope_proportion = rope_parameters_dict.get("partial_rotary_factor", 1.0)
   243  
   244      attention_factor = 1.0  # Unused in this type of RoPE
   245  
   246      rope_angles = int(rope_proportion * head_dim // 2)
   247  
   248      inv_freq_rotated = 1.0 / (
   249          base
   250          ** (torch.arange(0, 2 * rope_angles, 2, dtype=torch.int64).to(device=device, dtype=torch.float) / head_dim)
   251      )
   252  
   253      nope_angles = head_dim // 2 - rope_angles
   254      if nope_angles > 0:
   255          inv_freq = torch.cat(
   256              (
   257                  inv_freq_rotated,
   258                  torch.zeros(nope_angles, dtype=torch.float32, device=device),
   259              ),
   260              dim=0,
   261          )
   262      else:
   263          inv_freq = inv_freq_rotated
   264  
   265      inv_freq /= factor
   266      return inv_freq, attention_factor
   267  
   268  
   269  def _compute_dynamic_ntk_parameters(
   270      config: Optional["PreTrainedConfig"] = None,
   271      device: Optional["torch.device"] = None,
   272      seq_len: int | None = None,
   273      layer_type: str | None = None,
   274  ) -> tuple["torch.Tensor", float]:
   275      """
   276      Computes the inverse frequencies with NTK scaling. Credits to the Reddit users /u/bloc97 and /u/emozilla
   277  
   278      Args:
   279          config ([`~transformers."PreTrainedConfig"`]):
   280              The model configuration. This function assumes that the config will provide at least the following
   281              properties:
   282  
   283              *   rope_theta (`float`, *optional*): The base wavelength from which the inverse frequencies will be derived. Defaults to `config.default_theta` if omitted.
   284              *   hidden_size (`int`): The numerator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   285              *   num_attention_heads (`int`): The denominator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   286              *   max_position_embeddings (`int`): The default sequence length used to update the dynamic RoPE at
   287                  inference time
   288              *   rope_parameters (`dict[str, float]`): The standard RoPE scaling parameters, from which `factor`
   289                  will be accessed. The value of `factor` is used to determine the new base frequency, along with the
   290                  current sequence length (seq_len), the maximum positional embeddings (max_position_embeddings), and the
   291                  computed dimensionality (dim) of the rotary embeddings. If seq_len <= max_position_embeddings, this
   292                  factor has no effect. If seq_len <= max_position_embeddings, this factor effectively stretches the
   293                  context window using an exponent derived from `dim`.
   294  
   295              Additionally, this function will make use of the following properties if they are found in the config:
   296  
   297              *   head_dim (`int`, *optional*): The size of the key-value heads in the model. If None, this value will be
   298                  derived as hidden_size // num_attention_heads.
   299              *   partial_rotary_factor (`float`, *optional*): If less than 1.0, inverse frequencies will be returned for
   300                  the first fraction of the head_dim. Defaults to 1.0.
   301          device (`torch.device`):
   302              The device to use for initialization of the inverse frequencies.
   303          seq_len (`int`, *optional*):
   304              The current sequence length, used to update the dynamic RoPE at inference time. If `None` or shorter than
   305              max_position_embeddings, this value will be overridden by max_position_embeddings.
   306  
   307      Returns:
   308          Tuple of (`torch.Tensor`, `float`), containing the inverse frequencies for the RoPE embeddings and the
   309          post-processing scaling factor applied to the computed cos/sin (unused in this type of RoPE).
   310      """
   311      # Sloppy workaround for per-layer-config in gemma4 family which raises error for other models (DS4)
   312      try:
   313          config = config.per_layer_config[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config
   314      except ValueError:
   315          pass
   316  
   317      # For backward compatibility standardize the `rope_parameters_dict` if it uses old format
   318      config.standardize_rope_params()
   319      rope_parameters_dict = config.rope_parameters[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config.rope_parameters
   320  
   321      base = rope_parameters_dict["rope_theta"]
   322      partial_rotary_factor = rope_parameters_dict.get("partial_rotary_factor", 1.0)
   323      head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads)
   324      dim = int(head_dim * partial_rotary_factor)
   325      factor = rope_parameters_dict["factor"]
   326      attention_factor = 1.0  # Unused in this type of RoPE
   327  
   328      # seq_len: default to max_position_embeddings, e.g. at init time
   329      if seq_len is None:
   330          seq_len = config.max_position_embeddings
   331      elif isinstance(seq_len, torch.Tensor):
   332          seq_len = torch.maximum(
   333              seq_len,
   334              torch.tensor(config.max_position_embeddings, dtype=seq_len.dtype, device=seq_len.device),
   335          )
   336      else:
   337          seq_len = max(seq_len, config.max_position_embeddings)
   338  
   339      # Compute the inverse frequencies
   340      base = base * ((factor * seq_len / config.max_position_embeddings) - (factor - 1)) ** (dim / (dim - 2))
   341      inv_freq = 1.0 / (base ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=torch.int64).to(device=device, dtype=torch.float) / dim))
   342      return inv_freq, attention_factor
   343  
   344  
   345  def _compute_yarn_parameters(
   346      config: "PreTrainedConfig",
   347      device: Optional["torch.device"] = None,
   348      seq_len: int | None = None,
   349      layer_type: str | None = None,
   350  ) -> tuple["torch.Tensor", float]:
   351      """
   352      Computes the inverse frequencies with NTK scaling. Please refer to the
   353      [original paper](https://huggingface.co/papers/2309.00071)
   354  
   355      Args:
   356          config ([`~transformers."PreTrainedConfig"`]):
   357              The model configuration. This function assumes that the config will provide at least the following
   358              properties:
   359  
   360              *   rope_theta (`float`, *optional*): The base wavelength from which the inverse frequencies will be derived. Defaults to `config.default_theta` if omitted.
   361              *   hidden_size (`int`): The numerator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   362              *   num_attention_heads (`int`): The denominator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   363              *   max_position_embeddings (`int`): The maximum length of the positional embeddings.
   364              *   rope_parameters (`dict[str, float | int]`): The standard RoPE scaling parameters, from which the following
   365                  keys will be accessed:
   366                  *   `attention_factor` (`float`, *optional*): The scaling factor to be applied to the computed cos/sin.
   367                      If None, the value is inferred from `factor`, `mscale`, and `mscale_all_dim` as available.
   368                  *   `beta_fast` (`float`, *optional*, defaults to 32): Parameter to set the boundary for extrapolation
   369                      (only) in the linear ramp function.
   370                  *   `beta_slow` (`float`, *optional*, defaults to 1): Parameter to set the boundary for interpolation
   371                      (only) in the linear ramp function.
   372                  *   `factor` (`float`, *optional*): The scaling factor applied when interpolating the position IDs to
   373                      extend the possible context length. Additionally, if `attention_factor` is None, the log of this
   374                      value is used to compute a value for `attention_factor`, possibly in conjunction with `mscale` and
   375                      `mscale_all_dim`, if provided.
   376                  *   `mscale` (`float`, *optional*): If `attention_factor` is None and both `mscale` and
   377                      `mscale_all_dim` are provided, `mscale` acts scalar augmenting `log(factor)` when computing the
   378                      numerator for the inferred value of `attention_factor`. If not provided, `attention_factor` will be
   379                      calculated based on `factor` only.
   380                  *   `mscale_all_dim` (`float`, *optional*): If `attention_factor` is None and both `mscale` and
   381                      `mscale_all_dim` are provided, `mscale_all_dim` acts scalar augmenting `log(factor)` when computing
   382                      the denominator for the inferred value of `attention_factor`. If not provided, `attention_factor`
   383                      will be calculated based on `factor` only.
   384                  *   `original_max_position_embeddings` (`int`): The original max position embeddings used during pretraining.
   385                  *   `truncate` (`bool`, *optional*): Whether to truncate the correction range.
   386  
   387              Additionally, this function will make use of the following properties if they are found in the config:
   388  
   389              *   head_dim (`int`, *optional*): The size of the key-value heads in the model. If None, this value will be
   390                  derived as hidden_size // num_attention_heads.
   391              *   partial_rotary_factor (`float`, *optional*, defaults to 1.0): If less than 1.0, inverse frequencies
   392                  will be returned for the first fraction of the head_dim.
   393          device (`torch.device`):
   394              The device to use for initialization of the inverse frequencies.
   395          seq_len (`int`, *optional*):
   396              The current sequence length. Unused for this type of RoPE.
   397  
   398      Returns:
   399          Tuple of (`torch.Tensor`, `float`), containing the inverse frequencies for the RoPE embeddings and the
   400          post-processing scaling factor applied to the computed cos/sin.
   401      """
   402      # Sloppy workaround for per-layer-config in gemma4 family which raises error for other models (DS4)
   403      try:
   404          config = config.per_layer_config[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config
   405      except ValueError:
   406          pass
   407  
   408      # For backward compatibility standardize the `rope_parameters_dict` if it uses old format
   409      config.standardize_rope_params()
   410      rope_parameters_dict = config.rope_parameters[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config.rope_parameters
   411  
   412      base = rope_parameters_dict["rope_theta"]
   413      partial_rotary_factor = rope_parameters_dict.get("partial_rotary_factor", 1.0)
   414      head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads)
   415      dim = int(head_dim * partial_rotary_factor)
   416  
   417      factor = rope_parameters_dict["factor"]
   418      attention_factor = rope_parameters_dict.get("attention_factor")
   419      mscale = rope_parameters_dict.get("mscale")
   420      mscale_all_dim = rope_parameters_dict.get("mscale_all_dim")
   421      original_max_position_embeddings = rope_parameters_dict["original_max_position_embeddings"]
   422  
   423      # NOTE: DeepSeek-V3 (and potentially other models) have `original_max_position_embeddings` field
   424      # containing the pretrained value. They use the ratio between `max_position_embeddings` and this value
   425      # to compute the default attention scaling factor, instead of using `factor`.
   426      if factor is None:
   427          factor = config.max_position_embeddings / original_max_position_embeddings
   428  
   429      def get_mscale(scale, mscale=1):
   430          if scale <= 1:
   431              return 1.0
   432          return 0.1 * mscale * math.log(scale) + 1.0
   433  
   434      # Sets the attention factor as suggested in the paper
   435      if attention_factor is None:
   436          if mscale and mscale_all_dim:
   437              attention_factor = float(get_mscale(factor, mscale) / get_mscale(factor, mscale_all_dim))
   438          else:
   439              attention_factor = get_mscale(factor)
   440  
   441      # Optional config options
   442      # beta_fast/beta_slow: as suggested in the paper, default to 32/1 (correspondingly)
   443      beta_fast = rope_parameters_dict.get("beta_fast") or 32
   444      beta_slow = rope_parameters_dict.get("beta_slow") or 1
   445  
   446      # Compute the inverse frequencies
   447      def find_correction_dim(num_rotations, dim, base, max_position_embeddings):
   448          """Inverse dimension formula to find the dimension based on the number of rotations"""
   449          return (dim * math.log(max_position_embeddings / (num_rotations * 2 * math.pi))) / (2 * math.log(base))
   450  
   451      def find_correction_range(low_rot, high_rot, dim, base, max_position_embeddings, truncate):
   452          """Find dimension range bounds based on rotations"""
   453          low = find_correction_dim(low_rot, dim, base, max_position_embeddings)
   454          high = find_correction_dim(high_rot, dim, base, max_position_embeddings)
   455          if truncate:
   456              low = math.floor(low)
   457              high = math.ceil(high)
   458          return max(low, 0), min(high, dim - 1)
   459  
   460      def linear_ramp_factor(min, max, dim):
   461          if min == max:
   462              max += 0.001  # Prevent singularity
   463  
   464          linear_func = (torch.arange(dim, dtype=torch.float32) - min) / (max - min)
   465          ramp_func = torch.clamp(linear_func, 0, 1)
   466          return ramp_func
   467  
   468      # Note on variable naming: "interpolation" comes from the original technique, where we interpolate the position IDs
   469      # to expand the possible context length. In other words, interpolation = apply scaling factor.
   470      pos_freqs = base ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2).to(device=device, dtype=torch.float) / dim)
   471      inv_freq_extrapolation = 1.0 / pos_freqs
   472      inv_freq_interpolation = 1.0 / (factor * pos_freqs)
   473  
   474      truncate = config.rope_parameters.get("truncate", True)
   475      low, high = find_correction_range(beta_fast, beta_slow, dim, base, original_max_position_embeddings, truncate)
   476  
   477      # Get n-dimensional rotational scaling corrected for extrapolation
   478      inv_freq_extrapolation_factor = 1 - linear_ramp_factor(low, high, dim // 2).to(device=device, dtype=torch.float)
   479      inv_freq = (
   480          inv_freq_interpolation * (1 - inv_freq_extrapolation_factor)
   481          + inv_freq_extrapolation * inv_freq_extrapolation_factor
   482      )
   483      return inv_freq, attention_factor
   484  
   485  
   486  def _compute_longrope_parameters(
   487      config: "PreTrainedConfig",
   488      device: Optional["torch.device"] = None,
   489      seq_len: int | None = None,
   490      layer_type: str | None = None,
   491  ) -> tuple["torch.Tensor", float]:
   492      """
   493      Computes the inverse frequencies with LongRoPE scaling. Please refer to the
   494      [original implementation](https://github.com/microsoft/LongRoPE)
   495  
   496      Args:
   497          config ([`~transformers."PreTrainedConfig"`]):
   498              The model configuration. This function assumes that the config will provide at least the following
   499              properties:
   500  
   501              *   rope_theta (`float`, *optional*): The base wavelength from which the inverse frequencies will be derived. Defaults to `config.default_theta` if omitted.
   502              *   hidden_size (`int`): The numerator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   503              *   num_attention_heads (`int`): The denominator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   504              *   max_position_embeddings (`int`): The maximum length of the positional embeddings.
   505              *   original_max_position_embeddings (`int`, *optional*): The original max position embeddings used during
   506                  pretraining. If not provided, defaults to `max_position_embeddings`.
   507              *   rope_parameters (`dict[str, float]`): The standard RoPE scaling parameters, from which the following keys
   508                  will be accessed:
   509                  *   `attention_factor` (`float`, *optional*): The scaling factor to be applied on the attention
   510                      computation. If unspecified, it defaults to value recommended by the implementation, inferred from
   511                      the value of `factor`.
   512                  *   `factor` (`float`, *optional*): The scaling factor to apply to the RoPE embeddings. If both
   513                      `max_position_embeddings` and `original_max_position_embeddings` are provided, this value will be
   514                      overridden s the ratio between those values.
   515                  *   `long_factor` (`float`, *optional*): The scale factor applied when computing the inverse
   516                      frequencies if `seq_len` is provided and greater than `original_max_position_embeddings`.
   517                  *   `short_factor` (`float`, *optional*): The scale factor applied when computing the inverse
   518                      frequencies if `seq_len` is None or less-than-or-equal-to `original_max_position_embeddings`.
   519  
   520              Additionally, this function will make use of the following properties if they are found in the config:
   521  
   522              *   head_dim (`int`, *optional*): The size of the key-value heads in the model. If None, this value will be
   523                  derived as hidden_size // num_attention_heads.
   524              *   partial_rotary_factor (`float`, *optional*, defaults to 1.0): If less than 1.0, inverse frequencies
   525                  will be returned for the first fraction of the head_dim.
   526          device (`torch.device`):
   527              The device to use for initialization of the inverse frequencies.
   528          seq_len (`int`, *optional*):
   529              The current sequence length.
   530  
   531      Returns:
   532          Tuple of (`torch.Tensor`, `float`), containing the inverse frequencies for the RoPE embeddings and the
   533          post-processing scaling factor applied to the computed cos/sin.
   534      """
   535      # Sloppy workaround for per-layer-config in gemma4 family which raises error for other models (DS4)
   536      try:
   537          config = config.per_layer_config[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config
   538      except ValueError:
   539          pass
   540  
   541      # For backward compatibility standardize the `rope_parameters_dict` if it uses old format
   542      config.standardize_rope_params()
   543      rope_parameters_dict = config.rope_parameters[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config.rope_parameters
   544  
   545      base = rope_parameters_dict["rope_theta"]
   546      partial_rotary_factor = rope_parameters_dict.get("partial_rotary_factor", 1.0)
   547      head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads)
   548      dim = int(head_dim * partial_rotary_factor)
   549  
   550      long_factor = rope_parameters_dict["long_factor"]
   551      short_factor = rope_parameters_dict["short_factor"]
   552      factor = rope_parameters_dict.get("factor")
   553      attention_factor = rope_parameters_dict.get("attention_factor")
   554      original_max_position_embeddings = rope_parameters_dict["original_max_position_embeddings"]
   555  
   556      # NOTE: Phi3 (and potentially other models) modify `max_position_embeddings` and have a
   557      # `original_max_position_embeddings` field containing the pretrained value. They use the ratio between these two
   558      # values to compute the default attention scaling factor, instead of using `factor`.
   559      if factor is None:
   560          factor = config.max_position_embeddings / original_max_position_embeddings
   561  
   562      # Sets the attention factor as suggested in the paper
   563      if attention_factor is None:
   564          if factor <= 1.0:
   565              attention_factor = 1.0
   566          else:
   567              attention_factor = math.sqrt(1 + math.log(factor) / math.log(original_max_position_embeddings))
   568  
   569      # Compute the inverse frequencies -- scaled based on the target sequence length
   570      if seq_len and seq_len > original_max_position_embeddings:
   571          ext_factors = torch.tensor(long_factor, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
   572      else:
   573          ext_factors = torch.tensor(short_factor, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
   574      inv_freq_shape = torch.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=torch.int64, device=device).float() / dim
   575      inv_freq = 1.0 / (ext_factors * base**inv_freq_shape)
   576  
   577      return inv_freq, attention_factor
   578  
   579  
   580  def _compute_llama3_parameters(
   581      config: "PreTrainedConfig",
   582      device: Optional["torch.device"] = None,
   583      seq_len: int | None = None,
   584      layer_type: str | None = None,
   585  ) -> tuple["torch.Tensor", float]:
   586      """
   587      Computes the inverse frequencies for llama 3.1.
   588  
   589      Args:
   590          config ([`~transformers."PreTrainedConfig"`]):
   591              The model configuration. This function assumes that the config will provide at least the following
   592              properties:
   593  
   594              *   rope_theta (`float`, *optional*): The base wavelength from which the inverse frequencies will be derived. Defaults to `config.default_theta` if omitted.
   595              *   hidden_size (`int`): The numerator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   596              *   num_attention_heads (`int`): The denominator when deriving a head_dim, if not provided directly.
   597              *   rope_parameters (`dict[str, float | int]`): The standard RoPE scaling parameters, from which the following
   598                  keys will be accessed:
   599                  *   `factor` (`float`, *optional*): The scaling factor applied to the inverse frequencies when 1) the
   600                      wavelength is greater than `low_freq_wavelen` prior to smoothing, and 2) to all inverse frequencies
   601                      during smoothing.
   602                  *   `high_freq_factor` (`float`): The scale factor used to compute `high_freq_wavelen` and
   603                      the value for the denominator of the smoothing factor prior to the `low_freq_factor` shift.
   604                  *   `low_freq_factor` (`float`): The scale factor used to compute `low_freq_wavelen` and
   605                      the shift applied to the numerator and denominator of the smoothing factor.
   606                      frequencies if `seq_len` is None or less-than-or-equal-to `original_max_position_embeddings`.
   607                  *   `original_max_position_embeddings` (`int`): The original max position embeddings used
   608                      during pretraining. If not provided, the function falls back to `max_position_embeddings`.
   609  
   610              Additionally, this function will make use of the following properties if they are found in the config:
   611  
   612              *   head_dim (`int`, *optional*): The size of the key-value heads in the model. If None, this value will be
   613                  derived as hidden_size // num_attention_heads.
   614              *   partial_rotary_factor (`float`, *optional*): If less than 1.0, inverse frequencies will be returned for
   615                  the first fraction of the head_dim. Defaults to 1.0.
   616          device (`torch.device`):
   617              The device to use for initialization of the inverse frequencies.
   618          seq_len (`int`, *optional*):
   619              The current sequence length. Unused for this type of RoPE.
   620      Returns:
   621          Tuple of (`torch.Tensor`, `float`), containing the inverse frequencies for the RoPE embeddings and the
   622          post-processing scaling factor applied to the computed cos/sin.
   623      """
   624      # Sloppy workaround for per-layer-config in gemma4 family which raises error for other models (DS4)
   625      try:
   626          config = config.per_layer_config[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config
   627      except ValueError:
   628          pass
   629  
   630      # For backward compatibility standardize the `rope_parameters_dict` if it uses old format
   631      config.standardize_rope_params()
   632      rope_parameters_dict = config.rope_parameters[layer_type] if layer_type is not None else config.rope_parameters
   633  
   634      # Gets the default RoPE parameters
   635      base = rope_parameters_dict["rope_theta"]
   636      partial_rotary_factor = rope_parameters_dict.get("partial_rotary_factor", 1.0)
   637      head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", None) or config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads
   638      dim = int(head_dim * partial_rotary_factor)
   639      attention_factor = 1.0  # Unused in this type of RoPE
   640  
   641      # Compute the inverse frequencies
   642      inv_freq = 1.0 / (base ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=torch.int64).to(device=device, dtype=torch.float) / dim))
   643  
   644      factor = rope_parameters_dict["factor"]  # `8` in the original implementation
   645      low_freq_factor = rope_parameters_dict["low_freq_factor"]  # `1` in the original implementation
   646      high_freq_factor = rope_parameters_dict["high_freq_factor"]  # `4` in the original implementation
   647      old_context_len = rope_parameters_dict["original_max_position_embeddings"]  # `8192` in the original implementation
   648  
   649      low_freq_wavelen = old_context_len / low_freq_factor
   650      high_freq_wavelen = old_context_len / high_freq_factor
   651  
   652      wavelen = 2 * math.pi / inv_freq
   653      # wavelen < high_freq_wavelen: do nothing
   654      # wavelen > low_freq_wavelen: divide by factor
   655      inv_freq_llama = torch.where(wavelen > low_freq_wavelen, inv_freq / factor, inv_freq)
   656      # otherwise: interpolate between the two, using a smooth factor
   657      smooth_factor = (old_context_len / wavelen - low_freq_factor) / (high_freq_factor - low_freq_factor)
   658      smoothed_inv_freq = (1 - smooth_factor) * inv_freq_llama / factor + smooth_factor * inv_freq_llama
   659      is_medium_freq = ~(wavelen < high_freq_wavelen) * ~(wavelen > low_freq_wavelen)
   660      inv_freq_llama = torch.where(is_medium_freq, smoothed_inv_freq, inv_freq_llama)
   661  
   662      return inv_freq_llama, attention_factor
   663  
   664  
   665  # This maps the "rope_type" string field in rope config to the corresponding function to compute the RoPE parameters
   666  # from the model config. You can append new {'rope_type': callable} pairs to this rope_parameters to enable custom RoPE
   667  # parameterizations, as long as the callable has the same signature.
   668  ROPE_INIT_FUNCTIONS: dict[str, Callable[..., tuple["torch.Tensor", float]]] = {
   669      "linear": _compute_linear_scaling_rope_parameters,
   670      "dynamic": _compute_dynamic_ntk_parameters,
   671      "yarn": _compute_yarn_parameters,
   672      "longrope": _compute_longrope_parameters,
   673      "llama3": _compute_llama3_parameters,
   674      "proportional": _compute_proportional_rope_parameters,
   675  }
   676  
   677  
   678  class RopeParameters(TypedDict):
   679      """
   680      Args:
   681          rope_theta (`float`, *optional*, defaults to `RotaryEmbeddingConfigMixin.default_theta`):
   682              The base period of the RoPE embeddings. Optional in serialized configs — if omitted,
   683              the model's `default_theta` (typically 10000.0) is used.
   684          rope_type (`str`, *optional*, defaults to "default"):
   685              The sub-variant of RoPE to use. Can be one of ['default', 'linear', 'dynamic', 'yarn', 'longrope',
   686              'llama3'], with 'default' being the original RoPE implementation.
   687          partial_rotary_factor (`float`, *optional*):
   688              The percentage of the query and key head embedding on which RoPE will be applied.
   689          factor (`float`, *optional*):
   690              Used with all rope types except 'default'. The scaling factor to apply to the RoPE embeddings. In
   691              most scaling types, a `factor` of x will enable the model to handle sequences of length x *
   692              original maximum pre-trained length.
   693          original_max_position_embeddings (`int`, *optional*):
   694              Used with 'yarn', 'longrope' and 'llama3'. The original max position embeddings used during
   695              pretraining.
   696          attention_factor (`float`, *optional*):
   697              Used with 'yarn' and 'longrope'. The scaling factor to be applied on the attention
   698              computation. If unspecified, it defaults to value recommended by the implementation, using the
   699              `factor` field to infer the suggested value.
   700          beta_fast (`float`, *optional*):
   701              Only used with 'yarn'. Parameter to set the boundary for extrapolation (only) in the linear
   702              ramp function. If unspecified, it defaults to 32.
   703          beta_slow (`float`, *optional*):
   704              Only used with 'yarn'. Parameter to set the boundary for interpolation (only) in the linear
   705              ramp function. If unspecified, it defaults to 1.
   706          short_factor (`list[float]`, *optional*):
   707              Only used with 'longrope'. The scaling factor to be applied to short contexts (<
   708              `original_max_position_embeddings`). Must be a list of numbers with the same length as the hidden
   709              size divided by the number of attention heads divided by 2
   710          long_factor (`list[float]`, *optional*):
   711              Only used with 'longrope'. The scaling factor to be applied to long contexts (<
   712              `original_max_position_embeddings`). Must be a list of numbers with the same length as the hidden
   713              size divided by the number of attention heads divided by 2
   714          low_freq_factor (`float`, *optional*):
   715              Only used with 'llama3'. Scaling factor applied to low frequency components of the RoPE
   716          high_freq_factor (`float`, *optional*):
   717              Only used with 'llama3'. Scaling factor applied to high frequency components of the RoPE
   718      """
   719  
   720      rope_theta: float | None
   721      rope_type: str | None
   722      partial_rotary_factor: float | None
   723      factor: float | None
   724      original_max_position_embeddings: int | None
   725      attention_factor: float | None
   726      beta_fast: float | None
   727      beta_slow: float | None
   728      short_factor: list[float] | None
   729      long_factor: list[float] | None
   730      low_freq_factor: float | None
   731      high_freq_factor: float | None
   732  
   733  
   734  class RotaryEmbeddingConfigMixin:
   735      """
   736      A Mixin containing the functionality to standardize and validate RoPE parameters.
   737      """
   738  
   739      default_theta = 10_000.0
   740      ignore_keys_at_rope_validation = set()
   741  
   742      def convert_rope_params_to_dict(self, **kwargs):
   743          rope_scaling = kwargs.pop("rope_scaling", None)
   744          self.rope_parameters = rope_scaling or self.rope_parameters
   745          self.rope_parameters = self.rope_parameters if self.rope_parameters is not None else {}
   746  
   747          # Standardize and validate the correctness of rotary position embeddings parameters. Priority for these parameters is:
   748          # 1. Values in `rope_parameters` dict (where they should be after standardization)
   749          # 2. Values in `kwargs` (i.e. it's in config.json but not MyConfig.__init__'s args)
   750          # 3. Values in the config's attributes (i.e. it's in MyConfig.__init__'s args)
   751          # 4. Default values (i.e. not present at all but other RoPE parameters are present)
   752          rope_theta = kwargs.pop("rope_theta", getattr(self, "rope_theta", self.default_theta))
   753          self.rope_parameters.setdefault("rope_theta", rope_theta)
   754  
   755          partial_rotary_factor = kwargs.get("partial_rotary_factor", getattr(self, "partial_rotary_factor", None))
   756          if partial_rotary_factor is not None:
   757              self.rope_parameters.setdefault("partial_rotary_factor", partial_rotary_factor)
   758              self.ignore_keys_at_rope_validation = set(self.ignore_keys_at_rope_validation or []) | {
   759                  "partial_rotary_factor"
   760              }
   761  
   762          self.standardize_rope_params()
   763          return kwargs
   764  
   765      def standardize_rope_params(self):
   766          """
   767          Helper to standardize the config's rope params field by ensuring the params are defined for each
   768          later type. For old model the fn will duplicate a single rope param in each layer type (backward compatibility)
   769          """
   770          # Move `rope_theta` and `partial_rotary_factor` to the `rope_parameters`, if not there yet
   771          rope_theta = getattr(self, "rope_theta", None)
   772          partial_rotary_factor = getattr(self, "partial_rotary_factor", None)
   773          rope_parameters = getattr(self, "rope_parameters", None) or {}
   774  
   775          # Deepseekv4 has `layer_types` which are different from `_rope_type_labels`
   776          layer_types = getattr(self, "_rope_type_labels", getattr(self, "layer_types", None))
   777  
   778          # Case 0: no RoPE params defined
   779          if not (rope_parameters or rope_theta):
   780              # partial_rotary_factor without rope_theta is invalid, so we don't check for it here
   781              logger.warning("`standardize_rope_params` was called but no RoPE parameters were found.")
   782              return
   783          # Case 1: RoPE param keys do not intersect with possible `layer_types` -> one global dict
   784          elif layer_types is None or rope_parameters == {} or not set(rope_parameters.keys()).issubset(layer_types):
   785              rope_parameters.setdefault("rope_type", rope_parameters.get("type", "default"))
   786              rope_parameters.setdefault("rope_theta", rope_theta)
   787              if partial_rotary_factor is not None:
   788                  rope_parameters["partial_rotary_factor"] = partial_rotary_factor
   789  
   790              # Move pretraining-time maximum length to rope parameter dict for RoPE types with scaling
   791              if rope_parameters["rope_type"] in ["llama3", "yarn", "longrope"]:
   792                  if hasattr(self, "original_max_position_embeddings"):
   793                      # NOTE: Phi3 (and potentially other models) save `original_max_position_embeddings` field
   794                      # containing the pretrained value outside rope parameters. This is an exception case where we
   795                      # give priority to `self.original_max_position_embeddings
   796                      self.rope_parameters["original_max_position_embeddings"] = self.original_max_position_embeddings
   797                  else:
   798                      self.rope_parameters.setdefault("original_max_position_embeddings", self.max_position_embeddings)
   799  
   800          # Case 2: different RoPE for each layer -> several params as nested dict
   801          else:
   802              for layer_type in set(layer_types):
   803                  rope_parameters[layer_type].setdefault("rope_type", rope_parameters[layer_type].get("type", "default"))
   804                  rope_parameters[layer_type].setdefault("rope_theta", rope_theta)
   805                  if partial_rotary_factor is not None:
   806                      rope_parameters[layer_type]["partial_rotary_factor"] = partial_rotary_factor
   807  
   808                  if rope_parameters[layer_type]["rope_type"] in ["llama3", "yarn", "longrope"]:
   809                      self.rope_parameters[layer_type].setdefault(
   810                          "original_max_position_embeddings", self.max_position_embeddings
   811                      )
   812  
   813          self.rope_parameters = rope_parameters
   814  
   815      def validate_rope(self: "PreTrainedConfig"):
   816          """
   817          Validate the RoPE config arguments, given a `"PreTrainedConfig"` object
   818          """
   819          # Don't validate if no rope_parameters found (`None`) or if it's an empty dict
   820          # Note that validation runs every time a new config is created, even if config is non-RoPE
   821          rope_parameters_dict = getattr(self, "rope_parameters", None)
   822          if not rope_parameters_dict:
   823              return
   824  
   825          if getattr(self, "layer_types", None) is not None and set(rope_parameters_dict.keys()).issubset(
   826              self.layer_types
   827          ):
   828              pass
   829          else:
   830              rope_parameters_dict = {"full_attention": rope_parameters_dict}
   831  
   832          for rope_parameters in rope_parameters_dict.values():
   833              rope_type = rope_parameters.get("rope_type", rope_parameters.get("type", "default"))
   834              validation_fn = getattr(self, f"_validate_{rope_type}_rope_parameters", None)
   835              rope_parameters["rope_type"] = rope_type
   836  
   837              if validation_fn is not None:
   838                  validation_fn(rope_parameters, ignore_keys=self.ignore_keys_at_rope_validation)
   839              else:
   840                  logger.warning(
   841                      f"Missing validation function in 'RotaryEmbeddingConfigMixin' for 'rope_type'='{rope_type}'"
   842                  )
   843  
   844      def _validate_default_rope_parameters(self, rope_parameters: dict, ignore_keys: set | None = None):
   845          required_keys = {"rope_type"}
   846          optional_keys = {"rope_theta"}
   847          received_keys = set(rope_parameters.keys())
   848          rope_type = rope_parameters["rope_type"]
   849          self._check_received_keys(
   850              rope_type, received_keys, required_keys, optional_keys=optional_keys, ignore_keys=ignore_keys
   851          )
   852  
   853      def _validate_linear_rope_parameters(self, rope_parameters: dict, ignore_keys: set | None = None):
   854          required_keys = {"rope_type", "factor"}
   855          optional_keys = {"rope_theta"}
   856          received_keys = set(rope_parameters.keys())
   857          rope_type = rope_parameters["rope_type"]
   858          self._check_received_keys(
   859              rope_type, received_keys, required_keys, optional_keys=optional_keys, ignore_keys=ignore_keys
   860          )
   861  
   862          factor = rope_parameters["factor"]
   863          if factor is None or not isinstance(factor, (float, int)) or factor < 1.0:
   864              logger.warning(f"`rope_parameters`'s factor field must be a float or int >= 1, got {factor}")
   865  
   866      def _validate_dynamic_rope_parameters(self, rope_parameters: dict, ignore_keys: set | None = None):
   867          required_keys = {"rope_type", "factor"}
   868          optional_keys = {"rope_theta"}
   869          received_keys = set(rope_parameters.keys())
   870          rope_type = rope_parameters["rope_type"]
   871          self._check_received_keys(
   872              rope_type, received_keys, required_keys, optional_keys=optional_keys, ignore_keys=ignore_keys
   873          )
   874  
   875          factor = rope_parameters["factor"]
   876          if factor is None or not isinstance(factor, (float, int)) or factor < 1.0:
   877              logger.warning(f"`rope_parameters`'s factor field must be a float or int >= 1, got {factor}")
   878  
   879      def _validate_yarn_rope_parameters(self, rope_parameters: dict, ignore_keys: set | None = None):
   880          required_keys = {"rope_type", "factor", "original_max_position_embeddings"}
   881          optional_keys = {
   882              "rope_theta",
   883              "attention_factor",
   884              "beta_fast",
   885              "beta_slow",
   886              "mscale",
   887              "mscale_all_dim",
   888              "truncate",
   889          }
   890          received_keys = set(rope_parameters.keys())
   891          rope_type = rope_parameters["rope_type"]
   892          self._check_received_keys(rope_type, received_keys, required_keys, optional_keys, ignore_keys=ignore_keys)
   893  
   894          factor = rope_parameters["factor"]
   895          if factor is None or not isinstance(factor, (float, int)) or factor < 1.0:
   896              logger.warning(f"`rope_parameters`'s factor field must be a float or int >= 1, got {factor}")
   897  
   898          attention_factor = rope_parameters.get("attention_factor")
   899          if attention_factor is not None and (not isinstance(attention_factor, float) or attention_factor < 0):
   900              logger.warning(
   901                  f"`rope_parameters`'s attention_factor field must be a float greater than 0, got {attention_factor}"
   902              )
   903          beta_fast = rope_parameters.get("beta_fast")
   904          if beta_fast is not None and not isinstance(beta_fast, (float, int)):
   905              logger.warning(f"`rope_parameters`'s beta_fast field must be a float or int, got {beta_fast}")
   906          beta_slow = rope_parameters.get("beta_slow")
   907          if beta_slow is not None and not isinstance(beta_slow, (float, int)):
   908              logger.warning(f"`rope_parameters`'s beta_slow field must be a float or int, got {beta_slow}")
   909  
   910          if (beta_fast or 32) < (beta_slow or 1):
   911              logger.warning(
   912                  f"`rope_parameters`'s beta_fast field must be greater than beta_slow, got beta_fast={beta_fast} "
   913                  f"(defaults to 32 if None) and beta_slow={beta_slow} (defaults to 1 if None)"
   914              )
   915  
   916          # Double-check: `factor` should be the ratio between the pre-yarn and post-yarn context lengths.
   917          # NOTE: we might get `implicit_factor == 1` if config's `original_max_position_embeddings` was
   918          # inferred from `max_position_embeddings` during standardization
   919          original_max_position_embeddings = rope_parameters["original_max_position_embeddings"]
   920          implicit_factor = self.max_position_embeddings / original_max_position_embeddings
   921          if implicit_factor != factor and implicit_factor != 1:
   922              logger.warning_once(
   923                  f"The explicitly set RoPE scaling factor (config.rope_parameters['factor'] = {factor}) does not match "
   924                  "the ratio implicitly set by other parameters (implicit factor = "
   925                  "post-yarn context length / pre-yarn context length = "
   926                  "config.max_position_embeddings / config.rope_parameters['original_max_position_embeddings'] = "
   927                  f"{implicit_factor}). Using the explicit factor ({factor}) in YaRN. This may cause unexpected "
   928                  "behaviour in model usage, please correct the 'original_max_position_embeddings' fields in the model config."
   929              )
   930  
   931      def _validate_longrope_rope_parameters(self, rope_parameters: dict, ignore_keys: set | None = None):
   932          required_keys = {"rope_type", "short_factor", "long_factor", "original_max_position_embeddings"}
   933          optional_keys = {"rope_theta", "attention_factor", "factor"}
   934          received_keys = set(rope_parameters.keys())
   935          rope_type = rope_parameters["rope_type"]
   936          self._check_received_keys(rope_type, received_keys, required_keys, optional_keys, ignore_keys=ignore_keys)
   937  
   938          partial_rotary_factor = rope_parameters.get("partial_rotary_factor", 1.0)
   939          head_dim = getattr(self, "head_dim", self.hidden_size // self.num_attention_heads)
   940          dim = int(head_dim * partial_rotary_factor)
   941  
   942          short_factor = rope_parameters.get("short_factor")
   943          if not (isinstance(short_factor, list) and all(isinstance(x, (int, float)) for x in short_factor)):
   944              logger.warning(f"`rope_parameters`'s short_factor field must be a list of numbers, got {short_factor}")
   945          if len(short_factor) != dim // 2:
   946              logger.warning(
   947                  f"`rope_parameters`'s short_factor field must have length {dim // 2}, got {len(short_factor)}"
   948              )
   949  
   950          long_factor = rope_parameters.get("long_factor")
   951          if not (isinstance(long_factor, list) and all(isinstance(x, (int, float)) for x in long_factor)):
   952              logger.warning(f"`rope_parameters`'s long_factor field must be a list of numbers, got {long_factor}")
   953          if len(long_factor) != dim // 2:
   954              logger.warning(
   955                  f"`rope_parameters`'s long_factor field must have length {dim // 2}, got {len(long_factor)}"
   956              )
   957  
   958          factor = rope_parameters.get("factor")
   959          original_max_position_embeddings = rope_parameters["original_max_position_embeddings"]
   960  
   961          # Handle Phi3 divergence: we prefer the use of `attention_factor` and/or `factor` over
   962          # `original_max_position_embeddings` to compute internal variables. The latter is undesirable
   963          if factor is None and original_max_position_embeddings is not None:
   964              logger.warning_once(
   965                  "This model config has set a `rope_parameters['original_max_position_embeddings']` field, to be used together with "
   966                  "`max_position_embeddings` to determine a scaling factor. Please set the `factor` field of `rope_parameters`"
   967                  "with this ratio instead -- we recommend the use of this field over `original_max_position_embeddings`, "
   968                  "as it is compatible with most model architectures."
   969              )
   970          elif factor is None and original_max_position_embeddings is None:
   971              logger.warning("Missing required keys in `rope_parameters`: 'factor'")
   972          elif not isinstance(factor, (float, int)) or factor < 1.0:
   973              logger.warning(f"`rope_parameters`'s factor field must be a float or int >= 1, got {factor}")
   974  
   975          attention_factor = rope_parameters.get("attention_factor")
   976          if attention_factor is not None and (not isinstance(attention_factor, (float, int)) or attention_factor < 0.0):
   977              logger.warning(
   978                  f"`rope_parameters`'s attention_factor field must be a float or int greater than 0, got {attention_factor}"
   979              )
   980  
   981      def _validate_llama3_rope_parameters(self, rope_parameters: dict, ignore_keys: set | None = None):
   982          required_keys = {
   983              "rope_type",
   984              "factor",
   985              "original_max_position_embeddings",
   986              "low_freq_factor",
   987              "high_freq_factor",
   988              "rope_theta",
   989          }
   990          rope_type = rope_parameters["rope_type"]
   991          received_keys = set(rope_parameters.keys())
   992          self._check_received_keys(rope_type, received_keys, required_keys, ignore_keys=ignore_keys)
   993  
   994          factor = rope_parameters["factor"]
   995          if factor is None or not isinstance(factor, (float, int)) or factor < 1.0:
   996              logger.warning(f"`rope_parameters`'s factor field must be a float or int >= 1, got {factor}")
   997  
   998          low_freq_factor = rope_parameters["low_freq_factor"]
   999          high_freq_factor = rope_parameters["high_freq_factor"]
  1000          if low_freq_factor is None or not isinstance(low_freq_factor, (float, int)):
  1001              logger.warning(f"`rope_parameters`'s low_freq_factor field must be a float, or int got {low_freq_factor}")
  1002          if high_freq_factor is None or not isinstance(high_freq_factor, (float, int)):
  1003              logger.warning(
  1004                  f"`rope_parameters`'s high_freq_factor field must be a float or int, got {high_freq_factor}"
  1005              )
  1006          if high_freq_factor <= low_freq_factor:
  1007              logger.warning(
  1008                  "`rope_parameters`'s high_freq_factor field must be greater than low_freq_factor, got high_freq_factor="
  1009                  f"{high_freq_factor} and low_freq_factor={low_freq_factor}"
  1010              )
  1011  
  1012          original_max_position_embeddings = rope_parameters["original_max_position_embeddings"]
  1013          if original_max_position_embeddings is None or not isinstance(original_max_position_embeddings, int):
  1014              logger.warning(
  1015                  "`rope_parameters`'s original_max_position_embeddings field must be an integer, got "
  1016                  f"{original_max_position_embeddings}"
  1017              )
  1018          if original_max_position_embeddings >= self.max_position_embeddings:
  1019              logger.warning(
  1020                  "`rope_parameters`'s original_max_position_embeddings field must be less than max_position_embeddings, got "
  1021                  f"{original_max_position_embeddings} and max_position_embeddings={self.max_position_embeddings}"
  1022              )
  1023  
  1024      def _validate_proportional_rope_parameters(self, rope_parameters: dict, ignore_keys: set | None = None):
  1025          required_keys = {"rope_type", "rope_theta"}
  1026          rope_type = rope_parameters["rope_type"]
  1027          received_keys = set(rope_parameters.keys())
  1028          self._check_received_keys(rope_type, received_keys, required_keys, ignore_keys=ignore_keys)
  1029  
  1030          partial_rotary_factor = rope_parameters.get("partial_rotary_factor")
  1031          if partial_rotary_factor is None:
  1032              logger.warning(
  1033                  "`rope_parameters`'s partial_rotary_factor is None. This will default to 1.0 in the computation, "
  1034                  "making this equivalent to the linear_scaling RoPE type. Provide a value in the range [0.0, 1.0) to "
  1035                  "make use of the proportional RoPE functionality."
  1036              )
  1037  
  1038      @staticmethod
  1039      def _check_received_keys(
  1040          rope_type: str,
  1041          received_keys: set,
  1042          required_keys: set,
  1043          optional_keys: set | None = None,
  1044          ignore_keys: set | None = None,
  1045      ):
  1046          """Compare the received keys in `config.rope_parameters` against the expected and optional keys"""
  1047          # BC: "rope_type" was originally "type" -- let's check for "rope_type" when "type" is present
  1048          if "type" in received_keys:
  1049              received_keys -= {"type"}
  1050              required_keys.add("rope_type")
  1051  
  1052          optional_keys = optional_keys or set()
  1053          if "partial_rotary_factor" not in optional_keys:
  1054              optional_keys.add("partial_rotary_factor")
  1055  
  1056          # Some models need to store model-specific keys, and we don't want to throw warning at them
  1057          if ignore_keys is not None:
  1058              received_keys -= set(ignore_keys)
  1059  
  1060          missing_keys = required_keys - received_keys
  1061          if missing_keys:
  1062              raise KeyError(f"Missing required keys in `rope_parameters` for 'rope_type'='{rope_type}': {missing_keys}")
  1063  
  1064          unused_keys = received_keys - required_keys - optional_keys
  1065          if unused_keys:
  1066              logger.warning(f"Unrecognized keys in `rope_parameters` for 'rope_type'='{rope_type}': {unused_keys}")
  1067  
  1068  
  1069  def rope_config_validation(config: RotaryEmbeddingConfigMixin, ignore_keys: set | None = None):
  1070      """
  1071      This is a deprecated function.
  1072      It has been kept for backward compatibility with custom code models.
  1073      """
  1074      warnings.warn(
  1075          "`rope_config_validation` is deprecated and has been removed. "
  1076          "Its functionality has been moved to RotaryEmbeddingConfigMixin.validate_rope method. "
  1077          "PreTrainedConfig inherits this class, so please call self.validate_rope() instead. "
  1078          "Also, make sure to use the new rope_parameters syntax. "
  1079          "You can call self.standardize_rope_params() in the meantime.",
  1080          FutureWarning,
  1081      )
  1082      config.standardize_rope_params()
  1083      config.validate_rope()

    27  @auto_docstring(checkpoint="google/gemma-4-e2b-it")
    28  @strict
    29  class Gemma4AudioConfig(PreTrainedConfig):
    30      r"""
    31      subsampling_conv_channels (`list[int]`, defaults to `[128, 32]`):
    32          Channel sizes for the convolutional layers in the Sub-sample Convolution Projection.
    33      residual_weight (`float`, defaults to `0.5`):
    34          Scaling applied to hidden_states prior to combining with the residual in the feedforward.
    35      attention_chunk_size (`int`, defaults to `12`):
    36          The sub-sequence size for attention processing.
    37      attention_context_left (`int`, defaults to `13`):
    38          The leftward context size for the attention chunk.
    39      attention_context_right (`int`, defaults to `0`):
    40          The rightward context size for the attention chunk.
    41      attention_logit_cap (`float`, defaults to `50.0`):
    42          Cap applied to attention weights.
    43      attention_invalid_logits_value (`float`, defaults to `1e-9`):
    44          Value to use for invalid logits in attention.
    45      use_clipped_linears (`bool`, defaults to `True`):
    46          If true, apply clipping to the Linear layers, drawing bounds from the model checkpoint.
    47      gradient_clipping (`float`, defaults to `1e10`):
    48          Clipping value used to stabilize extremely large gradient values.
    49      output_proj_dims (`int`, defaults to `1536`):
    50          Dimension of the final linear projection from `hidden_size` to the model's output.
    51      """
    52  
    53      model_type = "gemma4_audio"
    54  
    55      hidden_size: int = 1024
    56      num_hidden_layers: int = 12
    57      num_attention_heads: int = 8
    58      hidden_act: str = "silu"
    59  
    60      # subsampling parameters
    61      subsampling_conv_channels: list[int] | tuple[int, int] = (128, 32)
    62  
    63      # conformer parameters
    64      conv_kernel_size: int = 5
    65      residual_weight: float = 0.5
    66      attention_chunk_size: int = 12
    67      attention_context_left: int = 13
    68      attention_context_right: int = 0
    69      attention_logit_cap: float = 50.0
    70      attention_invalid_logits_value: float = -1.0e9
    71  
    72      use_clipped_linears: bool = True
    73      rms_norm_eps: float = 1e-6
    74      gradient_clipping: float = 1e10
    75      output_proj_dims: int = 1536
    76      initializer_range: float = interval(min=0.0, max=1.0)(default=0.02)
    77  
    78      def __post_init__(self, **kwargs):
    79          # JSON serialization converts tuples to lists, convert back
    80          if isinstance(self.subsampling_conv_channels, tuple):
    81              self.subsampling_conv_channels = list(self.subsampling_conv_channels)
    82          super().__post_init__(**kwargs)

    85  @auto_docstring(checkpoint="google/gemma-4-e2b-it")
    86  @strict
    87  class Gemma4TextConfig(PreTrainedConfig):
    88      r"""
    89      use_bidirectional_attention (`str`, *optional*):
    90          Controls bidirectional attention behavior. When set to `"vision"`, vision tokens
    91          attend bidirectionally while text tokens use causal attention. When set to `"all"`,
    92          all tokens use bidirectional attention.
    93      vocab_size_per_layer_input (`int`, defaults to 262144):
    94          Vocabulary size for the per-layer input embeddings (PLE). Used by models with
    95          per-layer residual streams where a smaller embedding is added at each decoder layer.
    96      hidden_size_per_layer_input (`int`, defaults to 256):
    97          Per-layer hidden dimension for the PLE system. The actual embedding weight has shape
    98          `[vocab_size_per_layer_input, num_hidden_layers * hidden_size_per_layer_input]`
    99          because all layers are packed into a single table. See the [Gemma4](https://huggingface.co/docs/transformers/main/en/model_doc/gemma4#per-layer-embeddings-ple) docs
   100          for a description of the full PLE pipeline.
   101      attention_k_eq_v (`bool`, defaults to `False`):
   102          Whether keys and values share the same projection weights. When `True`, the key
   103          projection output is reused as the value projection.
   104      num_kv_shared_layers (`int`, defaults to 0):
   105          Number of consecutive decoder layers that share the same key-value projections.
   106          A value of 0 means no sharing (each layer has independent KV projections).
   107      enable_moe_block (`bool`, defaults to `False`):
   108          Whether to enable Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) blocks in the decoder layers. When
   109          `True`, eligible layers will use a sparse MoE feed-forward network.
   110      use_double_wide_mlp (`bool`, defaults to `False`):
   111          Whether to use a double-width MLP with fused gate and up projections.
   112      top_k_experts (`int`, *optional*):
   113          Number of experts activated per token in MoE layers. Only used when
   114          `enable_moe_block=True`.
   115      moe_intermediate_size (`int`, *optional*):
   116          Intermediate (hidden) size of each expert's feed-forward network in MoE layers.
   117          Only used when `enable_moe_block=True`.
   118      """
   119  
   120      model_type = "gemma4_text"
   121      keys_to_ignore_at_inference = ["past_key_values"]
   122      base_model_tp_plan = {
   123          "layers.*.self_attn.q_proj": "colwise",
   124          "layers.*.self_attn.k_proj": "colwise",
   125          "layers.*.self_attn.v_proj": "colwise",
   126          "layers.*.self_attn.q_norm": "replicated_with_grad_allreduce",
   127          "layers.*.self_attn.k_norm": "replicated_with_grad_allreduce",
   128          "layers.*.self_attn.o_proj": "rowwise",
   129          "layers.*.mlp.gate_proj": "colwise",
   130          "layers.*.mlp.up_proj": "colwise",
   131          "layers.*.mlp.down_proj": "rowwise",
   132          "layers.*.experts.gate_up_proj": "packed_colwise",
   133          "layers.*.experts.down_proj": "rowwise",
   134          "layers.*.experts": "moe_tp_experts",
   135      }
   136      base_model_ep_plan = {
   137          # EP plan for google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it: do not tp in attention (num_global_key_value_heads=2 too small to partition)
   138          "layers.*.mlp.gate_proj": "colwise",
   139          "layers.*.mlp.up_proj": "colwise",
   140          "layers.*.mlp.down_proj": "rowwise",
   141          "layers.*.router": "ep_router",
   142          "layers.*.experts.gate_up_proj": "grouped_gemm",
   143          "layers.*.experts.down_proj": "grouped_gemm",
   144          "layers.*.experts": "moe_tp_experts",
   145      }
   146      base_model_pp_plan = {
   147          "embed_tokens": (["input_ids"], ["inputs_embeds"]),
   148          "layers": (["hidden_states", "attention_mask"], ["hidden_states"]),
   149          "norm": (["hidden_states"], ["hidden_states"]),
   150      }
   151  
   152      vocab_size: int = 262_144
   153      hidden_size: int = 2304
   154      intermediate_size: int = 9216
   155      num_hidden_layers: int = 30
   156      num_attention_heads: int = 8
   157      num_key_value_heads: int = 4
   158      head_dim: int = 256
   159      hidden_activation: str = "gelu_pytorch_tanh"
   160      max_position_embeddings: int = 131_072
   161      initializer_range: float = 0.02
   162      rms_norm_eps: float = 1e-6
   163      use_cache: bool = True
   164      pad_token_id: int | None = 0
   165      eos_token_id: int | list[int] | None = 1
   166      bos_token_id: int | None = 2
   167      tie_word_embeddings: bool = True
   168      rope_parameters: dict | None = None
   169      attention_bias: bool = False
   170      attention_dropout: int | float | None = 0.0
   171      sliding_window: int = 512
   172      layer_types: list[str] | None = None
   173      final_logit_softcapping: float | None = None
   174      use_bidirectional_attention: Literal["all", "vision"] | None = None
   175      vocab_size_per_layer_input: int = 262_144
   176      hidden_size_per_layer_input: int = 256
   177      attention_k_eq_v: bool = False
   178      num_kv_shared_layers: int = 0
   179      enable_moe_block: bool = False
   180      use_double_wide_mlp: bool = False
   181      num_experts: int | None = None
   182      top_k_experts: int | None = None
   183      moe_intermediate_size: int | None = None
   184  
   185      def __post_init__(self, **kwargs):
   186          if self.use_bidirectional_attention == "all":
   187              self.is_causal = False
   188              self.sliding_window = (self.sliding_window // 2) + 1  # due to fa we set exclusive bounds
   189  
   190          if self.layer_types is None:
   191              sliding_window_pattern = 6  # by default 5:1
   192              self.layer_types = [
   193                  "sliding_attention" if bool((i + 1) % sliding_window_pattern) else "full_attention"
   194                  for i in range(self.num_hidden_layers)
   195              ]
   196  
   197          if self.layer_types and (last_layer_type := self.layer_types[-1]) != "full_attention":
   198              logger.warning(
   199                  f"Last layer must use `full_attention`, but got `{last_layer_type}`. Forcing last layer to `full_attention`."
   200              )
   201              self.layer_types[-1] = "full_attention"
   202  
   203          default_rope_params: dict[Literal["full_attention", "sliding_attention"] : dict[str, Any]] = {
   204              "sliding_attention": {"rope_type": "default", "rope_theta": 10_000.0},
   205              "full_attention": {"rope_type": "proportional", "partial_rotary_factor": 0.25, "rope_theta": 1_000_000.0},
   206          }
   207          if self.rope_parameters is None:
   208              self.rope_parameters = default_rope_params
   209  
   210          global_head_dim = kwargs.pop("global_head_dim", 512)
   211          num_global_key_value_heads = kwargs.pop("num_global_key_value_heads", None)
   212          if "per_layer_config" not in kwargs:
   213              layer_overrides: dict[str, Any] = {"head_dim": global_head_dim}
   214              # `attention_k_eq_v` gates the kv-head override for the models that declare it;
   215              # models that drop the attribute entirely are ungated, hence the `True` fallback.
   216              num_key_value_heads = num_global_key_value_heads if getattr(self, "attention_k_eq_v", True) else None
   217              if num_key_value_heads is not None:
   218                  layer_overrides["num_key_value_heads"] = num_key_value_heads
   219              kwargs["per_layer_config"] = {
   220                  layer_idx: layer_overrides
   221                  for layer_idx, layer_type in enumerate(self.layer_types)
   222                  if layer_type == "full_attention"
   223              }
   224  
   225          super().__post_init__(**kwargs)
   226  
   227      def convert_rope_params_to_dict(self, **kwargs):
   228          # No need to handle BC for new models, because they have no old-format `rope_scaling`
   229          return kwargs
   230
