   232  @auto_docstring(checkpoint="google/gemma-4-e2b-it")
   233  @strict
   234  class Gemma4VisionConfig(PreTrainedConfig):
   235      r"""
   236      pooling_kernel_size (`int`, *optional*):
   237          Spatial pooling kernel size applied after patchification.
   238      position_embedding_size (`int`, defaults to 10240):
   239          Maximum number of position embeddings for the vision encoder. Controls the size of
   240          the learned 2D position embedding table used by the patch embedder.
   241      use_clipped_linears (`bool`, defaults to `False`):
   242          Whether to use weight-clipped linear layers. When enabled, linear layer weights are
   243          clamped to a fixed range during the forward pass to improve numerical stability.
   244      standardize (`bool`, defaults to `False`):
   245          If true, applies a bias and scale to the soft tokens returned from the pooler.
   246      """
   247  
   248      model_type = "gemma4_vision"
   249      base_model_tp_plan = {
   250          "encoder.layers.*.self_attn.q_proj": "colwise",
   251          "encoder.layers.*.self_attn.k_proj": "colwise",
   252          "encoder.layers.*.self_attn.v_proj": "colwise",
   253          "encoder.layers.*.self_attn.q_norm": "replicated_with_grad_allreduce",
   254          "encoder.layers.*.self_attn.k_norm": "replicated_with_grad_allreduce",
   255          "encoder.layers.*.self_attn.o_proj": "rowwise",
   256          "encoder.layers.*.mlp.gate_proj": "colwise",
   257          "encoder.layers.*.mlp.up_proj": "colwise",
   258          "encoder.layers.*.mlp.down_proj": "rowwise",
   259      }
   260  
   261      default_theta = 100.0
   262  
   263      hidden_size: int = 768
   264      intermediate_size: int = 3072
   265      num_hidden_layers: int = 16
   266      num_attention_heads: int = 12
   267      num_key_value_heads: int = 12
   268      head_dim: int = 64
   269      hidden_activation: str = "gelu_pytorch_tanh"
   270      rms_norm_eps: float = 1e-6
   271      max_position_embeddings: int = 131_072
   272      attention_bias: bool | None = False
   273      attention_dropout: float | None = 0.0
   274      rope_parameters: dict | None = None
   275      pooling_kernel_size: int = 3
   276      patch_size: int = 16
   277      position_embedding_size: int = 10 * 1024
   278      use_clipped_linears: bool = False
   279      standardize: bool = False
   280      initializer_range: float = 0.02
   281  
   282      def __post_init__(self, **kwargs):
   283          if self.rope_parameters is None:
   284              self.rope_parameters = {"rope_type": "default", "rope_theta": 100.0}
   285  
   286          super().__post_init__(**kwargs)

   289  @auto_docstring(checkpoint="google/gemma-4-e2b-it")
   290  @strict
   291  class Gemma4Config(PreTrainedConfig):
   292      r"""
   293      boi_token_id (`int`, *optional*, defaults to 255999):
   294          The begin-of-image token index to wrap the image prompt.
   295      eoi_token_id (`int`, *optional*, defaults to 258882):
   296          The end-of-image token index to wrap the image prompt.
   297      boa_token_id (`int`, *optional*, defaults to 256000):
   298          The begin-of-audio token index to wrap the audio prompt.
   299      eoa_token_index (`int`, *optional*, defaults to 258883):
   300          The end-of-audio token index to wrap the audio prompt.
   301  
   302      Example:
   303  
   304      ```python
   305      >>> from transformers import (
   306      >>>     Gemma4AudioConfig,
   307      >>>     Gemma4Config,
   308      >>>     Gemma4ForConditionalGeneration,
   309      >>>     Gemma4TextConfig,
   310      >>>     Gemma4VisionConfig,
   311      >>> )
   312  
   313      >>> # Initializing a Gemma 4 Audio config.
   314      >>> audio_config = Gemma4AudioConfig()
   315  
   316      >>> # Initializing a Gemma 4 Text config.
   317      >>> text_config = Gemma4TextConfig()
   318  
   319      >>> # Initializing a Gemma 4 vision config.
   320      >>> vision_config = Gemma4VisionConfig()
   321  
   322      >>> # Initializing a Gemma 4 config similar to google/gemma-4-e2b-it
   323      >>> configuration = Gemma4Config(text_config, vision_config, audio_config)
   324  
   325      >>> # Initializing a model from the google/gemma-4-e2b-it configuration
   326      >>> model = Gemma4ForConditionalGeneration(configuration)
   327  
   328      >>> # Accessing the model configuration
   329      >>> configuration = model.config
   330      ```"""
   331  
   332      model_type = "gemma4"
   333      sub_configs = {
   334          "text_config": Gemma4TextConfig,
   335          "vision_config": Gemma4VisionConfig,
   336          "audio_config": Gemma4AudioConfig,
   337      }
   338  
   339      text_config: Gemma4TextConfig | dict[str, Any] | None = None
   340      vision_config: Gemma4VisionConfig | dict[str, Any] | None = None
   341      audio_config: Gemma4AudioConfig | dict[str, Any] | None = None
   342      boi_token_id: int | None = 255_999
   343      eoi_token_id: int | None = 258_882
   344      image_token_id: int | None = 258_880
   345      video_token_id: int | None = 258_884
   346      boa_token_id: int | None = 256_000
   347      eoa_token_index: int | None = 258_883
   348      audio_token_id: int | None = 258_881
   349      initializer_range: float | None = 0.02
   350      tie_word_embeddings: bool = True
   351  
   352      def __post_init__(self, **kwargs):
   353          if self.text_config is None:
   354              self.text_config = Gemma4TextConfig()
   355              logger.info("text_config is None. Using default Gemma4TextConfig.")
   356          elif isinstance(self.text_config, dict):
   357              self.text_config = Gemma4TextConfig(**self.text_config)
   358  
   359          if self.vision_config is None:
   360              logger.info("vision_config is None. Gemma4Model.vision_tower will not be initialized.")
   361          if isinstance(self.vision_config, dict):
   362              self.vision_config = Gemma4VisionConfig(**self.vision_config)
   363  
   364          if self.audio_config is None:
   365              logger.info("audio_config is None. Gemma4Model.audio_tower will not be initialized.")
   366          if isinstance(self.audio_config, dict):
   367              self.audio_config = Gemma4AudioConfig(**self.audio_config)
   368  
   369          super().__post_init__(**kwargs)
   370  
   371  
   372  __all__ = ["Gemma4AudioConfig", "Gemma4Config", "Gemma4TextConfig", "Gemma4VisionConfig"]

    64          if name.endswith("norm.weight"):

   120  @ModelBase.register("Gemma3ForCausalLM", "Gemma3ForConditionalGeneration")
   121  class Gemma3Model(TextModel):
   122      model_arch = gguf.MODEL_ARCH.GEMMA3
   123  
   124      def norm_shift(self, name: str) -> float:
   125          return 1.0 if name.endswith("norm.weight") else 0.0  # Gemma3RMSNorm adds 1.0 to the norm value
   126  
   127      def set_vocab(self):
   128          if (self.dir_model / "tokenizer.model").is_file():
   129              self._set_vocab_sentencepiece()
   130              self.gguf_writer.add_add_space_prefix(False)
   131          else:
   132              self._set_vocab_gpt2()
   133  
   134      def set_gguf_parameters(self):
   135          super().set_gguf_parameters()
   136          hparams = self.hparams
   137  
   138          # some default values are not specified in the hparams
   139          self.gguf_writer.add_context_length(hparams.get("max_position_embeddings", 131072))
   140          self.gguf_writer.add_head_count(hparams.get("num_attention_heads", 8))
   141          self.gguf_writer.add_layer_norm_rms_eps(self.hparams.get("rms_norm_eps", 1e-6))
   142          self.gguf_writer.add_key_length(hparams.get("head_dim", 256))
   143          self.gguf_writer.add_value_length(hparams.get("head_dim", 256))
   144          self.gguf_writer.add_rope_freq_base(self.rope_parameters.get("full_attention", self.rope_parameters).get("rope_theta", 1_000_000.0)) # for global layers
   145          # attn_logit_softcapping is removed in Gemma3
   146          assert hparams.get("attn_logit_softcapping") is None
   147          if (final_logit_softcap := hparams.get("final_logit_softcapping")):
   148              self.gguf_writer.add_final_logit_softcapping(final_logit_softcap)
   149          if hparams.get("sliding_window_pattern") != 1:
   150              self.gguf_writer.add_sliding_window(hparams["sliding_window"])
   151          self.gguf_writer.add_head_count_kv(hparams.get("num_key_value_heads", 4))
   152  
   153      def modify_tensors(self, data_torch: Tensor, name: str, bid: int | None) -> Iterable[tuple[str, Tensor]]:
   154          # remove OOV (out-of-vocabulary) rows in token_embd
   155          if "embed_tokens.weight" in name:
   156              n_vocab_real = -1
   157              if (self.dir_model / "tokenizer.model").is_file():
   158                  tokens = self._create_vocab_sentencepiece()[0]
   159                  n_vocab_real = len(tokens)
   160              else:
   161                  with open(self.dir_model / "tokenizer.json", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   162                      tokenizer_json = json.load(f)
   163                      n_vocab_real = len(tokenizer_json["model"]["vocab"]) + len(tokenizer_json["added_tokens"])
   164              data_torch = data_torch[:n_vocab_real]
   165  
   166          # ref code in Gemma3RMSNorm
   167          # output = output * (1.0 + self.weight.float())
   168          # note: this is not the case on gemma3n
   169          f_shift = self.norm_shift(name)
   170          if f_shift != 0.0:
   171              data_torch = data_torch + f_shift
   172  
   173          yield from super().modify_tensors(data_torch, name, bid)
   174  
   175  
   176  @ModelBase.register("Gemma3TextModel")
   177  class EmbeddingGemma(Gemma3Model):
   178      model_arch = gguf.MODEL_ARCH.GEMMA_EMBEDDING
   179      module_paths = []
   180      dense_features_dims = {}
   181  
   182      def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
   183          super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
   184          if self.sentence_transformers_dense_modules:
   185              # read modules.json to determine if model has Dense layers
   186              modules_file = self.dir_model / "modules.json"
   187              if modules_file.is_file():
   188                  with open(modules_file, encoding="utf-8") as modules_json_file:
   189                      mods = json.load(modules_json_file)
   190                  for mod in mods:
   191                      if mod["type"].endswith("Dense"):
   192                          mod_path = mod["path"]
   193                          # check if model.safetensors file for Dense layer exists
   194                          model_tensors_file = self.dir_model / mod_path / "model.safetensors"
   195                          if model_tensors_file.is_file():
   196                              self.module_paths.append(mod_path)
   197                              # read config.json of the Dense layer to get in/out features
   198                              mod_conf_file = self.dir_model / mod_path / "config.json"
   199                              if mod_conf_file.is_file():
   200                                  with open(mod_conf_file, encoding="utf-8") as mod_conf_json_file:
   201                                      mod_conf = json.load(mod_conf_json_file)
   202                                      # hparams dense_2_feat_out and dense_3_feat_in are required when loading model's dense weights
   203                                      prefix = self._get_dense_prefix(mod_path)
   204                                      if mod_conf["in_features"] is not None and mod_conf["out_features"] is not None:
   205                                          self.dense_features_dims[prefix] = (mod_conf["in_features"], mod_conf["out_features"])
   206  
   207      def generate_extra_tensors(self) -> Iterable[tuple[str, Tensor]]:
   208          from safetensors.torch import load_file
   209          module_paths = list(self.module_paths)
   210          for i, module_path in enumerate(module_paths):
   211              tensors_file = self.dir_model / module_path / "model.safetensors"
   212              local_tensors = load_file(tensors_file)
   213              tensor_name = self._get_dense_prefix(module_path)
   214              for name, local_tensor in local_tensors.items():
   215                  if not name.endswith(".weight"):
   216                      continue
   217                  orig_name = name.replace("linear", tensor_name)
   218                  name = self.map_tensor_name(orig_name)
   219                  yield name, local_tensor.clone()
   220  
   221      @staticmethod
   222      def _get_dense_prefix(module_path) -> str:
   223          """Get the tensor name prefix for the Dense layer from module path."""
   224          tensor_name = "dense_2" if module_path == "2_Dense" else "dense_3"
   225          return tensor_name
   226  
   227      def set_gguf_parameters(self):
   228          super().set_gguf_parameters()
   229  
   230          # Override the sliding window size as it gets adjusted by the Gemma3TextConfig
   231          # constructor. We want to use the value from the original model's config.json.
   232          # ref: https://github.com/huggingface/transformers/pull/40700
   233          with open(self.dir_model / "config.json", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   234              config = json.load(f)
   235              orig_sliding_window = config.get("sliding_window")
   236              if orig_sliding_window is None:
   237                  raise ValueError("sliding_window not found in model config - this is required for the model")
   238  
   239              logger.info(f"Using original sliding_window from config: {orig_sliding_window} "
   240                          f"instead of {self.hparams['sliding_window']}")
   241              self.gguf_writer.add_sliding_window(orig_sliding_window)
   242          if self.sentence_transformers_dense_modules:
   243              for dense, dims in self.dense_features_dims.items():
   244                  logger.info(f"Setting dense layer {dense} in/out features to {dims}")
   245                  self.gguf_writer.add_dense_features_dims(dense, dims[0], dims[1])
   246  
   247          self._try_set_pooling_type()
   248

   250  @ModelBase.register("Gemma3ForConditionalGeneration")
   251  class Gemma3VisionModel(MmprojModel):
   252      def set_gguf_parameters(self):
   253          super().set_gguf_parameters()
   254          hparams = self.hparams
   255          self.gguf_writer.add_clip_projector_type(gguf.VisionProjectorType.GEMMA3)
   256          # default values below are taken from HF transformers code
   257          self.gguf_writer.add_vision_attention_layernorm_eps(hparams.get("layer_norm_eps", 1e-6))
   258          self.gguf_writer.add_vision_use_gelu(True)
   259          # calculate proj_scale_factor (used by tinygemma3 test model)
   260          image_seq_length = self.preprocessor_config.get("image_seq_length", 256)
   261          n_per_side = int(image_seq_length ** 0.5)
   262          image_size = self.hparams["image_size"]
   263          patch_size = self.hparams["patch_size"]
   264          proj_scale_factor = (image_size // patch_size) // n_per_side
   265          if proj_scale_factor > 0 and proj_scale_factor != 4:
   266              # we only need to write this if it's not the default value
   267              # in this case, we are converting a test model
   268              self.gguf_writer.add_vision_projector_scale_factor(proj_scale_factor)
   269  
   270      def tensor_force_quant(self, name, new_name, bid, n_dims):
   271          # related to https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/issues/13025
   272          if "input_projection" in name:
   273              return gguf.GGMLQuantizationType.F16
   274          if ".embeddings." in name:
   275              return gguf.GGMLQuantizationType.F32
   276          return super().tensor_force_quant(name, new_name, bid, n_dims)
   277  
   278      @classmethod
   279      def filter_tensors(cls, item: tuple[str, Callable[[], Tensor]]) -> tuple[str, Callable[[], Tensor]] | None:
   280          name, gen = item
   281  
   282          if "vision_model.head." in name:
   283              # skip redundant tensors for tinygemma3
   284              return None
   285  
   286          if not name.startswith(("multi_modal_projector.", "vision_tower.", "multimodal_projector.", "vision_model.")):
   287              return None
   288  
   289          name = name.replace("_weight", ".weight")
   290  
   291          return super().filter_tensors((name, gen))
   292  
   293      def modify_tensors(self, data_torch: Tensor, name: str, bid: int | None) -> Iterable[tuple[str, Tensor]]:
   294          # correct norm value ; only this "soft_emb_norm" need to be corrected as it's part of Gemma projector
   295          # the other norm values are part of SigLIP model, and they are already correct
   296          # ref code: Gemma3RMSNorm
   297          if "soft_emb_norm.weight" in name:
   298              logger.info(f"Correcting norm value for '{name}'")
   299              data_torch = data_torch + 1
   300  
   301          yield from super().modify_tensors(data_torch, name, bid)
   302  
   303  
   304  class ConformerAudioModel(MmprojModel):
   305      _batch_norm_tensors: list[dict[str, Tensor]] | None = None
   306  
   307      @staticmethod
   308      def is_audio_tensor(name: str):
   309          return any(p in name for p in ["audio", "codebook", "conformer", "depth_embedding", "depthformer", "depth_linear"])
   310  
   311      def tensor_force_quant(self, name, new_name, bid, n_dims):
   312          if ConformerAudioModel.is_audio_tensor(name):
   313              if ".conv" in name or "_conv" in name and ".weight" in name:
   314                  return gguf.GGMLQuantizationType.F32
   315          return super().tensor_force_quant(name, new_name, bid, n_dims)
   316  
   317      def modify_tensors(self, data_torch: Tensor, name: str, bid: int | None) -> Iterable[tuple[str, Tensor]]:
   318          # fold running_mean, running_var and eps into weight and bias for batch_norm
   319          if "batch_norm" in name:
   320              if self._batch_norm_tensors is None:
   321                  self._batch_norm_tensors = [{} for _ in range(self.block_count)]
   322              assert bid is not None
   323              self._batch_norm_tensors[bid][name] = data_torch
   324  
   325              if len(self._batch_norm_tensors[bid]) < 5:
   326                  return
   327  
   328              weight = self._batch_norm_tensors[bid][f"conformer.layers.{bid}.conv.batch_norm.weight"]
   329              bias = self._batch_norm_tensors[bid][f"conformer.layers.{bid}.conv.batch_norm.bias"]
   330              running_mean = self._batch_norm_tensors[bid][f"conformer.layers.{bid}.conv.batch_norm.running_mean"]
   331              running_var = self._batch_norm_tensors[bid][f"conformer.layers.{bid}.conv.batch_norm.running_var"]
   332              eps = 1e-5 # default value
   333  
   334              a = weight / torch.sqrt(running_var + eps)
   335              b = bias - running_mean * a
   336              yield from super().modify_tensors(a, f"conformer.layers.{bid}.conv.batch_norm.weight", bid)
   337              yield from super().modify_tensors(b, f"conformer.layers.{bid}.conv.batch_norm.bias", bid)
   338              return
   339  
   340          # reshape conv weights
   341          if name.startswith("conformer.pre_encode.conv.") and name.endswith(".bias"):
   342              data_torch = data_torch[:, None, None]
   343          if "conv.depthwise_conv" in name and name.endswith(".weight"):
   344              assert data_torch.shape[1] == 1
   345              data_torch = data_torch.reshape(data_torch.shape[0], data_torch.shape[2])
   346          if "conv.pointwise_conv" in name and name.endswith(".weight"):
   347              assert data_torch.shape[2] == 1
   348              data_torch = data_torch.reshape(data_torch.shape[0], data_torch.shape[1])
   349  
   350          mapped_name = self.map_tensor_name(name, (".weight", ".bias", ".input_max", ".input_min", ".output_max", ".output_min"))
   351          yield (mapped_name, data_torch)
   352  
   353  
   354  @ModelBase.register("Gemma3nForConditionalGeneration")
   355  class Gemma3nVisionAudioModel(ConformerAudioModel):
   356      has_audio_encoder = True
   357      has_vision_encoder = True
   358  
   359      # Double indexed mapping for MobileNetV5 blocks (not supported by tensor_mapping.py)
   360      # This is the only known model having this, so we prefer implementing it outside of tensor_mapping.py
   361      block_tensor_mapping = {
   362          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.conv_exp.weight":             "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.conv_exp.weight",
   363          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.bn1.weight":                  "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.bn1.weight",
   364          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.conv_pwl.weight":             "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.conv_pwl.weight",
   365          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.bn2.weight":                  "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.bn2.weight",
   366          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.dw_start.conv.weight":        "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.dw_start.conv.weight",
   367          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.dw_start.bn.weight":          "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.dw_start.bn.weight",
   368          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.dw_mid.conv.weight":          "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.dw_mid.conv.weight",
   369          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.dw_mid.bn.weight":            "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.dw_mid.bn.weight",
   370          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.pw_exp.conv.weight":          "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.pw_exp.conv.weight",
   371          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.pw_exp.bn.weight":            "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.pw_exp.bn.weight",
   372          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.pw_proj.conv.weight":         "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.pw_proj.conv.weight",
   373          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.pw_proj.bn.weight":           "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.pw_proj.bn.weight",
   374          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.layer_scale.gamma":           "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.layer_scale.gamma",
   375          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.attn.query.proj.weight":      "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.attn.query.proj.weight",
   376          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.attn.key.proj.weight":        "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.attn.key.proj.weight",
   377          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.attn.value.proj.weight":      "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.attn.value.proj.weight",
   378          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.attn.output.proj.weight":     "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.attn.output.proj.weight",
   379          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.attn.key.down_conv.weight":   "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.attn.key.down_conv.weight",
   380          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.attn.key.norm.weight":        "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.attn.key.norm.weight",
   381          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.attn.value.down_conv.weight": "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.attn.value.down_conv.weight",
   382          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.attn.value.norm.weight":      "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.attn.value.norm.weight",
   383          "model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{bid}.{sid}.norm.weight":                 "v.blk.{bid}.{sid}.norm.weight",
   384      }
   385  
   386      def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
   387          # Parent init will call find_hparam which now returns 0 for empty keys
   388          super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
   389          assert self.hparams_vision is not None
   390          self.hparams_vision["n_layers"] = 128 # fake value for audio encoder, vision encoder doesn't use it
   391          self.hparams_vision["intermediate_size"] = self.hparams_vision.get("intermediate_size", 2048) * 4
   392          self.hparams_vision["num_attention_heads"] = self.hparams_vision.get("num_attention_heads", 8)
   393  
   394          # MobileNetV5 does not use image_mean/std
   395          self.preprocessor_config["image_mean"] = [0.0 ,0.0 , 0.0]
   396          self.preprocessor_config["image_std"] = [1.0 ,1.0 ,1.0]
   397          self.hparams_vision["image_size"] = self.preprocessor_config.get(
   398              "size", {"height": 768, "width": 768}
   399          )["height"]
   400  
   401          # Image sequence length (256 tokens = 16x16 for Gemma3n)
   402          image_seq_length = self.preprocessor_config.get("image_seq_length", 256)
   403          image_size = self.hparams_vision["image_size"]
   404          self.hparams_vision["patch_size"] = image_size // image_seq_length
   405  
   406          # remap audio hparams
   407          assert self.hparams_audio is not None
   408          self.hparams_audio["n_layers"] = self.hparams_audio["conf_num_hidden_layers"]
   409          self.hparams_audio["num_attention_heads"] = self.hparams_audio["conf_num_attention_heads"]
   410          self.hparams_audio["feat_in"] = self.hparams_audio["input_feat_size"]
   411          self.hparams_audio["intermediate_size"] = self.hparams_audio.get("intermediate_size", 6144)
   412  
   413      def set_gguf_parameters(self):
   414          super().set_gguf_parameters()
   415  
   416          # vision params
   417          self.gguf_writer.add_clip_vision_projector_type(gguf.VisionProjectorType.GEMMA3NV)
   418          self.gguf_writer.add_vision_attention_layernorm_eps(self.hparams.get("layer_norm_eps", 1e-6))
   419  
   420          # audio params
   421          assert self.hparams_audio is not None
   422          self.gguf_writer.add_clip_audio_projector_type(gguf.VisionProjectorType.GEMMA3NA)
   423          self.gguf_writer.add_audio_num_mel_bins(self.hparams_audio["feat_in"])
   424          self.gguf_writer.add_audio_attention_layernorm_eps(1e-5)
   425  
   426      def tensor_force_quant(self, name, new_name, bid, n_dims):
   427          # Force quantization settings for specific tensor types
   428          if "input_projection" in name or "input_proj" in name:
   429              return gguf.GGMLQuantizationType.F16
   430          if ".embeddings." in name or "stem" in name:
   431              return gguf.GGMLQuantizationType.F32
   432          return super().tensor_force_quant(name, new_name, bid, n_dims)
   433  
   434      def custom_map(self, name: str) -> str:
   435          """Parses names like model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.1.2.suffix and applies template mapping."""
   436          parts = name.split(".")
   437          # MobileNet blocks have at least 7 parts: model, vision_tower, timm_model, blocks, bid, sid, and suffix
   438          if len(parts) >= 7:
   439              bid, sid = parts[4], parts[5]
   440              suffix = ".".join(parts[6:])
   441              template = f"model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks.{{bid}}.{{sid}}.{suffix}"
   442              if template in self.block_tensor_mapping:
   443                  return self.block_tensor_mapping[template].format(bid=bid, sid=sid)
   444  
   445          raise ValueError(f"Unknown name: {name}")
   446  
   447      def modify_tensors(self, data_torch: Tensor, name: str, bid: int | None) -> Iterable[tuple[str, Tensor]]:
   448          if (ConformerAudioModel.is_audio_tensor(name)):
   449              name = name.replace("model.audio_tower.conformer.", "conformer.layers.")
   450              yield from super().modify_tensors(data_torch, name, bid)
   451  
   452          # Gemma3n uses
   453          # - model.embed_vision.* for projection layers
   454          # - model.vision_tower.* for vision encoder
   455          # Skip non-vision tensors
   456          if not (name.startswith("model.embed_vision.") or name.startswith("model.vision_tower.")):
   457              return
   458  
   459          if name.startswith("model.vision_tower.timm_model.blocks."):
   460              # Double-indexed block tensors through custom logic
   461              yield (self.custom_map(name), data_torch)
   462              return
   463          else:
   464              # Route non-repeating (conv_stem, msfa, embedding, etc.) and un-catched through tensor_mapping.py
   465              new_name = self.map_tensor_name(name)
   466  
   467          if new_name.endswith("conv_stem.conv.bias") or new_name.endswith("layer_scale.gamma"):
   468              data_torch = data_torch.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1) # [1, C, 1, 1]
   469  
   470          yield from ModelBase.modify_tensors(self, data_torch, new_name, bid)
   471  
   472  
   473  @ModelBase.register("Gemma3nForCausalLM", "Gemma3nForConditionalGeneration")
   474  class Gemma3NModel(Gemma3Model):
   475      model_arch = gguf.MODEL_ARCH.GEMMA3N
   476  
   477      _altup_proj: list[Tensor] = []
   478      _altup_unembd: list[Tensor] = []
   479  
   480      def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
   481          super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
   482          assert self.hparams["altup_num_inputs"] == 4, "Current conversion only supports 4 altup inputs"
   483          self._altup_proj = [
   484              torch.Tensor(), # to be replaced
   485              torch.Tensor(), # to be replaced
   486              torch.Tensor(), # to be replaced
   487          ]
   488          self._altup_unembd = [
   489              torch.Tensor(), # to be replaced
   490              torch.Tensor(), # to be replaced
   491              torch.Tensor(), # to be replaced
   492          ]
   493  
   494      def norm_shift(self, name: str) -> float:
   495          del name
   496          return 0.0 # same value with Gemma3p5RMSNorm scale_shift on python code
   497  
   498      def set_vocab(self):
   499          # For Gemma3n multimodal models, we need the FULL vocab_size (262400)
   500          # which includes special tokens from 262144-262399 for vision/audio.
   501          # The vocab_size_per_layer_input (262144) is only the embedding size per layer.
   502          # Temporarily override the hparams lookup order to prioritize vocab_size.
   503  
   504          # Store original vocab_size_per_layer_input if it exists
   505          vocab_size_per_layer_input = self.hparams.get("vocab_size_per_layer_input")
   506  
   507          # Temporarily remove vocab_size_per_layer_input to force using vocab_size
   508          if vocab_size_per_layer_input is not None:
   509              del self.hparams["vocab_size_per_layer_input"]
   510  
   511          # Call parent set_vocab which will now use vocab_size (262400)
   512          super().set_vocab()
   513  
   514          # Restore vocab_size_per_layer_input for later use
   515          if vocab_size_per_layer_input is not None:
   516              self.hparams["vocab_size_per_layer_input"] = vocab_size_per_layer_input
   517  
   518      def set_gguf_parameters(self):
   519          super().set_gguf_parameters()
   520          self.gguf_writer.add_altup_active_idx(self.hparams["altup_active_idx"])
   521          self.gguf_writer.add_altup_num_inputs(self.hparams["altup_num_inputs"])
   522          self.gguf_writer.add_embedding_length_per_layer_input(self.hparams["hidden_size_per_layer_input"])
   523          self.gguf_writer.add_shared_kv_layers(self.hparams["num_kv_shared_layers"])
   524  
   525          activation_sparsity_scale = []
   526          for s in self.hparams["activation_sparsity_pattern"]:
   527              normal_dist = torch.distributions.normal.Normal(0, 1)
   528              std_multiplier = normal_dist.icdf(torch.tensor(s, dtype=torch.float32))
   529              activation_sparsity_scale.append(std_multiplier.item())
   530          self.gguf_writer.add_activation_sparsity_scale(activation_sparsity_scale)
   531  
   532          sliding_window_pattern = []
   533          for t in self.hparams["layer_types"]:
   534              sliding_window_pattern.append(t == "sliding_attention")
   535          self.gguf_writer.add_sliding_window_pattern(sliding_window_pattern)
   536  
   537      def _stack_matrices(self, matrices: list[Tensor]) -> Tensor | None:
   538          has_all = all(m.numel() > 0 for m in matrices)
   539          if not has_all:
   540              return None
   541          else:
   542              return torch.stack(matrices, dim=0)
   543  
   544      @classmethod
   545      def filter_tensors(cls, item: tuple[str, Callable[[], Tensor]]) -> tuple[str, Callable[[], Tensor]] | None:
   546          name, gen = item
   547  
   548          if name.endswith("_scale"):
   549              name = name + ".weight"
   550  
   551          return super().filter_tensors((name, gen))
   552  
   553      def modify_tensors(self, data_torch: Tensor, name: str, bid: int | None) -> Iterable[tuple[str, Tensor]]:
   554          # TODO: implement self.prediction_coefs.weight.clamp_(...)
   555  
   556          # Pad token embeddings for vision/audio special tokens (262144-262399)
   557          if "embed_tokens.weight" in name or "embed_tokens_per_layer" in name:
   558              # Move to CPU to avoid meta device issues during padding
   559              data_torch = data_torch.to(device="cpu")
   560  
   561              vocab_size = self.hparams.get("vocab_size", 262400)
   562              current_size = data_torch.shape[0]  # First dimension is vocab_size
   563  
   564              if current_size < vocab_size:
   565                  # Pad with zeros for vision/audio tokens (they get embeddings from vision tower)
   566                  padding_size = vocab_size - current_size
   567                  tensor_type = "per-layer embeddings" if "per_layer" in name else "token embeddings"
   568                  logger.info(f"Padding {tensor_type} shape {list(data_torch.shape)} from {current_size} to {vocab_size} (adding {padding_size} vision/audio token slots)")
   569  
   570                  # Create padding with zeros (vision tokens won't use these embeddings)
   571                  padding = torch.zeros((padding_size, data_torch.shape[1]), dtype=data_torch.dtype, device=data_torch.device)
   572                  data_torch = torch.cat([data_torch, padding], dim=0)
   573  
   574              # Continue with normal processing
   575              yield from ModelBase.modify_tensors(self, data_torch, name, bid)
   576              return
   577  
   578          if "altup_unembed_projections" in name:
   579              data_torch = data_torch.to(device="cpu")
   580              # altup_unembed matrices are [hidden_size, hidden_size], NOT vocab-based
   581              # They should NOT be padded
   582              if ".0." in name:
   583                  self._altup_unembd[0] = data_torch
   584              elif ".1." in name:
   585                  self._altup_unembd[1] = data_torch
   586              elif ".2." in name:
   587                  self._altup_unembd[2] = data_torch
   588              else:
   589                  raise ValueError(f"Unknown name: {name}")
   590              out = self._stack_matrices(self._altup_unembd)
   591              if out is not None:
   592                  yield from ModelBase.modify_tensors(self, out, "model.altup_unembed_projections.weight", bid)
   593                  return
   594              else:
   595                  return
   596  
   597          if "altup_projections" in name:
   598              data_torch = data_torch.to(device="cpu")
   599              if ".0." in name:
   600                  self._altup_proj[0] = data_torch
   601              elif ".1." in name:
   602                  self._altup_proj[1] = data_torch
   603              elif ".2." in name:
   604                  self._altup_proj[2] = data_torch
   605              else:
   606                  raise ValueError(f"Unknown name: {name}")
   607              out = self._stack_matrices(self._altup_proj)
   608              if out is not None:
   609                  yield from ModelBase.modify_tensors(self, out, "model.altup_projections.weight", bid)
   610                  return
   611              else:
   612                  return
   613  
   614          yield from super().modify_tensors(data_torch, name, bid)
   615  
   616  
   617  @ModelBase.register("Gemma4ForConditionalGeneration", "Gemma4ForCausalLM")
   618  class Gemma4Model(Gemma3Model):
   619      model_arch = gguf.MODEL_ARCH.GEMMA4
   620  
   621      def norm_shift(self, name: str) -> float:
   622          del name # unused
   623          return 0.0
   624  
   625      def set_vocab(self):
   626          vocab = gguf.LlamaHfVocab(self.dir_model)
   627          tokens = []
   628          scores = []
   629          toktypes = []
   630          visible_tokens = {"<|channel>", "<channel|>", "<|tool_call>", "<tool_call|>", "<|tool_response>", "<tool_response|>", "<|\"|>"}
   631  
   632          for text, score, toktype in vocab.all_tokens():
   633              tokens.append(text)
   634              scores.append(score)
   635              text_str = text.decode()
   636              if text_str in visible_tokens:
   637                  # always render these tokens, so that the chat parser can read them
   638                  toktypes.append(gguf.TokenType.USER_DEFINED)
   639                  logger.info(f"Token '{text_str}' is set to USER_DEFINED")
   640              else:
   641                  toktypes.append(toktype)
   642  
   643          assert len(tokens) == vocab.vocab_size
   644  
   645          self.gguf_writer.add_tokenizer_model("gemma4")
   646          self.gguf_writer.add_token_list(tokens)
   647          self.gguf_writer.add_token_scores(scores)
   648          self.gguf_writer.add_token_types(toktypes)
   649  
   650          special_vocab = gguf.SpecialVocab(self.dir_model, load_merges=True)
   651          special_vocab.add_to_gguf(self.gguf_writer)
   652          self.gguf_writer.add_add_space_prefix(False)
   653          self.gguf_writer.add_add_bos_token(True)
   654  
   655      def set_gguf_parameters(self):
   656          super().set_gguf_parameters()
   657  
   658          num_kv_shared_layers = self.hparams["num_kv_shared_layers"]
   659          self.gguf_writer.add_shared_kv_layers(num_kv_shared_layers)
   660  
   661          # per-layer embedding is optional
   662          n_pl_embd = self.hparams.get("hidden_size_per_layer_input") or 0
   663          self.gguf_writer.add_embedding_length_per_layer_input(n_pl_embd)
   664  
   665          swa_layers = [t == "sliding_attention" for t in self.hparams["layer_types"]]
   666          self.gguf_writer.add_sliding_window_pattern(swa_layers)
   667  
   668          head_dim_full = self.hparams["global_head_dim"]
   669          head_dim_swa = self.hparams["head_dim"]
   670          # correct the head dim for global/swa layers
   671          self.gguf_writer.add_key_length(head_dim_full)
   672          self.gguf_writer.add_value_length(head_dim_full)
   673          self.gguf_writer.add_key_length_swa(head_dim_swa)
   674          self.gguf_writer.add_value_length_swa(head_dim_swa)
   675  
   676          expert_intermediate_size = self.find_hparam(["expert_intermediate_size", "moe_intermediate_size"])
   677          if expert_intermediate_size is not None:
   678              self.gguf_writer.add_expert_feed_forward_length(expert_intermediate_size)
   679  
   680          # if use_double_wide_mlp is set, we need to adjust the value for kv shared layers
   681          use_double_wide_mlp = self.hparams.get("use_double_wide_mlp", False)
   682          first_kv_shared_layer_idx = self.block_count - num_kv_shared_layers
   683          if use_double_wide_mlp:
   684              n_ff = self.hparams["intermediate_size"]
   685              n_ff_arr = [n_ff if il < first_kv_shared_layer_idx else n_ff * 2 for il in range(self.block_count)]
   686              self.gguf_writer.add_feed_forward_length(n_ff_arr)
   687  
   688          # handle num_global_key_value_heads
   689          num_key_value_heads_full = self.hparams.get("num_global_key_value_heads")
   690          num_key_value_heads_swa = self.hparams.get("num_key_value_heads")
   691          if num_key_value_heads_full is not None and num_key_value_heads_swa is not None:
   692              value_arr = [num_key_value_heads_swa if is_swa else num_key_value_heads_full for is_swa in swa_layers]
   693              self.gguf_writer.add_head_count_kv(value_arr)
   694  
   695          # handle n_rot differently for global vs swa layers
   696          partial_rotary_factor_swa = self.rope_parameters.get("partial_rotary_factor", 1.0)
   697          n_rot_full = int(head_dim_full) # "proportional" is used, see generate_extra_tensors
   698          n_rot_swa = int(head_dim_swa * partial_rotary_factor_swa)
   699          self.gguf_writer.add_rope_dimension_count(n_rot_full)
   700          self.gguf_writer.add_rope_dimension_count_swa(n_rot_swa)
   701  
   702      def generate_extra_tensors(self) -> Iterable[tuple[str, Tensor]]:
   703          # full layer uses "proportional" rope with partial_rotary_factor=0.25
   704          # the expected ordering is cc000000ss000000 (c = cos, s = sin, 0 = unrotated),
   705          # but ggml neox only supports ccss000000000000, and we cannot rearrange the head because that will break use_alternative_attention
   706          # solution is to set specific freq_factors for the unrotated dims
   707  
   708          # IMPORTANT: this ROPE_FREQS tensor is ONLY used by the full_attention layers
   709          rope_params_full = self.hparams["rope_parameters"]["full_attention"]
   710          assert rope_params_full["rope_type"] == "proportional"
   711          head_dim_full = (self.hparams["global_head_dim"])
   712          partial_rotary_factor_full = rope_params_full["partial_rotary_factor"]
   713          n_rot_full = int(head_dim_full * partial_rotary_factor_full / 2)
   714          n_unrot_full = int(head_dim_full / 2) - n_rot_full
   715          values = [1.0] * n_rot_full + [1e30] * n_unrot_full
   716          rope_freqs_full = torch.tensor(values, dtype=torch.float32)
   717          yield (self.format_tensor_name(gguf.MODEL_TENSOR.ROPE_FREQS), rope_freqs_full)
   718  
   719      def _generate_nvfp4_tensors(self):
   720          # Gemma-4 stores a per-layer router.per_expert_scale ([n_expert]) that scales
   721          # each expert's contribution. It's mathematically equivalent to a per-expert
   722          # scalar on the down_proj output, which is exactly where ffn_down_exps_s is
   723          # applied at inference. Fold it into each expert's NVFP4 weight_scale_2 so the
   724          # existing NVFP4 path produces the right scales.
   725          n_experts = self.find_hparam(["num_local_experts", "num_experts"], optional=True) or 0
   726          for name in [n for n in self.model_tensors if n.endswith(".router.per_expert_scale")]:
   727              bid_match = re.search(r"\.layers\.(\d+)\.", name)
   728              if bid_match is None:
   729                  continue
   730              bid = bid_match.group(1)
   731              prefix = name[: name.index(f".layers.{bid}.") + len(f".layers.{bid}.")]
   732              w2_targets = [f"{prefix}experts.{e}.down_proj.weight_scale_2" for e in range(n_experts)]
   733              present = [w2 in self.model_tensors for w2 in w2_targets]
   734              if not any(present):
   735                  continue
   736              assert all(present), f"layer {bid}: partial NVFP4 quantization across experts"
   737              r = self.model_tensors.pop(name)
   738              for e, w2 in enumerate(w2_targets):
   739                  s = self.model_tensors[w2]
   740                  self.model_tensors[w2] = lambda s=s, r=r, i=e: s() * r()[i]
   741          super()._generate_nvfp4_tensors()
   742  
   743      @classmethod
   744      def filter_tensors(cls, item: tuple[str, Callable[[], Tensor]]) -> tuple[str, Callable[[], Tensor]] | None:
   745          name, gen = item
   746  
   747          if name.endswith("per_dim_scale") or name.endswith("layer_scalar"):
   748              name = name + ".weight"
   749          if ".experts." in name and not name.endswith((".weight", ".weight_scale", ".weight_scale_2", ".input_scale")):
   750              name += ".weight"
   751  
   752          return super().filter_tensors((name, gen))
   753  
   754      def modify_tensors(self, data_torch: Tensor, name: str, bid: int | None) -> Iterable[tuple[str, Tensor]]:
   755          if name.endswith("router.scale"):
   756              name = self.format_tensor_name(gguf.MODEL_TENSOR.FFN_GATE_INP, bid, ".scale")
   757              yield (name, data_torch)
   758              return
   759          if ".per_expert_scale" in name:
   760              # convert per-expert scale to FFN down scale
   761              name = self.format_tensor_name(gguf.MODEL_TENSOR.FFN_DOWN_EXP, bid, ".scale")
   762              yield (name, data_torch)
   763              return
   764  
   765          yield from super().modify_tensors(data_torch, name, bid)
   766  
   767  
   768  @ModelBase.register("Gemma4UnifiedForConditionalGeneration")
   769  class Gemma4UnifiedModel(Gemma4Model):
   770      model_arch = gguf.MODEL_ARCH.GEMMA4
   771  
   772      def _get_suppress_tokens(self) -> Sequence[int] | None:
   773          gen_cfg_path = self.dir_model / "generation_config.json"
   774          if gen_cfg_path.is_file():
   775              with open(gen_cfg_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
   776                  gen_cfg = json.load(f)
   777                  return gen_cfg.get("suppress_tokens")
   778          return None
   779  
   780      def set_gguf_parameters(self):
   781          super().set_gguf_parameters()
   782  
   783          suppress_tokens = self._get_suppress_tokens()
   784          if suppress_tokens is not None:
   785              self.gguf_writer.add_suppress_tokens(suppress_tokens)
   786  
   787  
   788  @ModelBase.register("Gemma4AssistantForCausalLM", "Gemma4UnifiedAssistantForCausalLM")
   789  class Gemma4AssistantModel(Gemma4Model):
   790      model_arch = gguf.MODEL_ARCH.GEMMA4_ASSISTANT
   791  
   792      @classmethod
   793      def filter_tensors(cls, item: tuple[str, Callable[[], Tensor]]) -> tuple[str, Callable[[], Tensor]] | None:
   794          name, gen = item
   795  
   796          if "masked_embedding" in name:
   797              logger.debug(f"Skipping get tensor {name!r} in safetensors so that convert can end normally.")
   798              return None
   799  
   800          return super().filter_tensors(item)
   801  
   802      def set_gguf_parameters(self):
   803          super().set_gguf_parameters()
   804          self.gguf_writer.add_embedding_length_out(self.hparams["backbone_hidden_size"])
   805          self.gguf_writer.add_nextn_predict_layers(self.block_count)
   806  
   807  
   808  @ModelBase.register("Gemma4ForConditionalGeneration")
   809  class Gemma4VisionAudioModel(MmprojModel):
   810      has_audio_encoder = True
   811      has_vision_encoder = True
   812  
   813      def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
   814          super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
   815          assert self.hparams_vision is not None
   816          self.hparams_vision["image_size"] = 224 # unused, but set to avoid error
   817  
   818          # remap audio hparams
   819          if self.hparams_audio:
   820              self.hparams_audio["feat_in"] = self.hparams_audio.get("input_feat_size", 128)
   821              if "hidden_size" in self.hparams_audio:
   822                  self.hparams_audio["intermediate_size"] = self.hparams_audio["hidden_size"] * 4
   823          else:
   824              self.has_audio_encoder = False
   825  
   826      def set_gguf_parameters(self):
   827          super().set_gguf_parameters()
   828  
   829          # vision params
   830          assert self.hparams_vision is not None
   831          self.gguf_writer.add_clip_vision_projector_type(gguf.VisionProjectorType.GEMMA4V)
   832          self.gguf_writer.add_vision_attention_layernorm_eps(self.hparams_vision.get("layer_norm_eps", 1e-6))
   833  
   834          # audio params
   835          if self.has_audio_encoder:
   836              assert self.hparams_audio is not None
   837              self.gguf_writer.add_clip_audio_projector_type(gguf.VisionProjectorType.GEMMA4A)
   838              self.gguf_writer.add_audio_num_mel_bins(self.hparams_audio["feat_in"])
   839              self.gguf_writer.add_audio_attention_layernorm_eps(self.hparams_audio.get("layer_norm_eps", 1e-6))
   840  
   841      def is_audio_tensor(self, name: str) -> bool:
   842          return "audio_tower" in name or "embed_audio" in name
   843  
   844      def tensor_force_quant(self, name, new_name, bid, n_dims):
   845          if self.is_audio_tensor(name):
   846              if ".conv" in name or "_conv" in name and ".weight" in name:
   847                  return gguf.GGMLQuantizationType.F32
   848          if "position_embedding_table" in name:
   849              return gguf.GGMLQuantizationType.F32
   850          return super().tensor_force_quant(name, new_name, bid, n_dims)
   851  
   852      def modify_tensors(self, data_torch: Tensor, name: str, bid: int | None) -> Iterable[tuple[str, Tensor]]:
   853          del bid # unused
   854  
   855          if len(data_torch.shape) == 0:
   856              # convert scalar tensors (input/output_mix/max) to 1D tensors
   857              data_torch = data_torch.unsqueeze(0)
   858  
   859          if self.is_audio_tensor(name):
   860              assert self.hparams_audio is not None
   861              name = name.replace("model.audio_tower.", "conformer.")
   862              name = name.replace(".linear.", ".")
   863              if name.endswith("per_dim_key_scale") or name.endswith("per_dim_scale"):
   864                  name = name + ".weight"
   865                  data_torch = torch.nn.functional.softplus(data_torch)
   866              if "lconv1d.depthwise_conv1d" in name and name.endswith(".weight"):
   867                  assert data_torch.shape[1] == 1
   868                  data_torch = data_torch.reshape(data_torch.shape[0], data_torch.shape[2])
   869              mapped_name = self.map_tensor_name(name, (".weight", ".bias", ".input_max", ".input_min", ".output_max", ".output_min"))
   870              yield (mapped_name, data_torch)
   871  
   872          else:
   873              name = name.replace("model.vision_tower.encoder.", "vision_model.model.")
   874              name = name.replace(".linear.weight", ".weight")
   875              if name.endswith("layer_scalar") or name.endswith("position_embedding_table"):
   876                  name = name + ".weight"
   877              if name.endswith("patch_embedder.input_proj.weight"):
   878                  n_embd, ksize_sq_c = data_torch.shape
   879                  patch_size = int((ksize_sq_c // 3) ** 0.5)
   880                  data_torch = data_torch.reshape(n_embd, patch_size, patch_size, 3)
   881                  data_torch = data_torch.permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous()
   882              mapped_name = self.map_tensor_name(name, (".weight", ".bias", ".input_max", ".input_min", ".output_max", ".output_min"))
   883              yield (mapped_name, data_torch)
   884  
   885  
   886  @ModelBase.register("Gemma4UnifiedForConditionalGeneration")
   887  class Gemma4UnifiedVisionAudioModel(Gemma4VisionAudioModel):
   888      has_audio_encoder = True
   889      has_vision_encoder = True
   890  
   891      def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
   892          super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
   893          assert self.hparams_vision is not None
   894          assert self.hparams_audio is not None
   895          text_embd_dim = self.hparams_vision["mm_embed_dim"]
   896          self.hparams_vision["hidden_size"] = text_embd_dim
   897          self.hparams_audio["hidden_size"] = self.hparams_audio["audio_embed_dim"]
   898          # this is a transformer-less vision tower, the params below are redundant but set to avoid error
   899          self.hparams_vision["intermediate_size"] = 0
   900          self.hparams_vision["num_layers"] = 0
   901          self.hparams_vision["num_attention_heads"] = 0
   902          self.hparams_audio["intermediate_size"] = 0
   903          self.hparams_audio["num_layers"] = 0
   904          self.hparams_audio["num_attention_heads"] = 0
   905  
   906      def set_gguf_parameters(self):
   907          super().set_gguf_parameters()
   908          self.gguf_writer.add_clip_vision_projector_type(gguf.VisionProjectorType.GEMMA4UV)
   909          self.gguf_writer.add_clip_audio_projector_type(gguf.VisionProjectorType.GEMMA4UA)
   910  
   911      def modify_tensors(self, data_torch, name, bid):
   912          if name.endswith("pos_embedding"):
   913              name += ".weight"
   914              data_torch = data_torch.permute(1, 0, 2)
   915          elif ".pos_norm." in name:
   916              # rename to patch_ln3 to reuse the tensor name scheme
   917              name = name.replace(".pos_norm.", ".patch_ln3.")
   918          elif "patch_dense.weight" in name:
   919              # ggml im2col outputs in RR..GG..BB.. (CHW) order, but weight expects RGBRGB.. (HWC).
   920              # Permute columns so column i aligns with CHW input position i.
   921              assert self.hparams_vision is not None
   922              if "model_patch_size" in self.hparams_vision:
   923                  p = self.hparams_vision["model_patch_size"]
   924              else:
   925                  p = self.hparams_vision["patch_size"] * self.hparams_vision["pooling_kernel_size"]
   926              i = torch.arange(p * p * 3)
   927              ch  = i // (p * p)
   928              row = (i % (p * p)) // p
   929              col = i % p
   930              # perm[i] = HWC column index for CHW position i
   931              perm = row * p * 3 + col * 3 + ch
   932              data_torch = data_torch[:, perm]
   933          elif "patch_ln1.weight" in name or "patch_ln1.bias" in name:
   934              # same permutation for patch_ln1 as patch_dense to align with CHW input order
   935              assert self.hparams_vision is not None
   936              if "model_patch_size" in self.hparams_vision:
   937                  p = self.hparams_vision["model_patch_size"]
   938              else:
   939                  p = self.hparams_vision["patch_size"] * self.hparams_vision["pooling_kernel_size"]
   940              i = torch.arange(p * p * 3)
   941              ch  = i // (p * p)
   942              row = (i % (p * p)) // p
   943              col = i % p
   944              # perm[i] = HWC index for CHW position i
   945              perm = row * p * 3 + col * 3 + ch
   946              data_torch = data_torch[perm]
   947          return super().modify_tensors(data_torch, name, bid)

  5053      LITTLE = 0
  5054      BIG = 1

  5056  
  5057  class GGUFValueType(IntEnum):
  5058      UINT8   = 0
  5059      INT8    = 1
  5060      UINT16  = 2
  5061      INT16   = 3
  5062      UINT32  = 4
  5063      INT32   = 5
  5064      FLOAT32 = 6
  5065      BOOL    = 7
  5066      STRING  = 8
  5067      ARRAY   = 9
  5068      UINT64  = 10
  5069      INT64   = 11
  5070      FLOAT64 = 12
  5071  
  5072      @staticmethod
  5073      def get_type(val: Any) -> GGUFValueType:
  5074          if isinstance(val, (str, bytes, bytearray)):
  5075              return GGUFValueType.STRING
  5076          elif isinstance(val, list):
  5077              return GGUFValueType.ARRAY
  5078          elif isinstance(val, float):
  5079              return GGUFValueType.FLOAT32
  5080          elif isinstance(val, bool):
  5081              return GGUFValueType.BOOL
  5082          elif isinstance(val, int):
  5083              return GGUFValueType.INT32
  5084          # TODO: need help with 64-bit types in Python
  5085          else:
  5086              raise ValueError(f"Unknown type: {type(val)}")

  2534          ),
  2535  
  2536          # NextN/MTP tensors
  2537          MODEL_TENSOR.NEXTN_PROJ_PRE: (
  2538              "pre_projection",
  2539          ),
  2540  
  2541          MODEL_TENSOR.NEXTN_PROJ_POST: (
  2542              "post_projection",
  2543          ),
  2544  
  2545          MODEL_TENSOR.NEXTN_EH_PROJ: (
  2546              "model.layers.{bid}.eh_proj",
  2547          ),
  2548  
  2549          MODEL_TENSOR.NEXTN_EMBED_TOKENS: (
  2550              "model.layers.{bid}.embed_tokens",
  2551          ),
  2552  
  2553          MODEL_TENSOR.NEXTN_ENORM: (
  2554              "model.layers.{bid}.enorm",
  2555          ),
  2556  
  2557          MODEL_TENSOR.NEXTN_HNORM: (
  2558              "model.layers.{bid}.hnorm",
  2559          ),
  2560  
  2561          MODEL_TENSOR.NEXTN_SHARED_HEAD_HEAD: (
  2562              "model.layers.{bid}.shared_head.head",
  2563          ),
  2564  
  2565          MODEL_TENSOR.NEXTN_SHARED_HEAD_NORM: (
  2566              "model.layers.{bid}.shared_head.norm",
  2567          ),
  2568      }
  2569  
  2570      # architecture-specific block mappings
  2571      arch_block_mappings_cfg: dict[MODEL_ARCH, dict[MODEL_TENSOR, tuple[str, ...]]] = {
  2572          MODEL_ARCH.ARCTIC: {
  2573              MODEL_TENSOR.FFN_NORM: (
  2574                  "model.layers.{bid}.residual_layernorm",
  2575              ),
  2576              MODEL_TENSOR.FFN_NORM_EXP: (
  2577                  "model.layers.{bid}.post_attention_layernorm",
  2578              ),
  2579          },
  2580      }
  2581  
  2582      mapping: dict[str, tuple[MODEL_TENSOR, str]]
  2583  
  2584      def __init__(self, arch: MODEL_ARCH, n_blocks: int):
  2585          self.mapping = {}
  2586          for tensor, keys in self.mappings_cfg.items():
  2587              if tensor not in MODEL_TENSORS[arch]:
  2588                  continue
  2589              tensor_name = TENSOR_NAMES[tensor]
  2590              self.mapping[tensor_name] = (tensor, tensor_name)
  2591              for key in keys:
  2592                  self.mapping[key] = (tensor, tensor_name)
  2593          if arch in self.arch_block_mappings_cfg:
  2594              self.block_mappings_cfg.update(self.arch_block_mappings_cfg[arch])
  2595          for bid in range(n_blocks):
  2596              for tensor, keys in self.block_mappings_cfg.items():
  2597                  if tensor not in MODEL_TENSORS[arch]:
  2598                      continue
  2599  
  2600                  tensor_name = TENSOR_NAMES[tensor].format(bid = bid)
  2601                  self.mapping[tensor_name] = (tensor, tensor_name)
  2602                  for key in keys:
  2603                      key = key.format(bid = bid)
  2604                      self.mapping[key] = (tensor, tensor_name)
  2605  
  2606      def get_type_and_name(self, key: str, try_suffixes: Sequence[str] = ()) -> tuple[MODEL_TENSOR, str] | None:
  2607          result = self.mapping.get(key)
  2608          if result is not None:
  2609              return result
  2610          for suffix in try_suffixes:
  2611              if key.endswith(suffix):
  2612                  result = self.mapping.get(key[:-len(suffix)])
  2613                  if result is not None:
  2614                      return result[0], result[1] + suffix
  2615          return None
  2616  
  2617      def get_name(self, key: str, try_suffixes: Sequence[str] = ()) -> str | None:
  2618          result = self.get_type_and_name(key, try_suffixes = try_suffixes)
  2619          if result is None:
  2620              return None
  2621          return result[1]
  2622  
  2623      def get_type(self, key: str, try_suffixes: Sequence[str] = ()) -> MODEL_TENSOR | None:
  2624          result = self.get_type_and_name(key, try_suffixes = try_suffixes)
  2625          if result is None:
  2626              return None
  2627          return result[0]
  2628  
  2629      def __getitem__(self, key: str) -> str:
  2630          try:
  2631              return self.mapping[key][1]
  2632          except KeyError:
  2633              raise KeyError(key)
  2634  
  2635      def __contains__(self, key: str) -> bool:
  2636          return key in self.mapping
  2637  
  2638      def __repr__(self) -> str:
  2639          return repr(self.mapping)
  2640  
  2641  
  2642  def get_tensor_name_map(arch: MODEL_ARCH, n_blocks: int) -> TensorNameMap:
  2643      return TensorNameMap(arch, n_blocks)
